# PROJECT MEMORY - AABrowser

## 1. Current Status
- **Version / Milestone**: 2.8 prepared locally (15/11/2026), `versionCode 20` / `versionName "2.8"`; not yet published. It covers the 97 commits after tag `2.7` (`230b0ab`, published 11/11/2026): Other Apps with Shizuku and split view, one dock and one settings page for both car apps, the Wide home as a page, desktop per site, TV auto-resume and channel strip, Radio effects, Bluetooth mouse and keyboard, the GitHub repository with two names. In-app notes: `assets/whatsnew/2.8/en.txt|it.txt`; the GitHub release notes are given in the chat, not stored.

  - **Release 2.8 prepared (15/11/2026, user: "vorrei rilasciare l'aggiornamento … bump version, il file per le novità, readme e il changelog per GitHub")**: `browser/build.gradle` 20 / 2.8; `assets/whatsnew/2.8` rewritten for everything since 2.7 (IT first, EN; other languages fall back to English); README (EN, IT): the 2.7 markers removed, `(new)` became `(new in 2.8)` / `(novità 2.8)`, a bullet for Audio boost (it was missing); the Bluetooth button of Settings now says only "Apri" / "Open" (`bt_input_open`, 11 locales: the long label did not fit). The release is made by the user: push main, create the release with tag `2.8` after the push (CI builds the APK, which must end in `.apk`). Versions before 2.8 know only the repository name AABrowser: after the rename they need one manual install (said in the release notes).   - **Bluetooth mouse and keyboard of the phone on the car screens (15/11/2026, user: "poi vorrei aggiungere il supporto per dispositivi bt mouse tastiera ecc per navigare nell'app o interagire nei siti")**. v1, compiled and unit-tested (`BtInputTest` 12, 214 Kotlin tests in all), NOT tried on a phone or in the car. **Why a screen on the phone**: a mouse or keyboard paired with the phone sends its events to the foreground app of the phone, and with Android Auto in use that is never CarWeb; so `BtInputActivity` (new, Settings > Altre App > "Mouse e tastiera Bluetooth" > "Apri la schermata di controllo", phone only: action `bt_input` in `SettingsWebScreen.PHONE` and `SettingsPhoneActions`, started from the Activity because the Main's localized context cannot start activities) stays open on the phone, black at 20% brightness with `FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON`, says which car screen receives, and captures: `View.requestPointerCapture()` on its root view (asked at every window focus and at a touch) with an `OnCapturedPointerListener` (relative moves summed with the event's history, `buttonState` differences, `ACTION_SCROLL` wheel; a captured touchpad gives absolute positions, so only its buttons count) and `dispatchKeyEvent`. Not captured (they stay with the phone): Back, Home, recents, power, volume, camera, calls, assistant and the media keys (`BtInput.forwardable`). **Router** (`utils/BtInputRouter.kt`, main thread): one pointer in the coordinates of the screen that receives (starts at the centre, stops at the edges, speed 50-300% in steps of 25: `AppSettings.btPointerSpeed`, a Stepper in the new settings card), `Target` interface (`label`, `size`, `isInFront`, `cursor`, `pointer` DOWN/MOVE/UP, `wheel`, `key`, `shortcut`), targets registered while in front: `ui/MainBtTarget.kt` (`MainCarActivity` onResume/onPause/onDestroy) and `WideScreen.btTarget` (lifecycle onCreate/onDestroy, in front = `isWideResumed` and it is the active instance), **both off while the driving cover is up** (`DrivingState.isLocked`, `isDriveLocked()`). Left click = a finger, right click and the side "back" button = Back, side "forward" = forward, wheel = scroll (56 dp a notch, `BtInput.WHEEL_STEP_DP`), the cursor hides after 8 s. Shortcuts as in a desktop browser (`BtInput.shortcutFor`: Esc, Alt+←/→, F5, Ctrl+R, Ctrl+L/K, F6, Ctrl+Tab and Ctrl+Pg↑/↓, Ctrl+W/F4, Ctrl+T, Alt+Home); a shortcut key is consumed at its first press, with its repeats and its release. **Main**: real touches (finger, touchscreen source, same as the car's) to `getCarWindow().decorView.dispatchTouchEvent`, so pages, Compose and the phone-app tabs get them; wheel = `ACTION_SCROLL` through `dispatchGenericMotionEvent`; keys through `decorView.dispatchKeyEvent` (needs a focused view: a click on the page gives the WebView its focus); the cursor is `BtCursorOverlay` in `MainScreen` (`zIndex` 101, window coordinates minus the view origin) fed by `BtCursor.position`; shortcuts go through `BtShortcuts` (a `MutableSharedFlow`) collected by `MainScreen`, with a new shared `stepBack` (the phone Back's step: page history, home, previous screen; false on the home) and `NavigationOverlay(searchRequest)` for Ctrl+L. **Wide**: the click is `onClick(x, y)` at the point where the button went down (so sidebar, phone app, split and fullscreen video work unchanged), a drag beyond 8 dp scrolls like a finger (`onScroll`), the wheel is `onScroll`, keys go to the WebView (`requestFocus` + `dispatchKeyEvent`), Back closes QR card, passenger keyboard and input popup first (the same callbacks as the car's Back), the cursor is a view on top of the surface (`car/WideBtCursor.kt`, moved with `translationX/Y`). While the capture screen is open the ⌨️ 🎤 popup of text fields does not show (`BtInputRouter.capturing`, Main and Wide). 8 new strings in 11 locales (`bt_input_*`, `bt_pointer_speed`); README (EN, IT), `AABrowser.md` (new section). The Wide's plain keys are the unproven part: a WebView in a `Presentation` has never had a real keyboard (its proven way of writing is the JavaScript insertion), so if typing does nothing there the next step is to insert the printable characters with `getInjectTextScript`. Log tag `BtInput` (also release; coordinates and key names only, first 40 events per opening).

phone: second row of the same bar) and CarWeb itself can be opened from the list (`VirtualApps` no longer filters its own package; the start-page choice still does, `AutoOpenFavorite`); `efd5356` the split stays (Main: `splitAppId` = the active app tab or, while a web tab is active, the last app tab;   - **Wide home as a page of the app, with the app's theme, the Main's look and editable favorites (15/11/2026, user: "la home della wide non ha tema chiaro o scuro come app principale vorrei che anche lei seguisse il tema automaticamente … vorrei il tasto edit per modificare i preferiti anche da wide che poi si riflettano su main, e possibilmente creiamo la home della wide esteticamente uguale a quella della main con impostazioni in basso tasto matita cerca microfono ecc in alto")**. Step 6 of the one-dock plan. The home was an HTML string written in `HomePage.build` (always dark, a Unsplash background, links only). Now `HomeApi` (`utils/HomeApi.kt`) serves `https://home.local/` (the base URL stays: every `startsWith(HomePage.BASE_URL)` check of the Wide keeps working) from `WideScreen`'s `shouldInterceptRequest` (`HomeApi.intercept`: GET only, the page only for the main frame, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`; `/home.js`, `/home.css` assets; `/api/home/get` and `/api/home/fav` need the token that only the home HTML carries). Assets in `assets/home/` (`home.html|css|js`). Theme: `LocalPlayer.themeCss(carContext)` (the same variables as the CarWeb Media pages: automatic with the car's day/night, forced light/dark/AMOLED, phone colors); the blurred background image only with a dark theme (`body.dark`, from the luminance of `--bg`), as in the Main. Look = `DashboardScreen`: "CarWebWide vX", ↗ CarWeb (`about:toggle_open_main`), mic (`HomePage.VOICE` = `about:toggle_voice`), lens (`HomePage.SEARCH` = `about:toggle_search`), pencil; subtitle; cards (32 px icon circle tinted with the favorite's colour at 25%, name, domain as in `FavoriteCard`: `HomeApi.domainOf`), then the phone apps with the star; the update banner (`UpdateManager.lastInfo`, new) above the bottom pills Riprendi (`AppSettings.wideLastUrl`) and Impostazioni (no Tabs: the Wide has one page); grid `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(190px, 1fr))`, 100 px tall cards. **Editing**: the pencil turns edit mode on; each card gets ◀ ✎ 🗑 ▶ (44 px buttons: at 119 dpi 1 CSS px is 0.74 device px, so the Main's 28 dp buttons would be too small in the car; no 🗑 on the fixed CarWeb pages, `isFixed`) and the last card is +; the phone apps stay inert. The form is a sheet above the page (name, address, favicon, 5 colours, desktop switch, zoom − / + in 5% steps 25-150%, Cancel / Confirm; two columns on a wide screen, one on a phone; inputs are plain `<input>`s, which the Wide's input popup + Android Auto / Passenger keyboard already fill). Mutations are GETs (`/api/home/fav?op=add|update|delete|move&…`: `shouldInterceptRequest` has no request body) handled by the new pure `model/FavoriteEdits.kt` (`FavoriteEditsTest` 12: the rules that were in `FavoritesViewModel` — no own pages or apps, no duplicates by `normalizeUrl`, fixed = first favorite with that address cannot be deleted nor change address, zoom 25-150%, address typed by hand gets `https://` also with a port, `javascript:` / `intent:` / `file:` / `data:` refused with `not_allowed`, empty or siteless = `invalid`; `FavoritesViewModel` now calls it, and `updateFavorite` returns a Boolean so the dashboard says `favorite_not_allowed`). After a change `FavoritesRepository.notifyExternalChange()` + `Snapshot.sendApplyNotifications()` (outside any composition a write must be announced by hand, or the Main's `FavoritesViewModel` never reloads), and `WideScreen.watchFavorites` clears `favoritesCache` (desktop/zoom of the favorite) and calls `window.aabRefresh` on an open home; the Wide's dock star now notifies too (before, the Main did not see it). Removed: `HomePage.build`, `Item`, `homeHtml`, `buildHomeHtml`. Notices of the page are at the top centre: at the bottom they covered Confirm on the phone layout (found by the headless test). RTL: `config.rtl` → `<html dir>`, arrows and the Riprendi/↗ icons mirrored. New strings `home_delete`, `home_move_back`, `home_move_forward`, `home_error` (11 locales). Tests: `home.test.js` 7, `FavoriteEditsTest` 12, `HomeApiTest` 2; headless Chrome with the real page and a mock server that does what the API does (`/tmp/aab-videofit-test/homeui.mjs`, 40+ checks in 1075x514 dark and light, 390x844 dark and light, RTL: cards, links, theme colours, edit mode, move, delete, add with colour/desktop/zoom, update, fixed page, refused addresses, cancel, form inside the screen). NOT tried in the Wide or in the car: the interception of `home.local` assets and API, `about:toggle_search` / `about:toggle_voice` from the home, typing in the form with the Android Auto keyboard, the car's day/night colours, scrolling the grid with Android Auto's drag.
  - **Global "Desktop mode" setting removed (15/11/2026, user: "poi c'è da capire se il tasto modalità desktop nelle impostazioni serve ancora e forse si può tirare via tanto ogni sito ha il suo settaggio e quello più avanzato con zoom lo gestisci modificando il preferito")**. Checked: it only decided the sites that have neither a per-site choice (dock / tab bar / menu icon: `DesktopModeSites` overrides) nor a favorite setting, and the user's own value on the phone was `false`. Decision: removed (`AppSettings.desktopMode`, `setDesktopMode`, the row and its `registry` entry, the string `desktop_mode_desc` in 11 locales). The order is now site choice → favorite → mobile (`DesktopModeSites.wantsDesktop(url, favoriteOverride, daznForced)`, `isEnabledForUrl`, `decide` without `global`; `BrowserScreen` no longer takes `isDesktopMode`; `MainScreen`, `WideScreen`, `EditFavoriteScreen` use `false` as the base). Settings > Web pages keeps the two base zooms, now both always visible (before one showed according to the switch). Migration, once at startup (`AppSettings.init`): whoever had it on gets `isDesktopMode = true` on every favorite without its own choice (`FavoritesRepository.withLegacyDesktopMode`, pure and tested; CarWeb pages and apps untouched) and the `desktop_mode` key is removed; sites without a favorite go back to mobile. README (EN, IT) updated. Compiled; tests updated (`DesktopModeSitesTest`, `FavoritesRepositoryTest`).
  - **Wide split screen: touch, fullscreen video and a readable app panel (15/11/2026, user: "nell'app wide crea problemi con il touch fatichi a interagire nella parte sinistra poi in quella destra e se metto un video a schermo intero non riesco più a uscire")**. Found by reading the phone's logcat (wireless adb, debug build) that the split in that log (a 204x381 then 240x381 display, i.e. exactly 30% of 800 px) was the MAIN's, working; the Wide's test was not in the buffer, so the Wide defects below come from the code and from the numbers of an earlier Wide session in the same log: (1) with a fullscreen video (`customView`, which covers the app's panel too) taps in the app's zone went to the app under it, so the player's exit button (often bottom right) could not be tapped: now a fullscreen video takes all taps and they do not move the active panel; (2) the car's Back did not leave the fullscreen video (only the keyboard and the QR had callbacks): `closeVideoOnBack` (registered first: the dispatcher answers the last registered, and keyboard and QR sit above the video); (3) the keyboard/mic popup card now takes taps before the app; (4) the pinch has the fingers' position: in split it goes to the panel under them and makes it the active one (`WideVirtualApp.setFocus` replaces `noteTap`; a tap on the dock does not move the focus any more); (5) a 3 dp azure mark on the top edge of the active panel (`focusMark`) shows where scroll and Back go; (6) **minimum panel width**: in that log the Wide's region with the host's column was 503 px and 30% gave 150 px, i.e. an app display of 112 px at 60 dpi (a phone 24 mm wide): `VirtualSplit.appPanelWidth` / `panels(…, minPanel)` never go below the app bar + 240 dp of the car (`MIN_DISPLAY_DP`), within the 50% cap, in Wide and Main (`VirtualSplitTest` +2); (7) `Log.d` `WideScreen: split tap x= y= -> app|page|dock|video (focus=…) page=… app=…` for the first 60 taps per opening (coordinates only). If the user still has trouble: share the log right after a Wide split test (`adb logcat -d --pid=…`: lines `split tap` and `layout raw`).
  - **TV resumes by itself when it stops without a pause from the user (15/11/2026, user: "alcuni canali mentre stanno riproducendo potrebbero perdere un attimo il focus o segnale e smettono di riprodurre ma se cambi canale e lo rimetti torna a funzionare, servirebbe che se io non tocco pausa lui provi sempre a riprendere da solo la riproduzione")**. New `assets/player/recover.js` (`AABRecover.create()`, pure, no DOM, `recover.test.js` 13): once a second tv.js passes `{ paused, time, otherAudio }` and gets null, `'play'` or `'restart'`. Rules: paused without being asked = a `play()` after 1.5 s (`pauseGraceMs`), again every 3 s, and a restart if it stays still for 10 s (`stallMs`); "playing" but the time not moving = a restart after 10 s (25 s before the first movement, `startMs`); `error()` (hls.js fatal, mpegts.js, `<video>` error, `ended`, `/api/tv/play` failing) = a restart after the wait; waits 1.5, 3, 6, 12, 20 s, then every 30 s (`delays`), restarted from zero after 3 s of real movement (`movingToReset`) or when the user picks a channel (`begin(false)`; the auto restart `play(current, true)` keeps counting). **The intent of a pause is never read from the `pause` event** (a lost audio focus or a dropped signal makes the same event): it comes only from the hooks `window.aabMarkUserPause` / `aabMarkUserPlay`, which the app's own scripts already call in `PAUSE_SCRIPT`, `STOP_SCRIPT`, `PLAY_SCRIPT` (Android Auto player, wheel keys, voice, `MediaFocusManager` pausing the previous owner), and from the page's own paths (`userPause()`: the toggle button, the Media Session `pause` handler, voice `pause`; `resume()` calls `userPlay()`). tv.js wraps the two hooks with `Object.defineProperty` (a getter that runs ours and then the app's function, a setter that keeps a later definition of the app's), because the app's script defines them at document start and could define them again. A call or another app on the phone must not be disturbed: new bridge method `AndroidBridge.otherAudioPlaying()` in `BrowserScreen` (Main) and `WideScreen` (Wide), answered only when the page host is an app page (`LocalPlayer.isPlayerHost`), backed by `VirtualAppAudio.otherAudioPlaying(context)` (`AudioManager.mode != MODE_NORMAL`, or an active media/game/unknown player: the same count the audio handoff uses); asked only while the video is paused; while it is true the page waits (the idle clock restarts every second) and resumes about 1.5 s after it becomes false. Route `/recover.js` in `LocalPlayer`, script tag in `tv.html`. The Radio page was left alone (it reports stalls and the user can retry: the same recovery could be added with the same module). Checked in headless Chrome with the real page, a mock API and real timings (`/tmp/aab-videofit-test/tvrecover.mjs`: 17 checks, 1075x514): a system pause resumes in 2.3 s; a pause through the app's hooks stays paused for 8 s (and the app's own hook ran); a play through the hooks re-arms it; the page's pause button stays paused for 6 s and its play resumes; another app playing blocks the resume for 7 s and it resumes 1.3 s after it stops; a frozen "playing" stream (time stub) is requested again after 13.5 s; a 3 s clip (a dropped stream) reconnects 3 times in 12.6 s and stops after a user pause; a broken channel is retried with growing waits (2.2, 4.0, 7.0 s) and the retries stop when the channel changes. Not tried on a device or in the car: the real triggers (an audio focus loss, a network drop) were simulated.
  - **Favorites always in view in TV, Radio and Podcast (15/11/2026, user: "nelle iptv radio ecc i preferiti riusciamo a metterli sempre visibili esempio un tasto di fianco al cerca perché se ho iptv con tante categorie mi perdo quelle dei preferiti")**. In the TV page the ★ was the first chip of the scrolling row of categories, so with many categories it was lost. Now a round ★ button (`#favoritesToggle`, `.icon-button favorites-toggle`) sits beside the search field (`.search-row`, `tv.css`, shared by the three pages) and stays in view. TV (`tv.js`): on = `showFavorites()` (favorites of every list), off = `showGroup` of the category that was open before (`groupBeforeFavorites`, else the first); `markGroup` keeps the button `active` / `aria-pressed` in step with `state.group === 'fav'`; the chip is gone. Radio (`radio.js`): the two chips «Stazioni» / «Preferiti» became the toggle (`setFavoritesView`); Podcast (`podcast.js`): the chips Tutti / Seguiti became the toggle, and inside an open show the toggle is hidden (`updateTools`) and «‹ title» and Follow are in `#showTools`. Found while checking: `.icon-button { display: flex }` beats the `hidden` attribute, so the toggle stayed visible inside a podcast: `.favorites-toggle[hidden] { display: none }` (and `.groups[hidden]`, for `#showTools`). Checked in headless Chrome with the real pages and mock APIs (`/tmp/aab-videofit-test/tvrecover.mjs ... fav` and `radiopodcasttoggle.mjs`, at 1075x514 and 390x844, with 40 categories and the row scrolled to its end): the button is inside the window, 48x48, beside the search field, off at the start, on shows only the favorites / followed, off brings back the category (the same one), no JavaScript errors; screenshots looked right. The unused `allStations` string is still sent by `RadioApi` (harmless). An empty favorites list shows an empty list without a message (as before). Not tried on a device.
  - **The star no longer saves CarWeb's own pages, and the duplicates it made can be deleted (15/11/2026, user: "la stella per salvare i preferiti del browser mi permette erroneamente di salvare anche le pagine delle mie app iptv ecc creando dei doppioni non rimuovibili")**. Cause: the Main's radial-menu star (`MainScreen.onAddToFavorites` → `FavoritesViewModel.addFavorite`) added any tab URL, also `https://player.local/…` (Media, TV, Radio, Podcast, Other Apps, Settings) and `app://…`, and added it again at every tap (no check for an existing one); and `FavoritesRepository.isFixed(site)` meant "its URL is a player.local page", so every such favorite, the real default and its copies alike, had no delete button and a locked address. Now: `isOwnPage(url)` (player page or `app://`); `addFavorite` returns false for those (and unsafe schemes) and does not repeat an address already present (`normalizeUrl`); the star says `favorite_not_allowed` (11 locales) in the Main, in the Wide (`addCurrentPageToFavorites`, whose dock button was already off on those pages) and in the Dashboard's add form; `isFixed(site, list)` is true only for the FIRST favorite with that address, so a duplicate is deletable and keeps no locked address (`FavoriteCard(canDelete)`, `EditFavoriteScreen(urlLocked)`, `FavoritesViewModel.isFixed`); the default pages still come back when missing (`addNewDefaults` checks `LocalPlayer.isPlayerUrl`). No migration: copies saved before become deletable at once. Tests in `FavoritesRepositoryTest` (6). Compiled; not tried on a device.
  - **Audio handoff: the browser resumes when the phone app stops (15/11/2026, part of the split-view spec: "se il browser sta riproducendo e l'app virtuale inizia audio, l'app prende il focus; se l'app smette, il browser riprende il focus se stava riproducendo")**. The spec asked for an `AudioFocusRequest` for the virtual app and a handover through `CarMediaService`: NOT done, on purpose, because the project rule says never to request audio focus from our own package (it steals it from Chromium and pauses the page; the notes of 18/10). The first half already works by itself: the phone app is another process that asks for focus, Android takes it from the WebView and the page pauses. The missing half is resuming: new `smartphone/AudioHandoff.kt` (pure, `AudioHandoffTest` 10) and `smartphone/VirtualAppAudio.kt` (Android glue, reference-counted by `VirtualAppSession.init`/`close`): an `AudioPlaybackCallback` counts the active MEDIA/GAME/UNKNOWN players of the phone (no permission: other apps are anonymous), a 500 ms tick reads the Android Auto player state (`CarMediaService.isBrowserPlaying()`, new) and `AudioHandoff` decides. It resumes (`sendPlayerCommand(PLAY)`, log line `VirtualAppAudio: the app stopped…`) only if a player started while the browser played and the browser paused within 2.5 s, something kept playing for 1.5 s after the pause and started within that window (so a pause by the user, whose player disappears at once, and an unrelated sound that starts later never resume anything), then 2 s of silence (a gap between two tracks does not count); it gives up after 30 minutes. Known limits: another phone app playing in the background counts as "the app"; the count cannot tell our WebView's players from others (only the transitions are used); not seen on a device. Compiled; tests pass.
  - **Split view: the browser and a phone app side by side, 70/30 (15/11/2026, user: "split view tra il browser Main/Wide e un'app del telefono virtualizzata tramite Shizuku, con rapporto regolabile 70/30", with a written spec)**. New `smartphone/VirtualSplit.kt` (pure, `VirtualSplitTest` 9): `panels(total, percent, side)` (app 20-50%, default 30, step 5; browser the rest, no overlap), `displayWidth(panelW, panelH)` (portrait: the panel if already narrower than tall, else a 9:16 display as tall as the panel with black bands), `density(baseDpi, widthPx)` (not so high that the app sees less than 320 dp), `Side` LEFT/RIGHT. `AppSettings`: `virtualSplitEnabled` (false), `virtualSplitFraction` (30, clamped), `virtualSplitSide` (RIGHT); `virtualSidebarDocked` and its pref are gone (the bar is always beside the app: overlay removed; strings `vapp_sidebar_dock`/`vapp_sidebar_overlay` and `ic_vapp_dock` too); settings page rows (switch, − + stepper, side choice in the Other Apps card, `SettingsApi` keys) and 8 new strings in 11 locales (`vapp_split`, `vapp_split_desc`, `vapp_split_on`, `vapp_split_off`, `virtual_split_fraction`, `virtual_split_side`, `virtual_split_side_left|right`). `VirtualAppSession.splitMode` limits the density in `attach` (log `density … (split)`). **Main**: `VirtualAppTab(split, onToggleSplit)`: first bar button toggles the split (icon `Icons.VerticalSplit`, blue when on), the display is a `BoxWithConstraints` of `VirtualSplit.displayWidth`. `MainScreen`: `splitActive = split on && the active tab is an app && browser visible`; the companion web tab = `lastWebTabId` (updated when a web tab is active) or the first web tab, or a new home tab in the background when there is none; it is drawn above the other inactive tabs and below the active one (`sortedBy` 0/1/2), gets `isTabActive = true` for `BrowserScreen` (media can take the player, touch blocker off) and the paddings of its panel; the app tab gets the other panel (`onSizeChanged` width minus the dock, `VirtualSplit.clampPercent`); `reloadTrigger`, back and forward and `isFullScreen` stay tied to the really active tab. **Wide**: `WideVirtualApp` (bar always beside, `ic_vapp_split` button, texture centred and capped to a portrait width with a layout listener, `appOffset` includes the shift, `isSplit`, `receivesGestures()`, `noteTap`, `onSettingsChanged`), `WideScreen` (`refreshSplit` + `watchVirtualSplit`, the page stays VISIBLE in split and takes the browser panel: the boundary is computed once from the app region (below Android Auto's buttons, right of the sidebar) and applied to both; scroll, fling, pinch, Back and Forward go to the panel last tapped (the host sends the position only for taps); `loadUrl`/`showHome` do not hide the app in split; `virtualApp` got an explicit type because the callback reads it). Decisions that differ from the written spec, on purpose: (1) no swapping of width and height to "force" portrait or landscape: the display has the shape of the view that shows it (a swap would stretch the picture and the touches), the portrait comes from the panel and from the cap; full screen follows the view (landscape in the car, portrait on a standing phone: forcing landscape there would be wrong); (2) no `AudioFocusRequest` from our package (see the audio entry). Compiled; 148+ Kotlin tests pass; NOT tried on a phone or in the car: the two panels in the Main (the BrowserScreen of the companion as an "active" tab is the riskiest part), the Wide's page beside the app and the gesture routing, the display shape in a wide panel, and whether 30% is readable.

Strings `wide_sidebar_size` ("Sidebar size"), `video_menu_always` ("Sidebar always"), `video_menu_touch` ("Sidebar on tap") and `wide_video_menu_label` ("Sidebar with a fullscreen video") in the 11 locales: English and Italian say "sidebar" as the other dock strings already did; the other nine use each language's word already used in `sidebar_actions_label` (Seitenleiste, barra lateral, barre latérale, боковая панель, الشريط الجانبي, साइडबार, oldalsáv, 侧边栏). The same two choice labels are shown in the settings page, in the video bar of CarWeb Media (`video-zoom.js`) and in the label of the sites' fullscreen video (`video-fit.js`, through `LocalPlayer.videoZoomStrings` and `VideoFit`), so all three say it the same way; the JS fallbacks say `Sidebar`. README (EN and IT), `AABrowser.md` and the comments of `WideScreen`, `AppSettings`, `VideoFit` follow. Left as they are on purpose: the round "menu button" of the Main (that is the radial menu's own name: `main_dock_desc`, `menu_button`), the Android Auto menu and the Share menu. The saved preference keeps its old key `wide_video_menu_on_tap`. Compiled and `SettingsSchemaTest` passes; not seen on a device.
  - **Wide dock: buttons centred on the screen, not on the free area (15/11/2026, user: "verifica perché la sidebar non usa i tasti con gravità al centro ma sono spostati in basso")**. Read `WideScreen.layoutSidebar` (unchanged since `60ac203`): the container is a vertical `LinearLayout` with `gravity = TOP` and the first button gets `topMargin = firstTop`, computed as the centre of the STABLE AREA (`stableArea.top .. bottom`). The host keeps a much taller band at the top than at the bottom (the logs of 10/11 on a 1447x694 surface: stable `Rect(17, 96 - 1430, 681)`, i.e. 96 px above and 13 px below), so the centre of the free area is about 41 px under the centre of the screen, and with the 10 default buttons (the dock now has 10, it had 4-5 before the one-dock work: they shrink to fill the whole free area) the column starts right under the host's band and ends 13 px from the bottom edge: it looks shifted down. No device log was available (phone not connected), so this is read from the code and the old logs. Now `firstTop = ((surfaceHeight - total) / 2).coerceIn(top, bottom - total)`: centred on the screen, never inside the host's bands; a full dock still fills the free area (there is no room to centre it). Log line `dock count= icon= gap= total= first= free=top..bottom surfaceHeight=` (`Log.d`, also release) for the next test. The Main's dock (`MainDock`) was already centred (`contentAlignment = Center`). Compiled; not tried on a device.
  - **One settings menu for Main and Wide: the Compose Settings screen is gone (15/11/2026, user: "terminare il porting delle impostazioni c'è da spostarle tutte nel nuovo menu compatibile con app main e wide e renderlo unico menu di impostazioni al posto di quello kotlin stessa grafica e menu richiudibili"; step 5b of the one-dock plan)**. `https://player.local/settings` now holds every setting of the old `SettingsScreen` (about 60 rows) plus the Wide rows of 5a. **Schema** (`utils/SettingsSchema.kt`, pure data: sections, cards, rows `Switch`/`Choice`/`Stepper`/`Buttons`/`Heading`/`Group`/`Actions`/`Items`/`Checks`, string ids, value keys, `showIf` conditions that the page re-evaluates by itself) and **API** (`utils/SettingsApi.kt`: `registry` = key → read/write with validated values, `derived` read-only values, `datasets` = the lists the page asks for, `act` = button actions, `Outcome` = what the page must do after (restart, permission, notice, modal, cache label); routes `/api/settings/get|set|do|qr`; the snapshot carries strings, theme CSS, sections, values, data and the footer, in the app language, so a language or theme change shows at once). **Page** (`settings.js|css|html`): collapsible sections (Material look: secondary-container headers, bordered cards, M3-style switch), `−`/`+` steppers instead of sliders (the Wide cannot drag), in-page modals (passenger disclaimer, what's new, QR of the links in the car) instead of dialog windows, snackbar notices, requests serialised with optimistic updates and the server state adopted only when nothing is pending, section/group open state in `sessionStorage`, `?focus=tv|radio|podcast` opens the lists section. `LocalPlayer.themeCss` is now internal and carries `--outline`, `--secondary-container`, `--primary-container`, `--error*` too. **Main** (`ui/SettingsWebScreen.kt`): `Screen.Settings` shows the page in a dedicated WebView (not a tab: no extra tab, no `player.local` special cases in `BrowserScreen`/`TabManager`; it refuses every request that is not an app page); the bridge `AndroidBridge` has only `isPhone()` and `run(action)` (`SettingsNative.accepts`: `close`, `restart` everywhere, the phone actions only where `SettingsPhoneActions` is composed, i.e. where there is an `ActivityResultRegistryOwner`: the phone Main). Phone-only (`ui/SettingsPhoneActions.kt`, dialogs in `ui/SettingsNativeDialogs.kt` moved from the old cards): open a link, share the log (long press on the GitHub logo), app language, weather permission, backup export/import (SAF; import needs a second tap in the page first), local M3U and OPML pickers, the dialogs for M3U address, Xtream (server, user, password), RSS feed, country and the custom ad-list address. **Addresses and passwords never go through the page**: the page only gets name and type of each list. Removed: `SettingsScreen.kt`, `BackupCard.kt`, `IptvSourcesCard.kt`, `TabBarActionsCard.kt`, `SidebarActionsCard.kt`, `SitePermissionsList.kt`, `PassengerDisclaimerOverlay.kt` (`SettingsCard`, `SettingsSectionHeader`, `SettingsSwitchItem` stay in `SettingsComponents.kt` for `EditFavoriteScreen`; the dock icon mapping went into `MainDock.kt`; `calculateCacheSize` became `utils/CacheSize.kt`; `TabBarAction` got `labelRes`). `FavoritesRepository` gained `addMissingDefaults()` (pure `addMissingDefaults(list, defaults)` for the tests) and `externalChanges`, which `FavoritesViewModel` observes to reload when the page resets or completes the favorites. `AppSettings.setForceEnglish` changes the locale on the main thread (the page writes from a WebView thread). `VoiceCommands.mediaSectionOf` returns null for `/apps` and `/settings` (they are not Media pages). String `settings_restart_needed` (11 locales) replaces `settings_phone_note`. Checks: `SettingsSchemaTest` (11: every row has a writer and every key a row, every value/list the page reads is sent, steps reach their limits, every text exists in the 11 locales with the same placeholders), `settings.test.js` (14), `FavoritesRepositoryTest` (4), 71 JS tests in all; the real page was driven in headless Chrome with a mock server (`/tmp/claude-1000/.../scratchpad/settingsui/mock.mjs`, `run.mjs`: 47 checks with and without the phone bridge, no JS errors, screenshots at 411 px and 1075 px). NOT tried on the phone, in the Main in the car or in the Wide: the WebView screen, the bridge, the SAF pickers and dialogs, the language change and the restart are compiled but never run. Not done: the page does not animate sections (no `AnimatedVisibility`).

The choice "Usa un preferito come pagina iniziale" listed only `favoritesViewModel.favorites` (the website favorites); the starred phone apps live apart, in `FavoriteAppsManager` (prefs `SMARTPHONE_FAVORITES`, package names only). New `utils/AutoOpenFavorite.kt`: `options(context, sites)` = sites (id, name) followed by the starred apps that can still be opened (`VirtualApps.isAvailable`: installed, an icon in the launcher, not CarWeb) with key `app://package` and the app's label; `appPackage(id)` = the package of a saved `app://…` value, null for a site id (a UUID or a number of the defaults never looks like an app address) and for anything that is not a package name (`VirtualApps.packageOf`). The saved value stays the one pref `auto_open_favorite_id`. `MainScreen` startup: an app id opens the app's tab as the home tiles do (`TabManager.openOrSwitchTo` + `Screen.Browser`); an app uninstalled after the choice behaves like a favorite that is gone (the open tab or the Dashboard); the wait for the website favorites no longer blocks an app start.   - **Standby message stuck on the car screen (15/11/2026, user: "se il telefono è bloccato esce la scritta che il telefono è andato in stanby ma il problema è che la scritta non va più via anche se sblocco e l'app si vede correttamente c'è da mettere un timeout a quel messaggio")**. Cause found in the phone log of the user's test (debug build, wireless adb): at 09:37:14 an app was opened on the car with the phone asleep (`VirtualScreenPower: an app is in front on the car … (asleep now: true)`), the shell service woke it at once (`keepPhoneAwake(true) -> OK held interactive=true`, 5 ms later) and the `ACTION_SCREEN_ON` that clears `asleep` never reached `onScreenOn` (it would have started the standby screen again: no second `launch display=0` in the log, while in the cycle of 09:35:56 it did arrive), so `asleep` stayed true for good. Not a timeout: it would also hide the message while the phone really sleeps and leave the car black with no explanation. `VirtualScreenPower` now reads the truth from the system: `updateAsleep(value)` (not `setAsleep`: the property's private setter has that JVM name and the build refuses it) re-checks `PowerManager.isInteractive` every second while the message is up (`ASLEEP_RECHECK_MS`) and clears it with the log line `the phone is awake but the standby message was still up: clearing it`; it is also cleared by a touch on the car screen (`touchOnCar` checks `isInteractive` first), by `ACTION_USER_PRESENT` (unlock), and `activate()` now registers the receiver before reading the state. Compiled; not tried on a device.
  - **Radio: with an effect on, every station ended after about 2 seconds (15/11/2026, user: "la radio non capisco non funziona quasi niente siamo sicuri che le url si aggiornano sempre da sole?")**. My own bug of 14/11 (radio effects). The saved effect was `bars` (`media_pages.xml` of the app, read with `run-as` on the debug build), so `/api/radio/play` got `proxy=1` and every direct stream went through `IptvProxy` to the `<audio>`. The phone log (Wide, 09:34-09:35) shows Kiss Kiss, 60s Retro Hits and Studio Più, plain MP3 over http/https, with `duration` growing 0 → 1.008 → 2.04 and then `isPlaying=false` at 2.04 (0.984 for the other): the WebView takes `InputStream.available()` of the `WebResourceResponse` as the content length (Chromium: `InputStreamReader::VerifyRequestedRange`, `AndroidStreamReaderURLLoader::HeadersComplete` writes Content-Length from it and keeps reading to EOF) and the media stack ends the file there (`BlockingUrlProtocol::Read` returns EOF at the size the data source reports); `available()` of our `BufferedInputStream` was the 32 KB buffered, 2.048 s at 128 kbit/s. It also explains the user's report of the same morning ("ripetano sempre lo stesso pezzo": pressing play again restarts the same 2 seconds), which I wrongly put down to the stations in the entry below (the 10 bad stations of the PC test are real, but not the cause). It was never seen before because the headless Chrome tests served the proxy path from Node. The URLs DO refresh by themselves: `RadioApi.play` asks Radio Browser `/json/url/<uuid>` at every play and uses the cached `url_resolved` of the list (30 minutes, memory only) only if that call fails; favorites and the last station keep uuid and name, no address. Fix: `IptvProxy.LiveStream` (`available()` = `Int.MAX_VALUE`, `skip` consumes nothing) for `Target.live`, set by `RadioApi` for progressive streams (`live = !isHls`, also list stations with headers; TV is unchanged: mpegts.js and hls.js read with fetch/XHR, which ignore the declared length); `radio.js`: `media.duration` reads `Infinity` while a proxied progressive stream plays (`setLiveDuration`, otherwise the car player shows 37 hours); safety net `proxyFailed()`: a proxied progressive stream that ends before 120 s (`EARLY_END_S`) with the effects on (or the element wired) makes the page turn the effect off, keep it off through the reload (`sessionStorage` `aabRadioNoEffects`: the app copy of the state may not be updated yet and wins at load), reload and resume the station directly (`aabRadioResume`): an element wired to Web Audio cannot be unwired, so a direct stream on it would be silent; a new `ended` handler also says "No audio from the station" when the server closes a direct stream (before: silence). String `radio_effects_off` (11 locales). Tests: `IptvLiveStreamTest` (3); 148 Kotlin tests and 64 JS tests pass. NOT tried on the phone: the unknown is what the media stack does with a declared length of 2 GB (the `media/blink` sources, where range support and the response handling live, could not be fetched); if the radio with an effect still misbehaves the safety net turns the effect off by itself. The real fix for effects on every station would be reading the proxied stream with fetch and a MediaSource, as hls.js does, instead of `<audio src>`.
  - **The old app name still shown in nine languages, on the car player and in the shared log (15/11/2026, found while preparing the repository rename; user: "si allinea a carweb sarà sta una dimenticanza")**. `values-de|fr|es|pt|ru|ar|hi|hu|zh` still said "AABrowser" in six strings each (`notification_title`, `welcome_title`, `web_location_phone_toast`, `voice_command_hint_wheel`, `voice_command_mic_permission`, `voice_command_open_app`; the base and Italian files had been renamed, the other nine not): 54 places now say CarWeb, with the grammar the new name needs, copied from the forms those languages already use for CarWeb: French elision `d'AABrowser` → `de CarWeb`; Portuguese `da AABrowser` → `do CarWeb` (masculine, like `o CarWeb` everywhere else); Hungarian article and suffixes (`az AABrowser` → `a CarWeb`, `AABrowserben` → `CarWebben`, `AABrowsernek` → `CarWebnek`, `AABrowsert` → `CarWebet`: the article is `az` before a vowel and `a` before a consonant, and the accusative of a word ending in `b` takes a linking vowel); Italian: `concedilo ad CarWeb` → `a CarWeb` (the `d` was for the vowel of AABrowser). Also user-visible: the artist of the weather card on the Android Auto player (`CarMediaService`, now `getString(R.string.app_name)`), the default artist of the audio of any site (`BrowserJavascript`, `"AABrowser Audio"` → `"CarWeb Audio"`; nothing compared it) and the name of the shared log file (`SettingsScreen`, `CarWeb_logs.txt`; the `FileProvider` `cache-path` takes any name). Left on purpose, they are identifiers and not text anyone reads: the log tag `AABrowserPlayback` and the display name `AABrowserWideDisplay`, the console prefixes `AABrowser:`, `AABrowser AdBlock:`, `AABrowser VideoFit:`, `AABrowser DRM:` (the keys used to read the shared log, quoted in this file), `AABrowserTheme`, `FavoritesRepository.OLD_PLAYER_NAME` (migration of the old favorite name), the comments, the package and the names of `AABrowser.md` and `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`. Validation: `:browser:compileDebugKotlin` and `:browser:testDebugUnitTest` pass (145 tests), `node --test` 64 of 64, all `strings.xml` parse and none contains "AABrowser" any more. Not tried on a device.
  - **GitHub repository rename AABrowser → CarWeb: the app works with both names (15/11/2026, user: "preparare l'app a puntare a quei link con fallback sul vecchio aabrowser così che rilascio l'aggiornamento e dopo qualche giorno rinomino il repository e cmq funzioni tutto link in app processi di aggiornamento ecc"; the package stays `com.fcaronte.aabrowser`, another one would need a reinstall)**. New `utils/GitHubRepo.kt` is the only place that knows the names (`NAMES = [CarWeb, AABrowser]`, newest first) and the addresses built from them: `latestReleaseApiUrl(name)`, `releasesPageUrl(name)`, `pageUrl()`, `readmeUrl()`, `userAgent()`, `isOfficialReleaseUrl(url)`. (1) Updates: `UpdateManager.checkForUpdates` asks the API of `CarWeb` first and, when GitHub does not answer 200 (404 until the rename), the one of `AABrowser` (`latestRelease(name)` returns null on a non-200 and the loop goes on; a network error ends the check at once, because the other name is on the same server and would only add 15 s); the name that answered is kept in memory (`GitHubRepo.markLive`). The log has `Update: the repository CarWeb answered HTTP 404` (also in release) before the rename. (2) `isOfficialReleaseUrl`, the check that decides whether an APK goes to KingInstaller (which installs without asking), accepts https, `github.com`, no port or credentials, `/fcaronte/<CarWeb|AABrowser>/releases/download/…`, no `.` or `..` segments (also written `%2e`); it is now on `java.net.URI` instead of `android.net.Uri`, so the JVM tests cover it; owner and name are compared without case (for GitHub they are the same repository) and the first path pieces as written (a `%2F` does not join owner and name). (3) The links the app opens (GitHub icon and "Open README" in Settings, the QR code in the car) and the identification sent to Radio Browser, Apple and LRCLIB (`RadioApi`, `PodcastApi`, `LocalPlayer`, config `client` of the pages) come from `GitHubRepo.pageUrl()`: the name that answered last and, until a check has answered, the OLD one, the only address that works before the rename and, through GitHub's redirect, after it. Removed: the `url_github` and `url_readme` strings (11 files) and the JS fallbacks `config.client || 'CarWeb (https://github.com/fcaronte/AABrowser)'` in `player.js` and `radio.js` (now `'CarWeb'`: the app always sends `client`). The README has no link to the repository (only the credits `kododake/AABrowser` and `slashmax/AABrowser`, and KingInstaller): nothing to change there, and `BrowserJavascript` credits `kododake/AABrowser` too: those are other projects, never replace them. `build.yml` uses neither name nor address (its artifact is still called `AABrowser-<type>`: cosmetic, left). Checked on a public renamed repository (`rg3/youtube-dl` → `ytdl-org/youtube-dl`): the API of the old name answers 301 to `api.github.com/repositories/<id>/…` (`HttpURLConnection` follows it), the web page 301, and `browser_download_url` already carries the NEW name. So versions before this one (they accept only `/fcaronte/AABrowser/releases/download/`) cannot download an update by themselves after the rename ("download failed") and need one manual install. Test `GitHubRepoTest` (9): both names, case, other repositories (kododake, slashmax, KingInstaller, `CarWebX`, another owner), only a release download, tricks with scheme, host, port and credentials, dot segments, `%2e` and `%2F`, links and identification before and after the first answer. Validation: `:browser:compileDebugKotlin` and `:browser:testDebugUnitTest` pass (145 Kotlin tests, 0 failures), `node --test browser/src/test/js/` 64 of 64, all `strings.xml` still parse. NOT tried on a device, and the fallback has not met a real answer of GitHub: the name `CarWeb` does not exist yet, so the 404 → AABrowser path and the answer of `CarWeb` after the rename are reasoned through, plus the redirect behaviour measured on another renamed repository.
  - **Order to follow for the rename (agreed with the user, 15/11/2026)**: (a) publish the release with this change (2.8) on the repository still called AABrowser: older versions check `AABrowser` and accept only addresses with that name, so they update by themselves; (b) wait a few days; (c) rename on GitHub (Settings > General > Repository name): web, git and API addresses of the old name are redirected, and releases, tags, secrets, Actions and stars stay; (d) `git remote set-url origin https://github.com/fcaronte/CarWeb.git` (not before: the new address does not exist until the rename); (e) never create another repository called AABrowser (it would break the redirects). Not done on purpose: README, `build.yml` and the remote are untouched.
  - **Radio stations that start and then freeze (15/11/2026; the user's report was most likely the effect-through-proxy bug in the entry above: these stations exist but are a smaller problem; user: "ci sono alcune stazioni radio che fanno per partire e poi si bloccano ma sembra che ripetano sempre lo stesso pezzo, potrebbero averle tolte?")**. Not an app bug. Tested from the PC the 200 stations the app lists for Italy (`bycountrycodeexact/IT?hidebroken=true&order=clickcount&reverse=true`, `lastcheckok == 1`, `MAX_STATIONS` 200) with a raw-socket client that asks like the WebView (its user agent, `Range: bytes=0-`, redirects, bytes per 5 s over 60 s; for HLS: does `EXT-X-MEDIA-SEQUENCE` advance): 186 fine (163 direct, 23 HLS) and 10 not: 5 gone (Retesport 404, Radio Stereocittà connection refused, Radio Dancefloor 90s, Radio Cecchetto and Venice Classic Radio answer with an HTML page), 2 that never answer (Radio Zeta and RTL 102.5 Best at `streamingv2.shoutcast.com`: TLS handshake, then no HTTP response, also with curl and other user agents), 2 with a frozen source (Virgin Radio Rockstar: Rolling Stones and MUSIC STAR Roger Waters - Pink Floyd, `icy.unitedradio.it/Virgin….mp3`): at every connection the server sends the same 170-250 kB at once (11-15 s of audio at 128 kbit/s) and then nothing or a trickle (about 8 kB/s on a 16 kB/s stream, pauses of 15-25 s); two connections 45 s apart share 95-99% of their frames (`replay.mjs`) against 0-1% for healthy stations (Rai Radio 1, RDS relax, Italian Dance Network): that is "the same piece again, then it blocks" (the burst the server keeps in memory), and 1 frozen HLS list (Rai News 24: `EXT-X-MEDIA-SEQUENCE` unchanged for minutes with 2 s segments; hls.js answers with `fragLoadError` and reloads the list for ever: no sound, no fatal error, "loading" for ever). Radio Browser still lists them (last OK check 11-12/11/2026) because its check only wants the connection to open and some audio to arrive. Data and scripts are outside the repo: `/tmp/aab-radio-data` (the list and the results), `/tmp/aab-radio-scripts` (`streamcheck.mjs`, `summary.mjs`, `replay.mjs`, `hlsloop.mjs`, `subset.mjs`). The user did not say which stations: these five are the ones that behave like that in the top 200 (my detector's first false alarms, RDS relax, Italian Dance Network, FUTURA.FM and Rai Radio Classica, are fine).
  - **Radio: a station that does not start or stops now says so (15/11/2026)**: before, the page stayed mute and said nothing (no handler for stalls: only `playing`, `pause` and `error`). `radio.js`: `START_TIMEOUT_MS` 20 s from the tap to the first `playing` → «Stazione non disponibile» (`S.unavailable`); `STALL_MS` 8 s with `currentTime` not advancing while not paused → «Nessun audio dalla stazione» (`radio_stalled`, new, 11 locales, passed by `RadioApi.page` as `stalled`); it goes away by itself when audio resumes (`playing`, or the time advancing) or on pause; not shown with the Web Audio context suspended (the element stops then: not the station) and not after a `NotAllowedError` of `play()`; `stopWatching()` does not touch the message (`play()` writes «Caricamento…» before `stopStream()`). Netflix (Widevine L1) stays black in any case.
    - **"This app stays black" notice** (`vapp_black_notice`, 11 locales; user: the old notice came out for every app, also the ones that show): no longer tied to a refused request. `smartphone/BlackFrame.kt`: `Sampler` reads a 64x36 sample of the `TextureView` (`getBitmap(Bitmap)` into a reused bitmap, first filled with magenta so that "not read" is told from black; null if the view is not available or not attached); `looksBlack` = at most 3 of 2304 samples above level 16 (the pure black of protected windows; Material's dark #121212 is not black). `VirtualAppSession.frameProbe` (set by `VirtualAppTab` and `WideVirtualApp`) and `checkFrame`: first look 3 s after the launch reply, then once a second; the count restarts when the view is detached or the phone sleeps; it ends at the first sample with something on it; six black samples in a row (about 8 s after the launch) call `onNotice(LOOKS_BLACK)`, once per app per process (`noticed`); at most 40 looks. Main: feedback card; Wide: `CarToast`. A slow start or a dark theme must not trigger it: that is why the black has to last.
    - **TV fullscreen panel** (user: "quando apro a schermo intero con un tocco serve un piccolo pannello per il cambio canale"): `.zap` inside `.stage` (tv.html, tv.css, tv.js): ⏮, logo and name of the channel, ⏭, and a status line that repeats the page's message (the page's `#message` is outside the fullscreen element). It shows only in fullscreen and while the zoom bar shows: `video-zoom.js` puts `bar-shown` on the stage and `attach` now returns `toggleBar` and `keepBar` (the 4 s restart after a tap on the arrows, even while the new stream has no video). The arrows are `step(±1)` over the list shown. A channel that does not start has no video: `isVideo` is also true in fullscreen with a channel selected and a tap on the cover (`.art`) toggles the bar, so one can zap away from a dead channel. No labels on the buttons (generic Android Auto scripts click buttons by label). 19 checks in headless Chrome at 1075x514 and 390x844.
    - **Radio visual effects** (user: "effetti video come per la musica anche sulla radio"): drawing moved to `assets/player/effects.js` (`AABEffects.attach`, `bandLevels`, `EFFECTS`), shared with `player.js`, which keeps its audio graph (gain for the crossfade) and was refactored to use it (same pixels in the test: bars, wave, circle, off, pause, next track). Radio: `<canvas id="visual">` over the cover, `#effects` button after ★ (accent when an effect is on), the same `.sheet` as the music page with only the effects row, `state.effect` saved with the page state (so also in the app copy and the backup). Measured in headless Chrome: a stream of another origin without CORS wired to Web Audio gives only zeros (peak 0) and the element is silent, so the page never wires an element that plays a cross-origin direct stream: with an effect on, and for ever once the element is wired (`createMediaElementSource` cannot be undone), `/api/radio/play` gets `proxy=1` and direct streams go through `IptvProxy` (`RadioApi.viaProxy`, path `/tv/stream/<id>`, same origin); HLS stays with hls.js (MSE, same origin). Choosing an effect while a direct stream plays restarts the station through the proxy (a moment of pause) and then wires it; the `AudioContext` is created inside the tap, or in the first tap of a page opened with an effect saved (a capturing click listener). `IptvProxy.streamMime`: `application/ogg` (Icecast Ogg/Opus) is declared `audio/ogg` instead of MPEG-TS. Strings: the existing `player_options`, `player_effects`, `player_off`, `player_effect_*` go into the Radio page config; no new locale strings. Unit tests `effects.test.js` (7), `RadioApiTest` (viaProxy), `IptvStreamKindTest.mimeDeclaredToTheWebView`; headless Chrome (radiofx: premise, direct stream, restart through the proxy, three effects, off, HLS, a station played after the element is wired, reload of the page, pause; mediafx: the music page after the refactor).

User: the swipe now works in the Main; PosteID still black and the "system does not support" notice also appeared when the lock button was used, and the black screen gave no sign (the phone looked frozen); on the lock screen the app did not keep the phone awake: a double tap woke it and a few seconds later it went off and the car went black again; in the Wide: a button to put the bar beside the app, and the fixed menu seemed dead with an app open (Home did nothing until X); local videos: they play on the user's phone with test-video.co.uk, the users who reported it meant no SOUND; Settings: the tab bar buttons card should be closed by default; desktop then mobile again on YouTube loses the Wide's site fixes; an in-app changelog after an update; a translate button feasibility (also for people without Google); YouTube AdBlock: black until the ad ends even with only the 16x method. Compiled and tested (Kotlin tests, JS tests), NOT tried on a device or in the car:
    - **Phone asleep (lock button)**: the power button wins over any screen wake lock (a lock only holds against the timeout), so the car stayed black. `VirtualScreenPower` rewritten: receives `ACTION_SCREEN_OFF` and `ACTION_SCREEN_ON` while an app is on the car; on off: `asleep = true` and the Shizuku service is asked to wake the phone (`wakePhone`: the wake lock taken again with `ACQUIRE_CAUSES_WAKEUP` plus the hidden `PowerManager.wakeUp`, up to 3 tries, 0.6 s and 1.2 s apart); on on: `asleep = false` and the standby screen is started again over the lock screen; a touch on the car screen (`touchOnCar`: Main `OnTouchListener` DOWN, Wide `onClick`/`onScroll`/`onFling`) wakes a sleeping phone, rate limited to 1 s (AnyAuto's way: its README says the power button wins and the next tap on the car wakes the phone); four switch-offs within 15 s stop the automatic wake (a phone that will not stay on: no flicker) until the next tap on the car; the Main (Compose state `VirtualScreenPower.asleep`) and the Wide (`statusView`) show "the phone went to sleep, tap the car screen to wake it" (`vapp_phone_asleep`) instead of staying black. AIDL: `keepPhoneAwake` returns a String (`OK held interactive=..`) and `wakePhone()` is new (transaction 12), `SERVICE_VERSION` 4: the app log (tag `ShizukuAppBridge`, `VirtualScreenPower`) says what each step did. The cause of the original failure (the receiver, the wake, the standby over the keyguard) is NOT proven: if the car still goes black after the lock button, the log lines `screen off while an app is on the car`, `waking the phone (try n)`, `wakePhone -> OK interactive=.. was=..` tell where it stops. The protected-content notice came back after the lock because the display was recreated: it is now shown once per app per process and only when the service says `secure=refused`.
    - **Wide**: taps outside the app layer (the fixed menu) are no longer swallowed by `virtualApp.onClick` (`WideVirtualApp.contains`), so Home, Back, search work with an app open; the app's side bar has the beside/over button of the Main (`ic_vapp_dock`, `AppSettings.virtualSidebarDocked`, `marginStart` of the TextureView, touches offset by `appOffset`, RTL aware through `Callbacks.isRtl`).
    - **Settings**: `TabBarActionsCard` closed by default (title, number of visible buttons, arrow).
    - **Desktop then mobile on YouTube (`6bc5731`)**: root cause found: `DesktopModeSites` saved the choice with the exact host of the page (`m.youtube.com` from the mobile page) while the desktop page is `www.youtube.com`, so the desktop page asked for the mobile identity (the site bounced back), the way back removed another entry, and a stale `1|m.youtube.com` made `m.youtube.com` ask for the desktop identity: a mixed state where the mobile layout fixes never ran. `siteKey` now drops `www.` and the `m.`, `mobile.`, `touch.` prefixes (only when a dotted name remains: `m.com` stays); entries saved with a mobile host by older versions are dropped at `init`. Tests `mobileAndDesktopHostsOfASiteShareTheChoice`, `oldChoicesSavedWithAMobileHostAreDropped`. Checked in headless Chrome (`/tmp/aab-videofit-test/seq.mjs`: mobile, desktop, mobile in one session): the mobile page after a desktop visit is identical to the first (two columns, `div.related-items-container` scrolls), `www.youtube.com` with a mobile identity redirects to `m.`, so the cause was the app's state, not the site.
    - **What's new (`4d75b24`)**: `utils/WhatsNew.kt` (last version code seen in `aa_browser_settings`, `whats_new_seen_code`; fresh install = `lastUpdateTime - firstInstallTime < 10 s` shows nothing; notes are `assets/whatsnew/<versionName>/<language>.txt`, app language else `en.txt`; `latest()` for Settings), `ui/WhatsNewDialog.kt` (`WhatsNewHost` in `MainScreen`, phone only), Settings button "Novità". Draft notes for 2.8 (it, en) in `assets/whatsnew/2.8/`: to be edited at release time; for each release add the folder (bilingual notes, Italian first); other languages fall back to English.
    - **YouTube AdBlock (`d219cef`)**: searched the web (14/11/2026): no new confirmed method. uBlock Origin's own filters (snapshot of 23/10/2026) still use `json-prune` plus a rewrite that renames `adPlacements` to `no_ads` in player, watch and playlist responses (and blocks `googlevideo` `initplayback` requests of the TV client with an `oad` parameter); the uAssets issues #34947 and #35039 and #34657 report black screens at the start of videos with uBO Lite and nobody has a fix, so the black screen is a general problem of ads stitched into the stream, not of our script. Our hypothesis for the black screen with only the 16x method: the stitched ad arrives at real-time pace, so at 16x the buffer empties and the video stalls. `adSpeed()`: if the ad video does not advance for 1.5 s (after the first 2 s) the rest of that ad plays at normal speed (muted, visible). Console lines `AABrowser AdBlock: ad started (...)`, `ad +Ns ready= paused= moving|still rate= ahead= media=`, `ad ended after N s`, `the ad video stalls at 16x...` (logcat `chromium`, shared with the log button) say what really happens during an ad. Not tested with a real ad (headless Chrome never gets one).
    - **Local videos without sound**: the users meant no sound, not no picture. The WebView decodes video through the phone's MediaCodec (H.264 everywhere, HEVC/VP9/AV1 depends on the phone) and audio through Chromium's own decoders: AC-3, E-AC-3 and DTS (movies, recordings) are not decoded, and on Pixels the system has no Dolby decoder either, so the video plays silent. test-video.co.uk samples are AAC, so they play. Diagnosis added: `/api/tracks/<id>` (`MediaExtractor` + `MediaCodecList`: the MIME of the first audio and video track and whether the phone has a decoder) and `player.js` `checkSound()` (3 s after `playing`, if `webkitAudioDecodedByteCount` is still 0 and the element is not muted, it asks and shows "The sound of this video (AC-3) cannot be played here" or "This video has no sound track", 9 s). A real fix needs software decoding: Media3 with an FFmpeg extension (small, but it must be built or taken from a community build such as NextLib) or libVLC (about 25-30 MB per ABI); both are native players, so the page, the Media Session and `CarMediaService` would have to be redone; a lighter way is a native audio-only sidecar synced with the muted `<video>` (about a week).
    - **Translate button, feasibility (no code)**: (1) a button that opens the page through a web translator: Google (`translate.google.com/translate?sl=auto&tl=<lang>&u=<url>`, content on `*.translate.goog`, still seen in 2026, but it needs Google reachable and breaks interactive features and cookies) and Microsoft/Yandex (the old URL formats are from 2007-2008 and Yandex's site translation is a form on translate.yandex.com: no documented URL parameter), so only Google is reliable; (2) in-page translation done by the app: collect the text nodes with JavaScript, translate in batches natively (no CSP problem) through a provider chosen in Settings (Microsoft through the Edge token endpoint, unofficial; DeepL or Yandex Cloud or Azure with the user's own key; no key shipped), put the text back, undo, MutationObserver for dynamic pages: about 2 days, works without Google if the provider is Microsoft or Yandex; (3) on-device Bergamot (Firefox Translations, MPL-2.0, WebAssembly, models of tens of MB per direction from Mozilla): offline and private, but heavy to integrate (more than a week) and slow on a phone. Recommendation: (2) with Microsoft as the default for Russian users and Google elsewhere.

If it says granted and the app is still black, the app protects itself (it can detect a virtual display); nothing to do from CarWeb.
  - **Fit / Fill / Stretch on YouTube for computers, with a log (14/11/2026, `1a57fdd`; user report: "non funziona più su youtube")**. Reproduced with headless Chrome 157 (WebView user agent, touch, a CDP driver in `/tmp/aab-videofit-test`, not in the repo: a file under `browser/src/test/` would be run by `node --test`, which treats every file under a `test` folder as a test): on `m.youtube.com` the fullscreen element is `div#player-container-id` and the script works in portrait and in a car-like landscape (1075x514), but on `www.youtube.com` (the desktop UI, which the per-site desktop switch now really gives since `e74aeeb`) the fullscreen element is `<html>`, as tall as its content (0 px): Fill and Stretch gave the video `height:0` and it vanished while the label still cycled. `fullscreenBox()`: the box is the screen when the fullscreen element is `<html>`/`<body>` or smaller than half the screen (the real element otherwise); the label goes into `<body>` in that case; `window` `resize` applies the mode again. Also: videos inside open shadow roots (the rules are adopted in that root), a retry for about 3 s when the player adds the video after fullscreen starts, and console lines `AABrowser VideoFit: …` (shapes and sizes, no addresses; logcat tag `chromium`, shared with the log button in Settings): `ready`, `fullscreen <element>, video <element>`, `mode … not applied: only the video is in fullscreen`, `applied <mode> covers= position= box= video=`, `label shown in`, `tap in fullscreen without a video`. After the fix: 4 runs in headless Chrome (desktop and mobile, Fill/Stretch/Fit). Not tested in the app's WebView: if it still fails on the device, the log lines say what the page did (a bare `<video>` in fullscreen would be the next suspect: no label is possible there).
  - **Phone apps on the car screen, reworked after the user's test of 13/11 (14/11/2026, `c4f3615`, `91f8d76`)**. User report: no touch in the car on the Shizuku apps, no management of the phone screen (the black "CarWeb attivo" screen), the virtual screen not showing in the car ("Apro l'app…" and nothing, only now and then), Netflix black, apps should be landscape in the car. Done (compiled, tests pass, NOT tested on a device or in the car):
    - **Touch in the Wide**: `car/WideVirtualApp.kt` (TextureView inside the Presentation, above the page and the fixed menu; side bar back / apps / keyboard / close next to the menu; the top-right action, which replaces the AdBlock shield while an app is shown, shows or hides the bar; `WideScreen.onGetTemplate` swaps it back on the Wide home). Android Auto's `onClick`, `onScroll`, `onFling`, `onScale` go to `VirtualTouch` (taps, drags, flings and pinch rebuilt as real touches); a tap on the bar goes to the nearest button. Main: the TextureView forwards its raw `MotionEvent`s (display size = view size).
    - **Visibility in the car**: the view is a TextureView, not a SurfaceView (a SurfaceView stayed empty in the projected window and under Compose layers); `VirtualAppSession.step` (1 view has the surface, 2 creating the display, 3 starting the app) is shown as "Apro l'app… (n/3)" to tell where it stops; Main tab and Wide retry by themselves when Shizuku's binder arrives or the permission is given (`ShizukuManager.registerListeners` returns its remover; `status()` first tries `tryBindShizuku`). Most likely cause of the intermittent "Apro l'app…": on Android 17 the virtual display shares the phone's display group, so a sleeping or locked phone pauses it (a display with its own group is requested but not always granted: `phoneGroup()` tells).
    - **Phone screen** (`smartphone/VirtualScreenPower.kt`, `StandbyActivity.kt`): while an app is in front on the car and the display shares the phone's group (`sharesPhoneGroup`), the shell service holds a `SCREEN_BRIGHT_WAKE_LOCK | ACQUIRE_CAUSES_WAKEUP` and `StandbyActivity` (black, minimum brightness, `showWhenLocked`/`turnScreenOn`, "CarWeb attivo / Scorri per sbloccare", a vertical swipe of at least 72 dp, Back or Home gives the phone back, with `requestDismissKeyguard` on a locked phone) is started FROM THE SHELL (`launch` with display 0: no background-activity-start limits), so it also opens over the lock screen; `ACTION_SCREEN_OFF` re-engages it (1 s guard against ping-pong); after the last app leaves the car there is an 8 s grace, then everything goes back to normal; once the user took the phone the wake lock is released (the system timeout applies) and the standby comes back when the system turns the screen off. `daemon(false)`: if the app dies Shizuku destroys the service, which releases the lock. Settings: switch "standby screen" (`AppSettings.virtualStandby`, on). The activity is exported (the shell starts it) and closes at once when no app is on the car.
    - **Landscape**: the display has the shape of the view that shows it, rotation 0 is frozen and orientation requests are ignored (`keepOrientation`: `setIgnoreOrientationRequest`, `setFixedToUserRotation`, `freezeDisplayRotation`): landscape on a landscape car screen, portrait on a vertical one; portrait-only apps are pillarboxed (like AnyAuto).
    - **DRM / Netflix**: Widevine L1 video decoded by the secure hardware path cannot be drawn on a virtual display (black); an optional "protected content" switch (`AppSettings.virtualSecureContent`, off) asks for a `SECURE` display first (it only helps FLAG_SECURE windows, needs CAPTURE_SECURE_VIDEO_OUTPUT and may be refused: the service then falls back to a normal one); the flags actually granted are in the log (`VirtualDisplayService`: `flags=0x… orientation=[…] keyboard=…`, copied to the app log by `ShizukuAppBridge`).

The same commit carries the two switches of the apps (standby, protected content) and their strings in 11 locales.

  - **Assessments given to the user (14/11/2026, no code)**: (a) a VLC-like engine for the local player and IPTV: not before knowing which files fail (the WebView plays what Chromium's MediaCodec + FFmpeg demuxers play: no AC-3/E-AC-3/DTS audio, MKV/AVI/TS with such codecs, MPEG-4 part 2, WMV, HEVC only on some phones); libVLC would be a native player (not the HTML pages), +25-30 MB per ABI, and everything that now comes for free from the page (Media Session, scripts, `CarMediaService`) would have to be redone; the lighter way is to report the failing codec in the page (`MediaExtractor` MIME types) and, for the car, open the file with VLC or MX Player on the virtual screen. (b) YouTube in our own player: extracting the stream means reimplementing the signature and `n` descrambling, PO tokens and SABR, which break every few weeks (NewPipe, yt-dlp), breaks the YouTube Terms of Service more than the AdBlock does, loses SponsorBlock, login and live, and is GPL (NewPipe extractor): not recommended; the real need (Fit / Fill / Stretch and zoom) is rendering, fixable on the page's own `<video>`; a possible later step is a native label over the custom view (works even with a bare `<video>` in fullscreen, where no page element can be shown).

  - **Fit / Fill / Stretch for the videos of the sites (10/11/2026, user: for online videos no zoom, only the modes) and the fixed menu as the video's edge (user test: in Stretch the CarWeb Media video spread under the fixed menu)**. (1) `setVideoMenu(onTap, visible)` replaces `showSidebarOverVideo`: the Wide knows the choice, so with "Menu sempre" the player video stops at the menu's edge (`pageLp` margin on the menu side) and with "Menu al tocco" it goes under it without resizing at every tap (`675443e`). (2) `assets/scripts/video-fit.js` + `utils/VideoFit.kt`, document start in every frame of Main and Wide: in fullscreen a tap shows the mode at the top centre for 4 s, a tap on it cycles; remembered per site (`aabVideoFit` in the site's localStorage); rules in a constructed `CSSStyleSheet` with `!important`; on YouTube the video element is as big as the film (inline width/height/left/top, `.html5-video-container` 0 px high), so Fill and Stretch also give it the fullscreen element's box in px. Found in headless Chrome: a popover shown over the fullscreen element is drawn but gets no taps (Chromium makes everything outside the fullscreen element inert), so the label lives in a closed shadow root inside the fullscreen element; with a bare `<video>` in fullscreen there is no label. Checked on m.youtube.com (WebView user agent, touch) and two plain pages: 13 checks. Not tested on a device.

Apple charts of 18 countries (top 30 each, 540 shows) + `itunes.apple.com/lookup` for the feed address: 531 public feeds, 513 read, last episode opens 512/513, oldest 507/510 (real first bytes through a Range request), MP3 493 + M4A 19, seek (206) 512/512, http 18. China is the exception (14/30 feeds read, 403 from here). Less followed shows (Apple search, results after the first 40, 7 countries, 260 shows): 246 feeds read, last episode 241/246, oldest 203/208, MP3 172 + M4A 68, 69% silent for over a year but still working. Feeds are big: median 1.2 MB, p95 8 MB, max 29 MB, up to 6,194 episodes: read natively, keep only the latest ones. Catalogs: by country only Apple, no key: `itunes.apple.com/{cc}/rss/toppodcasts/limit=N/explicit=true/json` answered 18/18 in under 0.5 s (AntennaPod uses it), the newer `rss.marketingtools.apple.com` failed 8/18 in the same sequential run (502, timeouts); search `itunes.apple.com/search?media=podcast&country=` (about 20 calls a minute). Podcast Index: free key required on every call, `trending` has `lang` and no country, its Terms say credentials stay confidential (so not in this repository; a CI secret would be the way); fyyd by language only and thin (it 34, ru 8, fr 50, ar 13, hi/hu/zh 0); gpodder stale. Copyright: nothing goes in the repository (no catalog, audio or covers: downloaded at run time like the AdBlock lists), audio plays straight from the publisher's server through the public RSS feed as in every podcast app (AntennaPod, GPL, same Apple addresses). Apple's API text limits "promotional content" (previews, album art) to promoting the store: use Apple for names and feed addresses, covers from the publisher's feed. Not legal advice. Proposed v1: page `/podcast` beside TV and Radio with the same list menu, the country's chart as default list, search by name, episodes (latest N), direct `<audio>`, resume per episode; Settings section renamed "TV, Radio e Podcast" with RSS address and OPML file.

  - **Photos and videos in the Share menu (09/11/2026, user request)**: `ShareToCarActivity` also takes `image/*` and `video/*` (`ACTION_SEND`, one at a time). The read grant ends with the activity that received it, which closes at once, and gallery providers do not offer persistable grants, so `SharedMedia.accept` calls `grantUriPermission` on the app's own package (a global grant, until the next shared file or a reboot); no copy, so a big video costs nothing. Only `content:` addresses that are not the app's own provider (the activity is exported). Page `https://player.local/shared/<id>` (`assets/player/shared.html`: black page, `<video controls autoplay>` or `<img>`, in memory only: after an app restart it says to share again), file on `/api/shared` with the token through the same Range code as CarWeb Media (`LocalPlayer.serveFile`); HEIC/HEIF go through `ImageDecoder` to JPEG (at most 2048 px) because the WebView does not read them. `Command.OpenLink(url, label)`: the message shows the file name. `mediaSectionOf` is null for `/shared` (else voice commands went to a page that cannot answer and the Media favorite could switch to that tab). Main `update` block now keeps the gesture requirement off on player pages like `onPageStarted` (with autoplay off, the default, it could block the video). Texts that said "link" reworded (`share_to_car_waiting`, `_no_link`, `_not_connected`, 11 locales), `share_to_car_unavailable` new. Not tested on a device; the self-grant comes from reading `UriGrantsManagerService`, from memory.
