  - **Back and Forward keys, report from a Russian user (09/11/2026; fine for the user and in French)**. Language: nothing in the path depends on it (tab bar and radial menu → `backTrigger`/`forwardTrigger` → `canGoBack`/`goBack`; `back_button`/`forward_button` are only accessibility labels, correct in `values-ru`; search engines and default favorites are the same URLs), and Cyrillic addresses are not the cause (`onPageFinished` and `getUrl()` both give the GURL spec). Real causes, read in Chromium main: (a) `AwWebContentsObserver.processFailedLoad`: a cancelled navigation (`ERR_ABORTED`: Back pressed while the page has not answered yet, a second Back, a link stopped by `shouldOverrideUrlLoading`, a blocked 204) still calls `onPageFinished` with its address "for backwards compatibility"; the tab stored it and `LaunchedEffect(url)`, seeing another `webView.url` (`getVisibleUrl`, the pending Back), called `loadUrl`: Back undone, forward pages cut. On a fast connection the window is milliseconds, with a slow one (VPN, blocked or throttled sites) seconds. Now the tab records only what the WebView shows (`view.url == url`) and never reloads what the WebView itself reported (`lastReportedUrl`); the Wide's `lastUrl`/`saveUrl` ignore cancelled navigations too. (b) `WebViewContentsClientAdapter.handleJsBeforeUnload`: `onJsBeforeUnload` was not overridden, the WebView needs an Activity for its dialog and ours is the localized context, so `receiver.cancel()`: on a page asking "leave?" after a tap Back, Forward and links did nothing. Now the same dialog as `confirm()` (Main and Wide). The report does not say which site, app or connection: if it still happens, ask those. Not tested on a device.
  - **Update not detected during a GitHub outage (08/11/2026, user report: 2.6 not found, also with "Update test" on)**: not the app. `api.github.com` answered 504 or timed out from the PC and from the phone (`adb shell curl`, the phone has `/system/bin/curl`), the releases page gave 503 and the APK link timed out; GitHub incident "Increase in timeouts for some unauthenticated users", open from 10:36 UTC. `checkForUpdates` treats every answer other than 200 as "no update" and logs nothing, so the test switch changes nothing: the version is compared only after a valid answer. The check runs again at every opening of the Dashboard and at every creation of the Wide. The first tag `2.6` pointed to `ff10b3a` (origin/main at the time: the release was created before the push, as had happened with 2.5.1); the user deleted and recreated release and tag, now at `234beba` (checked with `git ls-remote`). The release must be created after the push. Offered, not done: a log line with the HTTP code.
  - **Release 2.6 prepared (08/11/2026, user request: "prepara bump 2.6 version readme e changelog")**: `versionCode 18` / `versionName "2.6"`. README (EN + IT): the "new in" labels now mark only 2.6 (Radio, SponsorBlock, Share to the car, AdBlock against pop-ups and invisible links, voice for stations and channels; the 2.4 and 2.5 labels are gone, as was done for 2.3), "search" added to the English CarWeb Media bullet, and the "Go to the English version" link fixed (its anchor still had the name from before the rename). Release notes (bilingual, each language ending with the donation links as in 2.4; given to the user, not stored in the repo): everything after the published 2.5.1, that is from `7b0bd06` on (`b03affb` and `ff10b3a` were already in 2.5.1); they open by saying that from 2.5.1 or older the update must be installed by hand once (the old updater can refuse the file as not valid, and its notification button does nothing). Checked: all string keys present in the 11 locales; the updater reads tag `2.6` as newer than `2.5.1` (also in the 2.5.1 code); the Radio favorite reaches existing users (`DEFAULTS_ADDED_IN`, `"2" to 6`); `:browser:assembleRelease` passes `lintVitalRelease` (CI runs the same task and the project has no lint block) and gives `CarWeb-v2.6.apk`, version 18 / 2.6, signed with the release key.
  - **Share entry only while connected to the car (08/11/2026, user test: SponsorBlock and Share work; "se c'è un modo per mostrare carweb nelle app di condivisione solo se sono connesso in auto … tirare via l'opzione")**. Option `share_to_car` removed (`AppSettings.shareToCar`, Settings card, `share_to_car_label`/`_desc`, 11 locales). Android has no conditional share entry, so `utils/CarShareTarget` enables `ShareToCarActivity` (disabled in the manifest) from `CarConnection(app).type.observeForever`, set up in `CarApplication.onCreate`. Limit, read in Android Auto 18.1: `CAR_CONNECTION_UPDATED` is sent implicitly (`ncd.dz`, `ncc`: `sendBroadcast` with only `FLAG_RECEIVER_FOREGROUND`), so a manifest receiver gets nothing and the entry appears only once CarWeb's process runs with the car connected (app opened in the car, or Android Auto bound to its player). If the process dies during a drive the entry can stay on after it: `ShareToCarActivity` reads the connection at share time, says `share_to_car_not_connected` (11 locales) and turns itself off; the next app start fixes it too. `PACKAGE_CHANGED` from the toggle: in Android Auto it is read only by the phone call service and the Play services watcher, so the car session is not disturbed. Not tested on a device.
  - **AdBlock list to choose, HaGeZi Multi PRO by default (08/11/2026, user: "era meglio HaGeZi Multi PRO? … una a piacimento")**. Compared on the example site's hosts: PRO (225,105 rules) blocks `nh.daffytrainel.com` (the pop-under loader), `smockeddesmoid.qpon`, `click.togglevpn.app`, which the AdGuard DNS filter (190,359) does not; AdGuard alone has `qj.lathielustly.click` and `whos.amung.us`; neither has `akqcrzyoqktvz.online`, `hitlytix.com`, `track.rise-u.vip`. On about 90 everyday hosts neither blocks a site or a CDN (Google, YouTube, DAZN, WhatsApp, Spotify, Netflix, Amazon, Facebook, Stripe, captchas, SponsorBlock, LRCLIB, Radio Browser, IPTV-org, Open-Meteo); both block analytics such as Conviva and Segment, PRO also Braze and `firebaselogging-pa`. HaGeZi's plain `domains/` folder gave 404 because the files are in `adblock/` (`||domain^`, the format we read best). Settings > AdBlock: "Lista da usare" (`SettingsChoiceList`: `AdBlockLists.SOURCES` HaGeZi PRO, PRO++, NORMAL, AdGuard; "Altra lista (indirizzo)" with a dialog, phone only, `adblock_lists_custom*`, `adblock_lists_choice`, 11 locales); `AdBlockSettings.listKey`/`customListUrl`. File format `AAB2` with address and name (the old file is ignored: the first start downloads again); downloaded also when the saved list is not the chosen one; if the choice changes during a download the new one follows; the old list stays until the new one arrives. Limits: 40 MB read (`LimitedStream`), 3,000,000 domains, under 1000 domains refused for known lists (custom: at least 1). Parser checked on the real files: 225,105 (PRO), 243,806 (PRO++), 206,256 (NORMAL), 189,909 + 203 exceptions (AdGuard). Not tested on a device.
  - **AdBlock: transparent links over the player (08/11/2026, user test: the site can be browsed but when starting a stream "vedo la pubblicità bloccata ma non riesco a chiuderla")**. Reproduced in headless Chrome on the user's site (driver in the scratchpad: CDP from Node, WebView user agent, list applied through `Fetch`, the real `POPUP_INTERCEPTOR_JS`, a fake bridge, `--disable-site-isolation-trials` as in WebView). `sportora.ru/stream/<id>` → `/embed?url=…` (four ad scripts) → `damitv.st/player/hls` (obfuscated, the real `<video>`). Every tap on the player hits `<a id="dontfoid" target="_blank" href="ad">` in the player frame: `position:fixed`, as big as the frame, z-index 2147483647, transparent, empty (a real trusted tap on a real link, so the `window.open` guard never sees it). So the tap never reached the player. Without multiple windows Blink opens `_blank` in the top frame (`create_window.cc`, `page == old_page`): the ad replaced the whole page, or `PopupGuard` stopped it and the link stayed over the player. The tap on the player was what the ad wanted, blocked or not. Also checked in the Chromium source: `shouldOverrideUrlLoading` sees only main-frame http(s) navigations (`AwContentBrowserClient::ShouldOverrideUrlLoading`), and 204/205 responses are not rendered (`NavigationRequest`). Now in `POPUP_INTERCEPTOR_JS`, capture `click` on trusted taps: an overlay link (no text or media, at least half of the frame, fixed or absolute, transparent, cross-site for `PopupGuard` with no tapped link) is removed and the tap is re-sent to the element below; other `_blank` links inside frames go to `openInNewTab` and the page stays (not `download` links, not in the main frame). `AdBlockHost.blockedResponse`: blocked pages (`Accept: text/html`) answer 204, so what was there stays instead of a blank page (a script moving a frame to an ad host). Checked: on the real page the first tap removes the link and reaches the player, no window; the next tap the ad script tries through a hidden `about:blank` frame and is blocked. Synthetic pages in Chrome: overlay in a frame and in the main frame (button below gets the tap), visible `_blank` link in a frame (`openInNewTab`, frame untouched), overlay toward the top page's site (not removed), link with a background image, small transparent link and `download` link untouched, `_blank` in the main frame as before. Not tested on a device; the 204 in a WebView is not verified.
  - **SponsorBlock on YouTube (07/11/2026, idea 3 of the agreed order)**: `assets/scripts/sponsorblock.js` registered at document start for `YOUTUBE_ORIGINS` in Main and Wide (`SponsorBlock.script`, read once from the assets). Checked before writing: YouTube's enforced CSP has no `connect-src` (only script-src, base-uri, object-src, trusted types) and the API answers with `access-control-allow-origin: *`, so the page fetches directly; response format checked on a real reply (prefix `5f6b`, 134 videos: `[{videoID, segments:[{category, actionType, segment:[s,e], UUID, …}]}]`). Hash-prefix request (4 hex of SHA-256 of the id), `credentials: 'omit'`, only `actionType` skip, at least 1 s, never with `ad-showing`/`ad-interrupting`, once per part (going back is respected), stops 0.3 s before the end, toast in the page (inside the fullscreen element when there is one). Bridge `sponsorBlockCategories()` ("[]" = off) and `sponsorBlockMessage()` (`sponsorblock_skipped`) in both bridges. Settings card after the YouTube quality: switch (on by default) + "Cosa saltare" (`SponsorBlock.LEVEL_SPONSOR` / `LEVEL_DEFAULT` = sponsor, selfpromo, interaction / `LEVEL_ALL` adds intro, outro, preview, music_offtopic; filler never), strings `sponsorblock_*` (11 locales). Data license CC BY-NC-SA 4.0, credited in the README. Tests: `browser/src/test/js/sponsorblock.test.js` (ids, categories, position, once, hash prefix); the page runtime run in Node with a fake YouTube page and a mocked API (no id in the request, chosen categories only, skip + message, once, no skip during ads, reload on a new video). Not tested on a device.
  - **AdBlock step 2: a real list (07/11/2026)**. `utils/AdBlockLists`: the AdGuard DNS filter, chosen after comparing lists on the user's example: it has the example's ad domain (`wayanatomyunavailable.com`) and the counters, StevenBlack (80k) does not; the HaGeZi raw paths gave 404 that day. Real list parsed on the PC: 189,748 domains, 203 exceptions, 1.8 s; lookup under 1 µs; Google, YouTube, DAZN (www, akamaized live, playback API), WhatsApp, Gemini, Spotify, Netflix, Amazon, Facebook connect, googletagmanager not blocked; google-analytics, histats, whos.amung.us, googlesyndication blocked. Downloaded at app start when older than 3 days (and from Settings > AdBlock > "Aggiorna", row with source, count and date: strings `adblock_lists_*`, 11 locales), parsed line by line, kept as sorted FNV-1a 64-bit hashes in `noBackupFilesDir/adblock_list.bin` (header with the date; temp file + rename), swapped atomically; a list under 1000 domains is refused. `AdBlockHost.isAdHost` = built-in hosts or the list (YouTube hosts never); used for subresources (`shouldBlock`) and the main frame (`PopupGuard`). New generic rule `AdBlockHost.isRotatingAdScript`: third-party `/xx/yy/zz/<32 hex>.js` where the folders are the start of the name (the example's script, Adsterra style: domains change constantly). Tests `AdBlockListsTest` (6: formats, subdomains, exceptions, `$badfilter`, skipped rules, the rotating rule). Not tested on a device: first start downloads about 1.5 MB.
  - **AdBlock step 1: pop-ups and pop-unders (07/11/2026, user report: streaming sites full of ads, not usable; example broadcasting-rotterdam.nl/sportora-ru)**. Cause found in our code: `GoogleLoginManager.POPUP_INTERCEPTOR_JS` (document start, all frames) sent every `window.open` to `AndroidBridge.openInNewTab`, which opened it in a new tab (Main) or in place of the page (Wide) after any tap: pop-under scripts open on every click. Now the script records the link under the last trusted tap (`closest('a[href]')`, 10 s) and asks `AndroidBridge.allowsPopup(url, link)`; `openInNewTab`/`openPopup` take the tapped link too and check again natively (a page can call the bridge directly). `utils/PopupGuard`: allowed = same site as the main page (`siteOf`: last two labels, three for `co.uk`-like), same site as the tapped link, sign-in (words at the start of a host or path token: oauth, login, signin, auth, sso, account; "sign-in"; payment sites paypal, stripe, klarna, satispay, scalapay), never a host of the AdBlock lists. Blocked windows get a fake window object (with null many scripts move the page itself). `HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click` and untrusted `click` `dispatchEvent` on anchors toward other sites are blocked too. Main frame (`shouldOverrideUrlLoading` in both screens): ad hosts never (stays on the page instead of the old blank page), and for 2 s after a script window (`PopupGuard.Attempts`, one per tab / Wide) no other site reached by gesture unless it is the tapped link's site or a sign-in. Google sign-in URLs are now recognized by parsed host and path (before, any URL containing "accounts.google.com" in its query passed). AdBlock off or the site's shield off = old behavior. `AdBlockHost.isAdHost`. Tests: `PopupGuardTest`; the real interceptor script run in Node with a fake page (8 scenarios: fake window, tapped link passed, Google sign-in through the original `window.open`, fake Google in a parameter, other sign-ins in the app popup, `a.click()` and synthetic clicks, relative URLs). Not tested on a device.
  - **Share from the phone to the car (07/11/2026, user request; idea 1 of the agreed order)**: Settings switch "Condividi verso l'auto" (`AppSettings.shareToCar`, pref `share_to_car`, off) enables `ShareToCarActivity` (`ACTION_SEND` text/plain, `android:enabled="false"` in the manifest, translucent, finishes at once; synced at app start because a restored backup brings the pref but not the component state). `utils/SharedLink.find()` takes the first http(s) address of the shared text (closing punctuation dropped, a `)` kept when it belongs to the address), YouTube links lose `si`/`feature`/`pp` and go to m.youtube.com (`youtu.be/ID` → `watch?v=ID`), so they reuse the YouTube tab; a bare domain goes through `UrlUtils.asUrlOrNull`. `VoiceCommandRouter.openInCar(url)` dispatches `Command.OpenLink` with `carOnly`: `Plan.Load` kind `LINK` (message `share_to_car_opened` in the car), `autoplay` for YouTube videos (watch, shorts, live, embed, youtu.be): the Wide sets `voiceAutoplay` before loading, the Main asks `requestAutoplay` and the tab consumes it in `onPageStarted` (before YouTube tries to play). Phone toast: sent / waiting (no car app open: 60 s pending, delivered only to a car screen) / no link. Router change: registrations are a list with `inCar` (the phone Main and the car Main both register as MAIN and used to overwrite each other, so car voice commands could reach the phone Main): the car one wins, the phone one is used only when no car screen of that kind is open. Strings `share_to_car_*` (7, 11 locales). Tests `SharedLinkTest`, `VoiceCommandsTest.sharedLinks`. Not tested on a device.
  - **Next ideas assessed, waiting for the user's choice (07/11/2026)**. (1) Share from the phone to the car, low: an `ACTION_SEND` text/plain activity, disabled in the manifest and enabled by a Settings switch (`setComponentEnabledSetting`, so it is not in everyone's share sheet); the first http(s) URL of `EXTRA_TEXT` goes through `VoiceCommandRouter` as a load (same screen choice, 60 s pending when no car screen is open, bring to front; a YouTube video starts like "metti"). (2) SponsorBlock, low-medium: a YouTube script in Main and Wide, `GET https://sponsor.ajay.app/api/skipSegments/<first 4 hex of sha256(videoId)>?categories=[…]` (the video id is not sent), skip on `timeupdate`, never during ads; switch + categories (11 locales); the data is CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 (attribution in README/Settings). (3) AdBlock: today only ~65 hard-coded hosts (`AdBlockHost.defaultAdHosts`) plus YouTube-only scripts, no popup control (`multi_window` is off by default, so a window opened by a tap, a pop-under, probably replaces the page in Main and Wide; with it on the Main shows any `window.open` as its popup, `onCreateWindow` has no checks). The user's example (broadcasting-rotterdam.nl/sportora-ru, a Webador page) embeds `sportora.ru`, which loads `wayanatomyunavailable.com/c7/93/5c/<32 hex>.js` (random domain, Adsterra-style path) plus counters (histats, whos.amung.us, hitlytix); none is blocked now. Steps: pop-up/pop-under control (navigation or new window toward another site only from a tapped link to it), medium-low; public lists downloaded and refreshed by the app + URL patterns for ad networks that rotate domains, medium; full uBlock-like engine (EasyList, element hiding, scriptlets; e.g. Brave's adblock-rust through JNI, MPL-2.0), high. (4) Audio/video delay, high: the sound comes about 0.5 s after the picture, so the picture must wait: only in the Wide and only for a fullscreen video, a GL frame queue between the VirtualDisplay and the Android Auto surface (`virtualDisplay.setSurface` swap), about 140 MB of GPU memory for 0.5 s at 2217x1052, taps on the video controls shown late while active; not possible in the Main. Suggested order: share, AdBlock steps 1-2, SponsorBlock, delay last.
  - **No-op session activity removed (07/11/2026, user question: leftover?)**: `mediaservice/NoOpActivity` and `CarMediaService.setWideActive` / `MediaSessionManager.setWideActive` (session activity = an empty activity while the Wide was not in use) were the October attempt to stop the player card tap from opening the Wide. Android Auto never used it for the tap (it reads the session extra `android.media.session.BROWSE_SERVICE`, or opens the first app of the package), and on the phone it made the system media controls open nothing. The session now has no activity, as when the Wide was in use.

Main: «CarWebWide» button at the top of the dashboard, only in the car (`DashboardScreen(onOpenWide)`, `MainScreen(openWide)`, `MainCarActivity.openWide()` = AA SDK `startCarActivity` with the Wide's component). Wide: «↗ CarWeb» on the home (`HomePage.OPEN_MAIN` = `about:toggle_open_main`, so only from the home like the other toggles) → `MainHandOver.openMainSoon`. Checked in Android Auto 18.1 (APK pulled from the phone, read only): `CarUiEntry.startCarActivity` → `AppDecorService` region `rty.h`: a component is accepted if its package is the caller's (`Binder.getCallingUid`) or, with a flag off, equals the `android.intent.extra.PACKAGE_NAME` extra (now added to both Main starts, `ownCarApp`), then `niu.i` = the same launcher used by the player tap (`ntw.u`), Gemini open (`lsd.a`) and the Car App Library `startCarApp` (`mgb.startCarApp`). User test (07/11): with CarWebWide hidden in Android Auto's launcher the Main's button does not start it, so the visibility matters somewhere after `niu.k` (probably the template app processor): the single-icon trick does not work, and code cannot remove the Wide from the menu. The user keeps the buttons anyway ("sono comode") with both apps in the menu. The Wide opened from the Main must not hand over to the Main with the player option on: `EXTRA_FROM_MAIN` marker plus 5 s after the button (`noteMainOpensWide`). Not tested on a device; Gearhead's own lines about it are not in logcat (verbose level).
  - **Why Gemini's "apri CarWeb" always opens the Wide, and the only way around it (07/11/2026, user test: the Main opens, but Waze is closed and CarWebWide takes its place)**. In Android Auto 18.1 the assistant's open-app result goes to `GH.AssistIntentResultPr` (`lsd`/`lsc`, log "processOpenAppIntent, Other app"), which takes the first app of the package in the menu list, hidden apps excluded (`lsc.b`: `lzx.e(…, LAUNCHER_CUSTOMIZATION_ENABLED + COMPATIBLE)`, filter by package, `findFirst`). The navigation provider comes before the OEM one, so CarWebWide is always first: it takes the navigation space (Waze closed) before CarWeb runs any code, and closing it makes Android Auto reopen it. Nothing to change in the app. User-side way: hide CarWebWide in Android Auto's launcher settings; then "apri CarWeb" and the player tap open the Main directly and Waze stays (CarWebWide is no longer in the menu).
  - **"Ok Google, apri CarWeb", second version (07/11/2026, 18:10, user test: the Main showed for a moment, then the Wide came back)**. Android Auto's assistant log: `Intent Result Target Package: com.fcaronte.aabrowser`, `Intent Result Action: android.intent.action.MAIN` (Gemini's "device assistance" tool asks for the package, no component; Android Auto opens the first app of the package in the menu, the Wide). The first version brought the Main in front (`CAR.CAM makeForeground …CarService` at .165), closed the Wide (.343), and Android Auto recreated the Wide at once (.527) and sent the Main back (.468), as with the player card on 06/11. Now the Wide never closes: `MainHandOver.openMainSoon` only brings the Main in front (posted after the launch), the Wide stays in the map area. `HandOverScreen`, `opening_main`, `WideScreen.wasInUseBeforeLaunch` and `MainCarActivity.isInFrontSince` removed. The same test confirmed the diagnosis: `CarWebVoiceDebug` lines only from the in-app mic (`Main executes …`), none from `onPlayFromSearch` while Gemini refused every play request for CarWeb. Not tested on the device.
  - **Gemini, test results and what is left (07/11/2026, afternoon)**. With the media app option on (user test, phone log): the menu had CarWeb (media app) and CarWebWide, the CarWeb icon opened the Main through `CarServiceUnlisted` (`CAR.CAM New task … CarServiceUnlisted`), the player card followed the option, AA connected its player to the media app. But Gemini never sent the request: in a debug build (`AppLog` lines of `CarMediaService` present at the same minutes) no `onPlayFromSearch` line, and nothing from Android Auto's media search (`GH.MediaSearchIntentPr`). After each request Gemini only made Android Auto open an app: `WideSession onNewIntent action=null … AppKey=…CarServiceWide` at 13:49:46, 13:50:03, 13:55:20, 13:55:57, also when the user said CarWeb. The media apps on the phone declare the same as CarWeb (Fermata: OEM projection icon + NAVIGATION car app + MediaBrowserService + `MEDIA_PLAY_FROM_SEARCH`; Metrolist: MediaBrowserService + `MEDIA_PLAY_FROM_SEARCH`, its shortcuts are only search/library). Gemini in Android Auto works with partner apps (YouTube Music, Spotify; Amazon Music reported failing since Gemini replaced the Assistant). User decision: option removed (`car/CarMediaApp.kt`, `CarServiceUnlisted`, `MEDIA_TEMPLATES`, strings); `AppSettings.restoreMainAfterMediaAppTest` re-enables `CarService` once for whoever had it on (component states survive updates). Instead "Ok Google, apri CarWeb" follows "Il tocco sul player apre CarWeb": the Wide recognizes Gemini's launch (no action, `AppKey`, none of `ANDROID_AUTO_APP_LAUNCHER_INTENT`, `EXTRA_STARTED_BY_DASHBOARD`, `EXTRA_CAR_ACTIVITY_FLAGS`, own `EXTRA_FROM_WIDE`) and `MainHandOver` brings the Main in front; a Wide session created for it shows `HandOverScreen` (no WebView) and closes, a running Wide closes only if it was not in use (`WideScreen.wasInUseBeforeLaunch`: in front for more than 1 s, or covered less than 10 s ago); if Android Auto reopens the Wide within 5 s it stays. Note: after an app crash Android Auto recreates the Wide with the same extras. Diagnostics (user brief), debug builds only through `AppLog`, tag `CarWebVoiceDebug`: `onPlayFromSearch` (caller package, query, extras as "key: value"), `onPrepareFromSearch` and `onSearch` (not declared or not used, only to see if anything arrives), `fromAssistant` command, `dispatch` target/open screens/pending, Main and Wide executor with the plan. Pipeline test without Gemini: `adb shell am broadcast -n com.fcaronte.aabrowser/.debug.VoiceCommandDebugReceiver --es text "gabry ponte" --es target assistant` (the same `playFromSearch` on our session). Not tested on the device.
  - **Gemini in the car, CarWeb as an Android Auto media app (07/11/2026, user request: commands like "metti gabry ponte su youtube in carweb" from Gemini; user choice: the new media app must replace the Main's icon, keep the player tap per setting, and Gemini goes to the app chosen by the setting unless something plays in Wide or Main)**. Research: on the phone Gemini routes media only to Google partner apps (Music Assistant's notes; matches the user's old test "your app doesn't support Gemini commands"); AppFunctions (Android 16, `androidx.appfunctions` 1.0.0-rc01) reach Gemini only in a private preview/Early Access Program. In the car Google's docs say the Assistant guidance (`onPlayFromSearch`) applies to Gemini too, but Android Auto's `GH.MediaSearchIntentPr` (`mfn.e`) finds the app through the media items of its menu (`mhb.a`) and drops the request otherwise ("Cannot identify app to process search intent. Dropping intent"): our MediaBrowserService is never there because of the Wide. Also from the code: the Main's menu icon comes from the OEM provider (`GH.OEMAppProvider`, `CATEGORY_PROJECTION_OEM`, the log lists `com.fcaronte.aabrowser/.CarService` next to Fermata), while Android Auto starts projection apps from the `CATEGORY_PROJECTION` services allowed by its `GH.ServiceAuthorizer` (`jdl.aU` → `jof.c`), and the interceptors (`mfz` media, `mvt` parked apps, `nid`) do not block a component that is not in the menu. New option "CarWeb tra le app media di Android Auto" (`AppSettings.mediaAppInMenu`, prefs `media_app_in_menu`, off; switch phone only: turning it on disables the Main the car is showing): `CarMediaApp.applySetting` enables `CarServiceUnlisted` (the Main without the OEM category) and `CarServiceMediaApp` (MEDIA category, label "CarWeb", yesterday's tested component: inner CarAppService + MediaBrowser forward), then disables `CarService`. `MediaAppScreen`: from the menu icon it opens the Main; from the player card (`MEDIA_SHOW_PLAYBACK_VIEW`) the Wide if "Il tocco sul player apre CarWeb" is off or the Wide owns the player, else the Main. `CarMediaApp.mainComponent()` is what the Main uses to come back in front and what the session declares. Voice: `onPlayFromSearch` → `VoiceCommandRouter.fromAssistant` → `choose()` (with the player option on: owner, else the Main). Strings `media_app_menu_*`, `media_app_opening` (11 locales), README (EN + IT), `AABrowser.md` section "Gemini and Assistant in the Car". NOT TESTED: whether Android Auto starts `CarServiceUnlisted`, and whether Gemini then routes "metti … su CarWeb" to the app.
  - **User test of 07/11 (afternoon) and fixes**: the player card works with the Main (title, cover, the tap opens the app chosen by the option): the forward in `CarService` solved the empty card. Two new problems. (1) Every voice request for TV or Radio, and every name search in TV, Radio and Media, closed the app: `MediaMatcher.BRACKETS` had a lone `}` (`\{[^}]*}`), which Android's regex engine (ICU) rejects (`PatternSyntaxException` at class init, from `RadioApi.find` in `shouldInterceptRequest`) while the JVM of the unit tests accepts it; now `\{[^}]*\}` (and `\]`). Checked with the real ICU on the PC (`uregex_open` of the system libicu, small C program in the scratchpad): the old pattern fails exactly like on the phone (U_REGEX_RULE_SYNTAX at 28), the new one compiles, and the other 59 fixed patterns in the Kotlin sources compile too; the `VoiceCommands` rules are built from templates and already compiled on the device. (2) The 🎤 of the player and the double tap at the wheel always went to the Wide: `VoiceCommandRouter.choose()` takes the screen in front first, and the Wide counts as in front also when it only sits in the map area of the Android Auto home. With "Il tocco sul player apre CarWeb" on, it now picks first the app the player tap opens (the Main, the Wide while it owns the player), if open; option off = unchanged. README (EN + IT). Not tested on a device.
  - **Player card: the session declares who gets the tap, no third icon (07/11/2026, user test of the fourth version: the tap opens the Main, but the AA menu has a third icon; the user wants only CarWeb and CarWebWide)**. Read in the code of Android Auto 18.1.664404 (APK pulled from the phone, `dexdump -d`; the names below are obfuscated and change with each version, the log strings do not). Menu list (`GH.AvailableAppFinder`, `mae.j`): one provider per kind. The media one (`GH.MediaAppProvider`, `mcs.b`) gives the MediaBrowserServices, dropped when the package has a car app other than SETTINGS (so `CarMediaService` never has an icon), plus the MEDIA category car apps: every MEDIA car app is an icon, and `GH.MediaAppFinder` (`mhb`) reads the same list, so a MEDIA app hidden from the menu gets nothing. With no media app for the package, `mik.g` (`GH.MediaNowPlayingApp`) builds the session's app as `ComponentName(package, extras["android.media.session.BROWSE_SERVICE"] or "")` with a flag that keeps it on the controller connection (`mht.e`, "Returning connection from the active media controller"): that is why the player works without a media app. At the tap (`ntw.u`, `mgg.c`, `niu.k`) the interceptor `mfz.b`: empty class = first launcher app of the package ("Found multiple launcher apps for package %s, choosing first in list"): the Wide, or the Main with the Wide hidden (the user's test of 06/11); class given = that component if the launcher list has it (`mae.a`, any type, hidden ones too), otherwise "open on the phone" (`mfz.a`). So `CarMediaService.applyPlayerCardTarget()` writes `CarService` in that extra with the option on, unless the Wide owns the player (nothing written: the Wide as always), and it applies at once. Other uses of the class name in AA's media code checked: `mhj.a` (label of the component: `CarService` has the app name), `mga.k` (only MEDIA type items go to AA's own media activity), `mir` (session lookup of that activity, not reached). An AA version that does not read the extra opens the Wide, as without the option. User test of that version (07/11, log): only two icons, Wide playing = player fine and the tap opens the Wide, Main playing = the tap opens the Main but the player is empty. In the phone log the session really is `app=…/CarService` on the controller connection, but when the card shows AA also connects a MediaBrowser to that component (`MediaBrowserCompat: Connecting…` in Gearhead, then in our process `enforceInterface() expected 'com.google.android.gms.car.ICarProjection' but read 'android.service.media.IMediaBrowserService'`): my reading of the code had missed it (with an empty class the bind just fails). Same failure as the empty player of 06/11 on `CarServicePlayerCard`, which ended when that component answered as a MediaBrowser: `CarService.onBind` now dispatches by action and forwards `android.media.browse.MediaBrowserService` to the real `CarMediaService` (`CarActivityService.onBind` is not final; local transact, so `Binder.getCallingUid()` stays Android Auto's). Empty player fixed by this is inferred from that earlier case, not tested yet. Removed: `car/PlayerCard.kt` (`CarServicePlayerCard`, `PassageScreen`), `MEDIA_TEMPLATES`, the `MainCarActivity` and `MediaFocusManager` helpers, `carHostValidator`, `player_card_opening_main`, "from the next connection" in the description (11 locales). NOT YET TESTED on a device. If the tap still opens the Wide: the log line `CarMediaService: Player card tap goes to:` and the AA lines with `GH.TargetComponent` and `Found multiple launcher apps`. Fallback, the user's idea: make the "CarWeb" icon itself the media category app, which needs a way to take `CarService` out of the menu while it stays startable (not checked).

(1) Forward from the Wide: the Main opens, but the Wide stays in the navigation space, side by side, in place of the open navigator. (2) `CarServicePlayerCard` of category MEDIA: the tap reaches it (`GH.TargetComponent=…CarServicePlayerCard`, action `MEDIA_SHOW_PLAYBACK_VIEW`), the Main opens, no extra icon, but the AA player stays empty. (3) Category IOT: the tap goes back to the Wide (`GH.TargetComponent=…CarServiceWide`), closing the Wide is useless (`Android Auto reopened the Wide`: a new session with action null 200 ms later), and it adds a menu icon. So only MEDIA gets the tap. Why (2) was empty: with a MEDIA car app in the package Gearhead uses that component as the media app and connects its MediaBrowser to it (`GH.MediaServiceConnLDP … app=…CarServicePlayerCard`), while `CarAppService.onBind` (final) always returns the car app binder, so the handshake never ends: Spotify's `AndroidAutoService`, same kind of component, shows `connectionStatus=CONNECTED`, ours never did. Now, NOT YET TESTED: MEDIA again, and `CarServicePlayerCard` is a plain `Service` dispatching by action: the car app action to an inner `CarAppService` (`PassageApp`, attached to this context), `android.media.browse.MediaBrowserService` to a forwarding `Binder` over the real `CarMediaService`, bound with `bindService` (local transact: same thread, so `Binder.getCallingUid()` stays Android Auto and `isValidPackage` passes). Wide fallback removed: `WideSession` is plain again. If the player is still empty, read the `GH.MediaServiceConnLDP` line `Fetching new connection … connectionStatus=` for `CarServicePlayerCard` and the `CarMediaService onGetRoot` line.

The tag `2.5.1` is at `215ff01`, before the fix `7b0bd06` (commit 27 minutes after publishing): installed 2.5.1 and older keep the old code and need one manual install. User correction: KingInstaller on `.MainActivity` with the file loaded was RIGHT (`.ApkHandler`, what a generic intent resolves to, goes straight to Android's standard install, without KingInstaller's choice): my change in `cd61c8d` was wrong and is reverted. The real failure is the message before it, in the old code: it checked the expected path in the public Downloads. Measured with the app's uid (`run-as`): an APK copied there by another app can be listed but reading gives `Permission denied` (a file DownloadManager wrote for the app was not measured). Probable cause: a leftover with the same name (the user keeps copies there) that the app cannot delete, so DownloadManager renames the new one and the check looks at the old, unreadable one: `getPackageArchiveInfo` finds nothing, "not valid". The check itself is right: a probe with the phone's real `PackageManager.getPackageArchiveInfo` (Android 17, temporary dex run from adb and removed) reads package, version and signer of the APKs in Downloads (`8629c57d…`, same as the installed one). The stale `pending_apk_path` in the phone's prefs shows the last attempt there used the old code; the new code had never run on it. Now the APK is downloaded into the app's own external folder (`getExternalFilesDir(DOWNLOADS)`, no permission issue, real file name, the old ones deleted there), copied to `cacheDir/update/` and checked; the app no longer touches the public Downloads (it could delete the user's copies). Installed APK and release have the same certificate (`8629c57d…`, read with apksigner). Other fixes (`cd61c8d`): "Download" of the update notification went to `UpdateDownloadReceiver`, which ignores it (wrong since the audit split, should be `UpdateStartReceiver`); with the app behind, Android silently refuses to open KingInstaller: a "ready" notification opens it; copy and check off the main thread (`goAsync`); messages by reason (`update_invalid_apk`, `update_not_newer`, `update_unreadable`) and reasons in `Log` also in release (`Update: …`). Not tested on the device: to try it, debug build + "Update test" on.

Keeps only the track on air (labels in all languages: strong ones also without colon, weak ones like "now", "next", "dopo" only with a colon, so "Now That We Found Love" is safe); formats `Titolo~Artista~Album~Anno~ISRC~Durata~…` (order differs: mixed case long form = title first, uppercase or short form = artist first), `Song*TITOLO*ARTISTA*…`, iHeart `Artista - text="Titolo" song_spot="M"` (T = ads and shows), `Artista: X Titolo: Y`; drops station name, addresses, years, version parts, brackets, feat.; not songs: ads, phone numbers, placeholders, "*** programma", station slogan. `lyrics.js` `findRecord`: exact signature (file tags with album and duration, then clean artist + title), then free search (artist + title, then title) accepted only if `matches()` says it is that track in either order ("Radio Gaga - Queen"); a single phrase ("vasco rossi albachiara") only if a track covers all its words. Live check on real titles: 21 of 30 found, the misses are instrumentals or very rare tracks (LRCLIB rate-limited an aggressive run, so at most 3 requests per title). The `startsWith("AABrowser") || startsWith("CarWeb")` change only cleans older APKs in Downloads and does not validate or hand off the newly downloaded APK. Likely regression point is the earlier security hardening commit `64db9d3` (included in the 2.5 code): it made package + signing-certificate + newer-version checks mandatory and removed the fallback to DownloadManager's content URI when the public Downloads file / FileProvider path could not be used. The exact rejecting branch is unknown because no attempt log was collected. Current working-tree fix: use `DownloadManager.getUriForDownloadedFile()` via `ContentResolver`, copy into app-private cache, then run all existing checks and give KingInstaller the validated cache file via FileProvider; no checks are weakened. Build passed; on-device updater retest still required.

  - **Audio/video out of sync with wireless Android Auto (user question, 03/11/2026, nothing done yet)**: Android Auto sends the screen (H.264, low latency for touch) and the media audio (separate channel, bigger buffers, more with wireless and adapters: a Carlinkit FAQ speaks of 1-2 s on track changes) separately, so on the car the sound usually comes after the picture. The app cannot change Android Auto's buffers, and data compression does not help (it is buffering, not bandwidth). Options given to the user: delay the audio inside the page with Web Audio (`createMediaElementSource` + `DelayNode`; easy, but it only helps if the sound comes first); delay the picture: Wide only, a ring of frames between the VirtualDisplay and the Android Auto surface while a fullscreen video plays (heavy: GPU memory, about 15 frames for 0.5 s at 30 fps; touches would lag, so only during video), not possible for the Main (Android Auto projects its window directly; a page canvas copy of the video would be fragile and fails on DRM). Both would be a user setting in ms (each car and adapter has its own delay). Waiting for the user: which comes first (lips or sound), roughly how much, Main or Wide, wired vs wireless.

Other apps are also called "AABrowser": the user is looking for a short new name, one for the Main and one for the Wide, clearly a browser (suggested: DashWeb / DashWeb Wide, CarWeb / CarWeb Wide, AAB Web / AAB Wide next to CarWeb Media; availability not checked).
  - **Fullscreen restore, second version (`355725a`)**: user test of `eac7dfb`: back from the background the app looked frozen "for minutes", then went fullscreen; background and back once more unblocked it at once. Not seen on the device; likely the tap came 400 ms after ON_RESUME while the WebView was still becoming visible, so Chromium kept the page hidden (timers down to one wake-up per minute) and the layer took every tap. New `ui/FullscreenRestore.kt`: waits for the window focus, then a page frame (`WebViewCompat.postVisualStateCallback`, delivered only with the WebView visible; 500 ms delay without the feature), arms the layer, waits for the frame with the layer, checks it is still there (`getFullscreenRestoreArmedScript`: the tap must never reach the page) and taps; no fullscreen within 1.5 s = the app removes the layer (`getCancelFullscreenRestoreScript`); the page's own timeout is now 10 s. Layer listeners passive except `touchend`. The fullscreen `AndroidView` is wrapped in `key(view)`: a new fullscreen view arriving before the old one left the composition stayed detached (AwContents detached = page stopped). Log `Fullscreen restore: <step> (+N ms)` (debug builds). Headless Chrome re-test OK.
  - **Fullscreen video back after the background (`eac7dfb`, user report of 02/11/2026)**: the Main leaves fullscreen on ON_PAUSE (playback stopped in the background in fullscreen) and on ON_RESUME called `document.querySelector('video')?.requestFullscreen()` from `evaluateJavascript`: Chromium allows it only with a user activation, so it always failed silently (`.catch`). Now `BrowserJavascript.getFullscreenMemoryScript()` (document start, all origins) keeps `window.__aabFullscreenTarget` = the last fullscreen element (YouTube: the player container); on resume, only in the visible tab, `getRestoreFullscreenScript()` arms a transparent layer over the page ("armed" / "already" / "no-target") and the Main sends one tap to the WebView center after 400 ms (`MotionEvent` DOWN/UP, `SOURCE_TOUCHSCREEN`: real input for Chromium); the layer's `touchend` calls `requestFullscreen` on the remembered element (else the first `<video>`), `preventDefault` keeps the tap and the click away from the page, the layer removes itself after 2 s. The synthetic tap does not update `lastTouchAt` (popups/new tabs) nor the interaction tracking (`syntheticTap`). Headless Chrome 156 (scripts from the Kotlin source, CDP touch): without a gesture "Permissions check failed"; restore + one touch = player fullscreen, no touch/click reached the page, layer gone after 2 s with no touch. Debug log `Fullscreen restore: "armed"`. The Wide does not leave fullscreen when another app is in front (its surface stays), so it is unchanged.
  - **Passenger mode and Passenger keyboard (`46e55b0`, docs `083645d`, user request of 02/11/2026; user choices: page covered with a message that explains how to unlock it from Settings, keyboard with a text line + Enter, no lock when Android Auto sends no status)**:
    - `utils/DrivingState.kt`: driving status from Android Auto only (no checks of our own, user decision): AA SDK sensor `DRIVING_STATUS` (11; 0 = unrestricted = parked, any restriction = moving), the same one that locks the AA keyboard. One connection for the whole app (`android.support.car.Car.createCar` -> `CarServiceLoaderGms` -> Gearhead `DynamicApiFactory.newCarApiConnection`), acquired by `MainCarActivity` and the Wide (`acquire`/`release`, ref-counted), on its own `HandlerThread` with an uncaught exception handler (the SDK callbacks catch nothing: an error stops only that thread, state unknown). Second source: the Main's host (`a("sensor")` = GMS `CarSensorManager`, `byteValues[0]`); `onHostStopped()` forgets its status when the app connection never reported. `moving` (Compose State, null = unknown), `isLocked` = moving and not `AppSettings.passengerMode`, listeners for the Wide (also on a settings change, `notifyChanged`). Log tag `DrivingState` with `Log.d` (release builds too): `Car connected, driving status listener=`, `Host driving status listener=`, `Driving state: moving|parked|unknown (car|host status=N)`, `... not available: <exception>`.
    - Lock: Main `ui/DriveLockCover.kt` in `MainScreen` (`isAutomotive`, zIndex 97: the voice listening popup and the messages stay above); Wide `driveLockReference` over page and sidebar (voice card above, kept above a fullscreen video), message centered in the stable area; taps, scroll, fling, zoom and the input popup are blocked. Pages keep running underneath (audio, AA player, voice commands).

Main: ⌨️ of the input popup on page fields (capture script + `getSetInputTextScript`, popup suppressed 1.5 s and while the panel is open), `SearchOverlay` (keys under the search card), `EditFavoriteScreen` (panel per field). Wide: ⌨️ of the input popup (`openFieldInput`) and the lens (`openSearch`; under the lock it falls back to the AA SearchTemplate). Non-Latin languages type by voice.
    - Settings (app section, after the weather): "Passenger mode" and "Passenger keyboard", both off; strings `passenger_mode_*`, `passenger_keyboard_*`, `drive_lock_*` (11 locales, the descriptions say the driver must not use them). README (EN + IT) bullet and Widescreen limit, `AABrowser.md` "Passenger Mode (2.5)". JVM test `PassengerKeyboardTest`.
  - **User report of 01/11/2026**: location on websites works now (user: "ora la posizione funziona"; setup not stated). New report: the Wide shows the AdBlock shield at the top left instead of the top right. The shield is the only action of the NavigationTemplate's top `ActionStrip` (search and mic on its left without the fixed sidebar), and Android Auto draws that strip: the app gives the actions, never the corner. `onGetTemplate()` last changed in `3333b63` (23/10, inside 2.4: `git diff 1e3d008 HEAD` has no strip change) and nothing after 2.4 touches the template (`WideSitePermissionScreen` / `WideJsDialogScreen` are pushed MessageTemplates without the shield), so the move comes from the host side: test setup (DHU on the emulator vs the car), an Android Auto update, a flipped Android Auto layout, or another app navigating. Resolved (user, 02/11/2026): the open-source head unit emulator on the phone draws the strips mirrored (right-hand drive keeps the menus on the right and vice versa), Waze shows the same; not the app. The Wide's `Log.d` lines (release builds too) `CALLBACK onVisibleAreaChanged` / `onStableAreaChanged` / `layout raw=… reserveL=… reserveR=…` show where the host keeps its buttons (reserveL > 0 = on the left). Also unclear: "fa installazione standard ma senza post fix", sent with an emulator logcat where KingInstaller installs `app.morphe.manager` (likely another project). Debug builds are signed with the release key when `signing.properties` exists, so the "Update test" can hand the 2.4 release to KingInstaller: if the Wide home title says v2.4, the 31/10 fixes are not on that device (and 2.4 grants the location without asking, `web_location` on by default).

Still wrong: Google Maps never asks for the location, also when tapping its location button (where the user tested was not said; phone not on adb, AABrowser not on the emulator, so no log). Checked in headless Chrome with the WebView user agent (Android 17, `wv`, mobile metrics; probe in the scratchpad): Maps mobile web shows "Aprire l'app Google Maps?" (tap "Rimani nella versione web"), `permissions.query({name:'geolocation'})` = "prompt", and a touch on "Riposiziona la mappa in base a dove ti trovi" calls `getCurrentPosition({enableHighAccuracy:false, timeout:180000})` (a synthetic mouse click did not); at load (~2 s) Maps navigates to `intent://www.google.com/maps?…utm_campaign=ml-ardi-wv…;package=com.google.android.apps.maps;S.browser_fallback_url=…`, which the Main turns into one reload of the fallback. WebView source: `AwBrowserContext.getGeolocationPermission` (ASK without a retained decision) exists since M131 at least (branch-heads 6778, 6943, 7103, 7204 checked). So the request reaches the app. Silent paths left in the app: in the car without "Allow all the time" (or precise) the request is refused with a message, and the Main showed it as a `Toast`, which appears on the phone and not on the car screen: fixed in `9c39429` (`BrowserScreen(onShowFeedback)` -> MainScreen `feedbackMessage`; messages longer than 60 characters stay 6 s). Also silent by design: the "grant without asking" switch on, or a saved "Non consentire" for google.com (Settings list).
  - **User tests of 31/10/2026, second round, and fixes**: OK: WhatsApp Web and Gemini write in the Wide (keyboard and dictation), Google sign-in works again (DAZN). Fixed:
    - Wide keyboard "typing blind" (`fd108ff`): `getCaptureInputTargetScript` returned `innerText` = "\n" for an empty rich editor (Lexical, Quill), so the full-screen AA keyboard started on an empty first line and the typed text went on the second one, out of sight (it still reached the page). The prefill is now one line: leading/trailing line breaks dropped, inner ones become a space.
    - Main typing into a field that lost focus (`32a6d3b`; WhatsApp Web in the Main wrote nothing, car keyboard and dictation): `getInjectTextScript` finds the field like the Wide (`activeElement`, else `__aabTypingTarget` / `__savedInputElement`; `getAutoSaveActiveElementScript` is now registered in the Main tabs too), gives it the focus back (`preventScroll`), and only if the caret left the field puts it at the end and dispatches `selectionchange`. It returns how it wrote: debug log `Voice input written:` (Main dictation) and `Car keyboard text written:` (`CarInputConnection`, only when not a plain `"exec"`). Headless Chrome (harness rebuilt in the scratchpad, same approach as before): focused, blurred by the page, caret in the middle then blur, selection removed, unfocused page; Lexical, Quill, plain/email/textarea. Before the fix the blurred field got nothing. The real cause on the device was not seen: if WhatsApp still writes nothing, the log tells `no-target` from `refocused:exec`.
    - Location (`0be4520`): Chromium WebView source checked (main, `AwBrowserContext.getGeolocationPermission`, `AwContents.onGeolocationPermissionsShowPrompt`): with no retained decision `navigator.permissions.query` gives ASK ("prompt") and every request reaches the app's `onGeolocationPermissionsShowPrompt` unless the WebView setting is off (the app never turns it off) or a decision was retained (the app never retains). So the fixes are on the app side. Phone Main: when the phone permission is missing, Android asks and the site's request waits for the answer (`SitePermissionPrompts.onPhonePermissionMissing` returns true = waiting; `MainActivity.onRequestPermissionsResult` -> `SitePermissions.onPhonePermissionsAnswered()` bumps `phonePermissionAnswers`; `BrowserScreen` `LaunchedEffect` -> `sitePrompts.onPhonePermissionsAnswered()` re-runs the waiting requests); before, the site got a no and needed a second tap. `SitePermissionPrompts(context, inCar, …)` (Wide true, Main `activity == null`): `isAvailable(LOCATION)` = precise or approximate (`WebLocation.hasLocation`) plus "Allow all the time" in the car; `isComplete` = precise (plus always in the car) decides when to ask (phone) or warn (car). A site allowed in the car without "Allow all the time" used to get no position at all. Settings row "Phone location for websites" removed (user: it looked like granting the location to every site) with `site_permissions_location_phone_*` and `site_permissions_grant` (11 locales); `web_location_phone_toast` (shown only in the car now) says to set precise + "Allow all the time" on the phone (11 locales). Debug log: `Location: allowed=… (web=…), phone: precise=…, any=…, always=…, car=…` or `Location: waiting for the phone permission`.

The Wide loads one page at a time in a single WebView; logins stay shared (one WebView profile). Main `onRenderProcessGone` (renderer shared by all tabs, also killed by Android for memory): releases the player and pending prompts, removes and destroys the dead WebView and bumps `webViewGeneration` (`key()` around the AndroidView, the factory reloads the tab's `url`); a second crash within `RENDER_CRASH_RETRY_MS` (5 s) leaves the tab empty.

CI `build.yml`: a release build without `KEYSTORE_BASE64`/`KEYSTORE_FILE` fails.
