  - **Security audit of October 2026 (report "AABrowser — Audit di sicurezza, bug e affidabilità", Claude Docs https://claude.ai/artifact/JgbrMGUmKC3NExt17tifhw, base `dca3f54` = 2.4) reviewed with the user (30/10/2026)**. Verdicts checked in the code: S1, S2, P1, P2, P3, S5 (in part), S6, S7, S9, B1, B3, B5, B6, C1, P4, S13 real; S4 already fixed (`3444da9`); B2 mostly by design (the active Main tab must take the player from behind for the next track; `isPlaybackStart = isPlaying` only on real status changes); S11 matters only with S1 (closed); S12 and P5 are features (home sites with resources on other hosts; log sharing); S3 only in a light version; R8 stays off (reflection on Android Auto internals). User decisions: per-site permission prompts with a compact expandable per-site list in Settings, an "all site permissions" item in Clear data, the `web_location` auto-grant switch removed, the camera grant removed; the "Wide:" tab of the Main was made when the player focus between the two apps could not be handled (now `MediaFocusManager` does it) and WhatsApp Web really shows "Usa qui" (B1 confirmed); cookies out of the backup if it is a real risk; `libs/v2`, `libs/v5` removable if unused (checked: older copies of the AA SDK, v5 included with exactly its three AARs excluded, v2 not referenced, `libs/lib` only empty ABI folders; the app uses `libs/v4/aauto.aar`).
  - **Audit Block 1 (S1, S2, S9)**: `859739d` intent:// links (Main and Wide) are followed only for main-frame requests and only to http(s) fallbacks (`UrlUtils.isWebUrl`): an iframe with `browser_fallback_url=javascript:` made the app run that code in the main page; the parse error log no longer carries the URI. `0085d3b` `getInjectTextScript` takes the text as a JSON string (`JSONObject.quote`, like `getSetInputTextScript`): with only the apostrophe escaped inside double-quoted strings, quotes, backslashes and new lines broke the insertion and a crafted text ran code; `injectText` removed from both bridges, `MainCarActivity` (voice result) and `CarInputConnection` (single car keyboard characters) evaluate the script directly, still only in WebViews with the bridge (not the login popup). Tested in Node with mocks (quotes, backslash, new line, U+2028, injection attempt inserted literally, no code run). `ad52051` the Wide home commands (`about:toggle_*`, update banner links) are accepted only from the home page in the main frame: any page could toggle a setting and close the Wide.
  - **DAZN removed from the default favorites and the README (`030c607`, `87ce971`, user decision after the DAZN known limit)**: default id "9" removed from `getDefaultFavorites()` with its `favorite_default_name_9` / `favorite_default_url_9` strings (10 files, `values-it` never had them). New installs, "reset to defaults" and "add missing defaults" no longer get it; lists that already have it keep it (no migration: user data). README (EN + IT): "DRM (Widevine L3)" left the features for a "Protected content (DRM)" bullet in Disclaimer & Compatibility (the WebView offers only L3, sites that need L1 cannot work, L3 content such as Spotify plays); DAZN left the voice site search list. Still in the code: the DAZN site search (`VoiceCommands` `KnownSite`, Wide `SiteSearch`), the login proxy, the DAZN CORS preflight and the click interceptor: kept on purpose (user, 26/10/2026: without them DAZN did not work even for its unprotected content).
  - **Wide update banner (`fa1aa09`, user report: only the Main's dashboard showed it)**: `WideScreen.checkForUpdate()` (at creation, `lifecycleScope`) calls `UpdateManager.checkForUpdates`; with a newer release it stores `updateInfo`, shows the phone notification like the Main, clears the cached home and reloads it if the home is shown. `HomePage.build(…, updateText)` adds a banner at the top (`about:update_download` → `UpdateManager.startDownload` + CarToast with the result, `about:update_dismiss` → `UpdateManager.isBannerDismissed`, shared with the Main). `startDownload` now returns Boolean. The Wide home's "no favorites" text is `wide_no_favorites` (11 locales).
  - **DAZN protected content (lives, protected clips): known limit, not fixable in the app** (24-25/10/2026, six debug logs). Clear highlights play. Protected content: the licence is accepted ("Key successfully added") but contains no content key (`keystatuseschange` lists none), so decryption fails with "requested key has not been loaded" and the player stops with COR-25000. The WebView DRM runs at L3 ("Create MediaDrmBridge with level 1" = SW_SECURE_CRYPTO); every L1 request (video HW_SECURE_ALL) is refused by the WebView (NotSupportedError), and L1 output would likely be black on the Android Auto screen anyway. Capping every format above 576p (decodingInfo) changed nothing. Tried and reverted: AdBlock exception (`aa9a588`/`79eedfd`), robustness rewrites (`917d83d`, `012e8af` / `60cbcb2`), L1 for DAZN (`5c45cdc`/`ba63dfb`), SD cap + key log (`64fe62d`, `f1a010c` / `d6c742b`). Kept: the `AUTH_PROXY_JS` fetch wrapper fix (`05bea8e`, it consumed Request bodies: "Cannot construct a Request with a Request object that has already been used") and the `AABrowser DRM:` console lines of `DRM_L3_ENFORCER_JS`. Do not retry without new facts (e.g. DAZN changing its licence policy, or a WebView that grants HW_SECURE_ALL).
  - **Security: fake CORS preflight only for DAZN (`3444da9`)**: the Main answered every OPTIONS request of every site with a fake 200 allowing any origin with credentials, so any page could send credentialed non-simple requests to other sites and LAN devices. Now Main and Wide answer only when the target or the Origin is a DAZN host (`DaznProxy.isDaznHost`; the Wide's "contains dazn.com" also matched notdazn.com).
  - **Release 2.4 preparation**: `versionCode 15` / `versionName "2.4"`; README main features updated (AdBlock per-site shield, YouTube quality, location for websites, log sharing, weather only in the car, "new in 2.3" labels dropped); final bilingual release notes given in the chat (Widescreen no longer Beta).
  - **User tests after `6465ef3`**: single YouTube videos have ±10 s again, playlists skip; voice commands work with the app behind (player commands stay in the background, "metti …" brings the app to the front: OK for the user). The weather's 🎤 always brings the **Wide** to the front: not an Android Auto limit but `VoiceCommandRouter.choose()` (foreground screen, then the AA player owner, then Wide before Main); with the weather there is no owner. Choosing the last used screen instead was offered, not done: the Main's bring-to-front (AA SDK `startCarActivity`) is still unverified, while the Wide's works. README and release notes now say that commands work with the app behind.
  - **Voice command with the screen behind (`b93a5c7`, user report: from the player's 🎤 the mic listened but the song started only after opening the app)**: the Main is Compose and `MainCarActivity` moves its lifecycle to ON_STOP when another Android Auto app is in front, which pauses the composition's frame clock (`WindowRecomposer`): `TabManager` changes and `LaunchedEffect(url)` → `loadUrl` wait for the next start. Also Chromium does not start media in a hidden page that never played (`RenderFrameImpl::DeferMediaLoad`). Now a command other than the player ones brings its screen to the front: Main → `MainScreen(bringToFront)` from `MainCarActivity` = AA SDK `startCarActivity(Intent(component = CarService))` (the SDK forwards it to Gearhead's `CarUiEntry.startCarActivity`, reflection); Wide → `carContext.startCarApp(Intent(component = CarServiceWide))` (documented "start this app" intent); the check is lifecycle not STARTED (Main) / `!isWideResumed` (Wide). The phone Main passes no callback. The "In ascolto…" overlay of the AA player is now set by `VoiceCommandInput.setListening()` (the `LaunchedEffect` that forwarded it did not run with the Main behind). Not tested: whether Gearhead still honors the SDK call.
  - **Widescreen no longer "Beta" (`3b2d71b`, user decision)**: its limits (full-screen AA keyboard only, no double-tap zoom, no drags) are set by Android Auto for Car App Library apps. README, `AABrowser.md` (section title, "Android Auto Limitations", changelog rule) and the release notes ("Limiti imposti da Android Auto"). The YouTube AdBlock description still says Beta (different feature).
  - **🎤 on the Android Auto player (`77ad629`, narrowed in `bbf0001`)**: the wheel keys reach the app only after it played something, but taps on the player's own buttons always reach the session Android Auto shows. A custom action `com.fcaronte.aabrowser.VOICE_COMMAND` (icon `res/drawable/ic_voice_command.xml`, Material mic; label `voice_command_button`) opens the mic through `MediaSessionCallback.onCustomAction` → `VoiceCommandRouter.startListening()` (else `showVoiceCommandNeedsApp()`). User test: visible and working, but on every state it took the free slots where Android Auto shows its own ±10 s seek buttons for players without skip actions (single YouTube videos lost ±10 s). Now it is only in `weatherPlaybackState()` (the weather exists only in the car); page states have no custom actions and `publishPlaybackState()` copies the state again.
  - **Wide "portami a …" (`ab9f2f0`, user request)**: loads the Google Maps directions page in the Wide (like the Main) and still calls `startCarApp(ACTION_NAVIGATE)`, now with `+` for spaces (`navigationGeoUri`, as in the startCarApp docs). The likely reason it did nothing before: the Wide is a NAVIGATION category app, so Android Auto may hand the request back to the Wide itself; `WideSession.onNewIntent` logs `Navigation request came back to the Wide` in that case (debug builds). If Android Auto gives it to Maps/Waze, the stock navigation takes the map area. `voice_command_navigate_failed` removed (11 locales). Still not in the hints/README/notes until tested.
  - **User tests after `a7bbc71`**: Wide fullscreen video during a phone call: OK (resolved). Player card tap: Maps keeps navigating, the Wide still comes to the front (known Android Auto behavior, in the notes). "Portami a …": does not work in the Wide (`CarContext.startCarApp(ACTION_NAVIGATE, geo:0,0?q=…)`; the Wide is a NAVIGATION category app, and the query is `%20`-encoded while the docs show `+`: cause not verified) and in the Main Google Maps on the web only shows the route, so the user asked to drop it from the suggestions: hints (`64a333f`, 11 locales: replaced by «cerca … su Amazon»), README (`3a9e059`) and release notes. The command itself is unchanged (Main: route in a new tab; Wide: tries Android Auto navigation).
  - **Wheel hint with its condition (`64a333f`)**: `voice_command_hint_wheel` now says the double tap works after something has played in AABrowser (11 locales).
  - **Hungarian site search (`ef04fbe`)**: the patterns used only the article "a"; before a vowel Hungarian uses "az" ("az amazon oldalon"), so Amazon/eBay never matched. "az" variants added; tests for both articles and for the hint examples in the other languages.
  - **Weather and its notification only in the car (`bc6c7ba`, user report: weather always visible on the phone)**: `CarMediaService` runs on the phone too (every Main tab binds it; the system also binds it for media resumption) and never checked for the car: the weather card and its foreground notification stayed on the phone. Weather mode now needs `isInCar()` (`CarConnection` type, `wasConnectedToCar == true`); the observer handles null→connected (service created in the car) and connected→disconnected (`onCarDisconnected()`: cancels the weather job, ends the "Listening…" overlay, clears the player unless a page is still playing, removes the notification). `updateNotification()` posts only with a state other than NONE/STOPPED and otherwise calls `removeNotification()` (`stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)` + `cancel()`: `cancel()` alone does not remove a foreground service notification, and the weather-off branch used `STOP_FOREGROUND_DETACH`). `onStartCommand` keeps the `startForegroundService` contract (placeholder notification, removed at once when there is nothing to show; `CarMediaNotificationManager.getPlaceholderNotification()`). `onGetRoot` returns null for `EXTRA_RECENT` (system media resumption) except for Android Auto. ▶ on the weather and the weather fetch results also require the car; turning the weather off in Settings clears it at once. The car radio changing station with the wheel keys is the head unit's own source: unrelated, nothing to do.
  - **Mic hint with the double tap (`5fc40ca`, user request)**: when the Android Auto player is enabled (`AppSettings.mediaPlayerEnabled`), the Main listening popup and the Wide listening card add `voice_command_hint_wheel` (11 locales) under the examples.
  - **Weather texts translated (`0e199ee`)**: `getWeatherDescription(context, code, isDay)` and the loading/refresh titles use `weather_*` strings (11 locales) instead of hard-coded Italian; ▶ recognizes the refresh title by `R.string.weather_updating`.
  - **README voice commands (`6c3d126`)**: "Voice commands" / "Comandi vocali" sections (mic locations, double tap and its conditions, command table, Gemini limit). Translation check: all 234 keys present in the 11 locales; new 2.4 strings translated (only "YouTube: %1$s" labels are equal to English, nothing to translate).
  - **Release notes 2.4**: drafted (IT then EN, Wide Beta with limits, donation links), given to the user in the chat; not stored in the repo.
  - **Weather card: ⏮ ⏭ tried and removed (`15fcf16`, reverted in `dfee234`)**: `15fcf16` gave every weather state ⏮ ⏭ so the double tap could reach the app with nothing playing, assuming Android Auto sends the steering wheel keys to the app of its media card. User test: wrong. With the AAB Media card visible and selected the wheel keys did nothing on the weather, and the double tap started working only after something had played in AABrowser. Android routes media keys to the session of the app that last played audio (media button session, `MediaSessionStack`, based on audio playback), not to the card Android Auto shows; the weather plays no audio on purpose (a silent sound to grab the keys could make the car switch its source away from the radio: not done). Now every weather state (loading, loaded, refreshed) is paused with play/pause only (`WEATHER_ACTIONS`, ▶ refreshes). `onMediaButtonEvent` does not depend on the declared actions, so when AABrowser played last the double tap still works with the weather shown. Kept from `15fcf16`: `showingWeather = true` on entering weather mode and ▶ recognized by `showingWeather`.
  - **"Listening…" on the Android Auto player (`media: "Listening…"…`, user request)**: the double tap on ⏮ also works with the app behind another one, where the listening card is not visible. `CarMediaService.showVoiceListening(listening, text)` (Main: `LaunchedEffect` on `VoiceCommandInput.isListening/partialText`; Wide: `showVoiceCard`/`hideVoiceCard` + partial results) sets the player's metadata to "🎤 In ascolto…" with the text understood so far (or "Comando vocale") below; metadata arriving meanwhile from pages or weather goes through `setSessionMetadata()` and is kept aside (`metadataUnderVoice`) until the end. Only while connected to a car (`wasConnectedToCar`).
  - **Review of the commits made with Gemini (`f9a5655`..`51f1bc1`)**: accepted as they are: dashboard mic (`eee3b4e`), radial menu rings (`270efdf`: inner 5 at 125 dp, outer 7 at 200 dp, no overlap), fixed tab width (`deb573c`). Fixed: log sharing (`02a52a3`), unmute regression (`862f023`), playlist scroll script scope (`59c74ad`), double tap on ⏮ (`9458024`); see the entries below.
  - **Wide, YouTube video page: "columns first" scrolling (`e341093`, reverted in `c2ffdc6`, reapplied in `60a0a85`)**: Android Auto gives no finger position for drags (`SurfaceCallback.onScroll(dx, dy)` / `onFling(vx, vy)` in Car App Library 1.7, checked in the AAR; only `onClick` and `onScale` have coordinates), so the dragged side cannot be known. On YouTube `/watch` pages the page stays still (video up) while the columns scroll; the user first missed scrolling the left side, then re-checked that there is nothing to see there and asked to keep it. Elsewhere page and columns scroll together.
  - **Wide fullscreen video inside the free area (`f21a8ec`, user report: during a phone call the fullscreen YouTube video did not resize)**: `showCustomView` laid the video over the whole surface (also under the host UI) until the next visible area change, and the vertical insets were never applied (the page ignores them on purpose). `layoutCustomView()` gives it the page's side margins plus the visible area's top/bottom margins, when it opens and at every area change, and logs `Fullscreen video margins l= t= r= b=` (`Log.d`, also release). Not verified with a call: if the video still stays under the call UI, the shared log tells whether Android Auto sent `CALLBACK onVisibleAreaChanged` at all (an overlay that does not change the area cannot be avoided).
  - **YouTube early ad data removal off by default (`9a71707`)**: the user confirmed the black video for the ad's length with it on; the DOM skip (16x, mute, Skip, jump to the end) gets past those ads in seconds. A value already saved is kept.
  - **"Ok Google / Gemini, metti … su AABrowser / AAB Media" (user tests)**: with either name Gemini answers "I'm asking your app to play…" and then "your app doesn't support Gemini commands"; with "AAB Media" it once offered YouTube Music instead. In 2026 Gemini handles media only for apps with a Gemini extension (YouTube Music, Spotify; reports of Gemini itself saying Amazon Music needs one), not through the standard `onPlayFromSearch` path the old Assistant used, which the app implements (`ACTION_PLAY_FROM_SEARCH` on every state). Nothing to fix on our side until Google opens it; the steering wheel way is the double tap on ⏮ (own mic). In Android Auto the media app's name is `service_name` = "AAB Media".
  - **Tab bar fixed size (`deb573c`)**: changed `TabItem` width in `PersistentTabBar.kt` from `widthIn(max = 200.dp)` to fixed `width(160.dp)` with `weight(1f)` text for consistent tab bar sizing across tabs.
  - **Main dashboard voice command button (`eee3b4e`)**: added microphone button in `DashboardScreen` top bar next to search, wired to `toggleVoiceCommand`.
  - **YouTube audio when a video starts muted (`1ca0688`, narrowed in `862f023`)**: YouTube starts the opened video muted without a user tap (refresh, voice command "metti …"). `1ca0688` unmuted any muted playing video at every check: also the home feed previews (`getMainVideo` falls back to the first `<video>` where there is no `#movie_player`), which then took the AA player, and a user mute was undone every 500 ms. Now `checkAds()` looks once per video (`video.dataset.aabStartAudioKey` = `videoKey`) on `/watch` pages only: a video that started muted in its first 5 s gets `player.unMute()` + `video.muted = false`.
  - **Double tap on ⏮ (steering wheel media key) = voice command mic (`d65bb4b`, reworked in `9458024`)**: `CarMediaService.MediaSessionCallback.onMediaButtonEvent` consumes `KEYCODE_MEDIA_PREVIOUS`: the first tap waits `DOUBLE_TAP_MS` (400 ms) and then goes to `onSkipToPrevious()`, a second tap in time cancels it and calls `VoiceCommandRouter.startListening()` (same screen choice as the assistant: foreground, then the AA player owner, then the Wide; Main and Wide register a `listen` callback that starts their mic). The first version sent a "TriggerVoice" session event only to the AA player owner (nothing with no media or a Main tab playing) and still did "previous" on the first tap. `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE` added in the same commit is needed by `registerNetworkCallback` (weather refresh on network): without it the call threw and was skipped.
  - **NavigationOverlay radial menu layout adjustment (`270efdf`)**: reorganized radial menu into Inner Ring (5 items: Back, Forward, Home, Tabs, Refresh) with `125.dp` radius and Outer Ring (7 items: Search, Star, Shield, Mic, Share, Settings, Close) with `190.dp`/`200.dp`/`225.dp` radius so buttons don't overlap.
  - **Share logs (`f9a5655`, fixed in `02a52a3`)**: long press on the GitHub icon in `SettingsScreen` (version card) writes the app's logcat (`logcat -d --pid=…`, now on `Dispatchers.IO`) to `cacheDir/AABrowser_logs.txt` and opens the share chooser (`FileProvider`, `cache-path`); texts `share_logs_title` / `share_logs_failed` (11 locales); in the car only a message (`share_logs_phone_only`), the chooser would open on the phone. Release builds: only `Log.d` lines (Wide layout, fullscreen margins) and `chromium` console lines, no `AppLog`.
  - **YouTube playlist auto-scroll/focus on small screens**: on small screens, YouTube loads the playlist below the video player. When advancing tracks, YouTube's JS focuses or calls `scrollIntoView` on the active playlist item, scrolling the page down and hiding the video player at the top. New script `YouTubeManager.getPreventPlaylistScrollScript()` injected at document start and page route on YouTube: intercepts `scrollIntoView`, `focus` (passing `preventScroll: true`), `window.scrollTo` for playlist elements, and listens for track navigation (`yt-navigate-finish`, `ytmusic-navigate-finish`, `popstate`, video `play`), keeping `window.scrollTo(0, 0)` so the video player stays visible at the top when the user is at top. Narrowed in `59c74ad`: everything acts only on `/watch` pages with a `list` parameter (`inPlaylist()`); its selectors are wide (`ytm-item-section-renderer` holds results, home feed and comments) and on other pages it blocked YouTube's scroll restoration when going back to the results.
  - **YouTube AdBlock: black screen for the length of the ad with the data removal (user report)**: consistent with YouTube's server-side ads (stitched into the video): when the client removes the ad data the player shows black for the ad's duration (gHacks 2024-07-30 "YouTube's server-side ads resulted in a black screen for ad blocker users"; uBlockOrigin/uAssets issue #34947, black screen at the start of each video with uBO Lite). On the emulator (Main, AdBlock on) a normal pre-roll was removed and the video started after a short black. New switch "Remove ads in advance" under the YouTube AdBlock (`AdBlockSettings.isYouTubePruneEnabled`, pref `adblock_youtube_prune`, default on, 11 locales): off = only the DOM fallback (16x, mute, Skip, jump to the end). The bridge method is now `isYouTubeAdPruneOn()` (Main and Wide, `AdBlockSettings.isYouTubePruneOn(host)`). Diagnostics in logcat (tag `chromium`, also release builds): `AABrowser: ad data removed video=… sabr=… placements=[START:linearAdSequenceRenderer…] slots=[PLAYER_BYTES:…] playerAds=…`, then `after ad removal +Ns state=… ad=… paused=… ready=… media=…/… moving|still content=… moving|still` at every change and `content playing after N s` (or `not playing after 45 s`). With a black wait those lines tell whether the ad media plays hidden (media moving, content still) or the player just waits.
  - **Voice command from a steering wheel button (user idea: long press of "back")**: not possible: Android Auto gives apps only "back pressed", without duration or long press (Wide: Car App Library `OnBackPressedDispatcher`; Main: the AA SDK `CarActivity` exposes only `onBackPressed()`, checked in `libs/v4/aauto.aar`). The voice button belongs to the assistant, which now reaches the app with "metti … su AABrowser". To check whether a held media key (⏮/⏭) is recognizable, `CarMediaService` logs `Media button: KEYCODE_… action=… repeat=… held=… ms long=…` (`onMediaButtonEvent`) and `Transport: skip to next/previous, fast forward, rewind` (debug builds).
  - **Voice commands (mic button in Main and Wide, "Ok Google / Gemini, metti … su AABrowser")** (user request; tested on the Pixel 10 emulator through the debug adb receiver, not by voice):
    - `utils/VoiceCommands.kt`: phrase patterns per command in all 11 app languages, matched all together (the recognizer listens in the phone language, which can differ from the app's). `{q}` = free text, `{site}` = site name, space = `\s+`, `~` = optional spaces (Chinese). Text normalized: lowercase, Arabic diacritics/alef forms, Hindi nukta, aliases as whole words only ("you tube", "ютубе", "يوتيوب", "यूट्यूब", "油管", "гугле", "амазоне", "亚马逊"...: "загугли" must not become "заgoogleи"). Kind order = priority (one-word commands, YouTube Music, YouTube, navigate, site search, web search, open, search, play); inside a kind the pattern with more fixed text wins ("search for {q} on {site}" before "search {q} on {site}"). Commands: YouTube search/play, YouTube Music search/play, site search ("cerca … su Amazon", "cerca su google maps …": known sites with their own search, Amazon/eBay of the phone's country, Wikipedia in its language; a favorite or a spoken address uses its site's search or the engine with `site:`; "su Google/internet/web" = web search), web search, open (favorite by name/site name/one wrong letter, spoken address "repubblica punto it", else search), navigate, back, reload, home, pause, resume, next, previous; anything else = search on the open site (YouTube/YT Music) or the engine. `plan()` = what to do, shared by Main and Wide.
    - `utils/VoiceCommandRouter.kt`: Main and Wide register while open (foreground first, then the owner of the AA player, then the Wide); a command with no screen waits 60 s for the one that opens; "play the first result" requests (30 s) consumed on the results page (`YouTubeManager.getPlayFirstResultScript()`: first `/watch?v=` link, sponsored results skipped; tested on m.youtube.com and music.youtube.com).
    - "Play" with autoplay off: `mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture` is lifted and the `!autoplayMedia` pause skipped while the page stays on YouTube (Main per tab, Wide `voiceAutoplay`).
    - Main: mic left of the shield in the tab bar (red while listening) and in the radial menu (outer arc 8 items, radius 225 dp); `ui/VoiceCommandInput.kt` (SpeechRecognizer, partial results, 5 alternatives, permission check); `VoiceListeningPopup` shows the live text or the examples. YouTube/YT Music searches reuse that tab; directions = Google Maps web directions in a new tab. Player commands = `CarMediaService.sendPlayerCommand()` (session transport controls, same path as the AA buttons).
    - Wide: fifth sidebar button, or without the sidebar a mic action between search and shield (bottom strip full); listening card at the top of the free area; searches through `SiteSearch.resolve` like the lens; "portami a …" = `CarContext.startCarApp(ACTION_NAVIGATE, geo:0,0?q=…)` (Maps/Waze of Android Auto, not tested).
    - Assistant/Gemini (docs: developer.android.com/media/implement/assistant, "applies to Gemini too"): every state published by `CarMediaService` declares `ACTION_PLAY_FROM_SEARCH` (`publishPlaybackState`); `onPlayFromSearch` → `VoiceCommandRouter.fromAssistant()` (empty query = resume, a query = play on YouTube or YT Music); with no screen open a non-fatal error message is shown (`voice_command_open_app`). Legacy `MEDIA_PLAY_FROM_SEARCH` intent filter on `MainActivity` (phone). PREPARE actions not declared (the Assistant then calls PLAY directly). Not tested with a real Assistant/Gemini (no account on the emulator); the session path was tested with `transportControls.playFromSearch`.
    - Debug only: `src/debug` receiver `VoiceCommandDebugReceiver` (permission DUMP = only the shell): `adb shell am broadcast -n com.fcaronte.aabrowser/.debug.VoiceCommandDebugReceiver --es text "…" [--es target main|wide|assistant]`.
    - Verified on the emulator (Main): "metti never gonna give you up su youtube" → results → first result plays (also with autoplay off and with AdBlock on, no ad); "pausa"/"riprendi" pause/resume through the AA session; "cerca gatti" on YouTube searches YouTube in the same tab; "portami a via roma 10 milano" → Google Maps directions; mic button in the tab bar.
  - **YouTube AdBlock off in a Wide opened directly (user report: first video with the ad at normal speed and no skip; it worked only after opening the Main)**: `AdBlockSettings.init()` was called only by `MainScreen`, so when Android Auto started the process for the Wide alone (`CarAppService`, no Activity) `isYouTubeEnabled` stayed at its default `false` (and `isEnabled` at `true`, ignoring a user who turned AdBlock off): neither the DOM skip script nor the ad data pruning ran until the Main was opened in that process. Now it is initialized in `CarApplication.onCreate()` with the other settings; the call in `MainScreen` is removed.
  - **WhatsApp Web voice input, real fix (first try did not work)**: WhatsApp Web's message box is a Lexical editor. Tested in Chrome 156 headless on playground.lexical.dev: `textContent` + synthetic `InputEvent('input', {inputType: 'insertText', data})` (what `getSetInputTextScript` did, keyboard and voice) is reverted by Lexical and nothing reaches its state; `document.execCommand('insertText')` and a synthetic `paste` both work. To replace (keyboard) the selection must be read by Lexical first: select all + `document.dispatchEvent(new Event('selectionchange'))` + insertText works, without the event nothing changes; appending at the end works either way. `getSetInputTextScript(text, append)`: contenteditable -> selection (all, or collapsed at the end with `append`), `selectionchange`, `execCommand('insertText')` (or `delete` for empty text), old textContent + InputEvent only if the command fails; inputs/textareas unchanged (native setter + input/change), `append` adds a space and the text to the current value. The Wide voice input calls it with `append = true` after capturing the field (the text present at result time is used, not the one at the tap) and logs the result. Tested with the real script rebuilt from Kotlin: Lexical replace + append end up in the editor state, a real key afterwards continues; a plain input gets "primo" then "primo secondo" with input events. Probably the keyboard never worked in the WhatsApp message box either (maybe it was tested on the chat search input). The Main's mic still uses `getInjectTextScript`.
  - **YouTube ad data pruning at document start (vBlockTube-like technique, own code)**: the user pointed to vBlockTube (its LICENSE says proprietary/all rights reserved while the header says MIT, so no code was copied; the technique, removing ad keys from player JSON, is the well-known uBlock Origin json-prune one). `AdBlockJavascript.getYouTubeAdPruneScript()` is registered with `addDocumentStartJavaScript` for `YOUTUBE_ORIGINS` (youtube.com, *.youtube.com, youtubekids.com, *.youtubekids.com) in the Main tabs and in the Wide; it asks `AndroidBridge.isYouTubeAdBlockOn()` (YouTube AdBlock on and page host not whitelisted; new bridge method in both bridges) and then removes `adPlacements`, `adSlots`, `playerAds`, `adBreakHeartbeatParams` (top level, `playerResponse`, arrays) and `auxiliaryUi.messageRenderers.(bka)EnforcementMessageViewModel` from: `ytInitialPlayerResponse` (accessor on window, so the inline `var` assignment goes through the setter), `fetch` responses of `/youtubei/v1/player`, `get_watch` and `pbj=1` (cloned, pruned, rebuilt without content-length/encoding) and the same XHR responses (`responseText`/`response` getters). The DOM script (16x, mute, skip, jump to end) stays as fallback for ads that still play (e.g. stitched by the server). Tested in Chrome 156 headless on m.youtube.com with a mobile UA: script off -> initial response has `adPlacements` + `adBreakHeartbeatParams`, player requests `adBreakHeartbeatParams`; on -> none of them, initial and fetch/XHR; the content video (213 s) plays normally in both cases (YouTube served no pre-roll to the fresh profile, so the ad-skip effect itself is not proven).
  - **Settings: YouTube quality and location for websites moved to "Web pages"** (user request): the quality card now follows AdBlock, the location card is before Clear data (both were in the app section).
  - **Wide voice input on WhatsApp Web (keyboard worked, mic wrote nothing)**: the mic used `getInjectTextScript` (writes into `document.activeElement` with `innerText` and a plain `Event('input')`), which WhatsApp Web's message editor (contenteditable driven by JS) ignores. Now `startVoiceListening()` does like the keyboard: `getCaptureInputTargetScript()` when the mic is tapped (field stored as `window.__aabTypingTarget`, current text returned), then on the result `getSetInputTextScript(existing + " " + spoken)` (real `InputEvent`, native setter for inputs; search fields are submitted, others not), with `suppressInputPopupUntil` so the refocus does not reopen the popup. `parseCapturedField()` shared with `openFieldInput()`. The Main's voice input still uses `getInjectTextScript` (same problem likely on WhatsApp).
  - **Wide keyboard/mic popup on big screens (user report: 14" screens could not tap it)**: `updatePopupPosition()` set the margins in `card.post` with `card.width`, which was 0 at the first show (the overlay was GONE), so the card sat half a card off centre, on big screens partly under the host UI; without a visible area it was not positioned at all. Now the card is measured at once (`measure` AT_MOST the area), centred and clamped inside the visible area (whole surface if none). Sizes in dp (padding, corners, buttons min 72x56 dp, text 22 sp): in raw pixels the buttons were small on high-density screens. Auto-hide `INPUT_POPUP_TIMEOUT_MS`: tried 6 s, the user set it back to 3 s (wants the popup on screen only briefly: keep 3 s). Checked: a tap outside the card already closes it at once (overlay click listener, timer cancelled); that tap is not passed to the page.
  - **YouTube quality: chosen level instead of a fixed 1440p**: the "YouTube video quality" switch (`force_max_youtube_quality`) now opens a choice like "Use favorite as start page": 480p / 720p / 1080p / 1440p / 2160p (4K) (`AppSettings.youtubeQualityOptions`, player levels `large`, `hd720`, `hd1080`, `hd1440`, `hd2160`; saved as `youtube_quality`, default `hd1440` = old behavior). `WebViewScriptRouter.getYouTubeQualityScript(maxLevel)` picks the highest level of the video not above the choice (`setPlaybackQualityRange(target, target)` + `setPlaybackQuality`), keeps the slow-network downgrade (2g -> lowest, 3g/<3 Mbps -> at most 720p) and keeps a single interval (`window.aabQualityInterval`, before a new one was added at every page finished). Main and Wide pass `youtubeQuality` to `routeAndInject`. New `ColumnScope.SettingsChoiceList` in `SettingsScreen` (selected value card + expandable radio list), now also used by the start page favorite. Strings: label/description updated, `youtube_quality_selected_label` (11 locales).
  - **YouTube AdBlock v13: long non-skippable ads**: some ads were sped up to 16x but "never ended" (minutes long, or stalled because the buffer cannot keep up at 16x). During an ad `checkAds()` now also seeks the ad video to its end (`currentTime = duration - 0.1`, only with a finite duration > 1 s and more than 1 s left), so YouTube moves on to the content or the next ad of the pod. Only when the player itself has `ad-showing`/`ad-interrupting` (class read in the same call), never from the weaker signals (skip button, overlay, YT Music bars), so the real video is never sent to its end.
  - **Opening a new tab stopped the media of the playing tab**: the `AndroidView` `update` block of `BrowserScreen` did `if (it.tag != reloadTrigger) it.reload()`, and `MainScreen` passes `reloadTrigger = 0` to inactive tabs: when a tab stopped being the active one (new tab, other tab selected) and the global reload counter was not 0 (any reload or AdBlock shield toggle before), it reloaded (video stopped, `onPageStarted` released the player, weather back). The tag was never initialized either, so every new tab was also reloaded right after its first `loadUrl`. Removed: the reload is done only by `LaunchedEffect(reloadTrigger)` for the active tab (guarded by `lastProcessedReloadTrigger`, synced on activation). Now the playing tab keeps playing and the AA player; a playback started in the new tab takes it (the old one is paused by `MediaFocusManager`); the weather comes back when the owner tab is closed.
  - **Player card tap: the Wide no longer stops Maps' navigation (final approach)**: user log of 20/10 23:52 (release build, so no `AppLog` lines; only the `Log.d` layout lines of `WideScreen`): at every tap, Wide in front or Maps in front, `CarAppService.onUnbind` destroyed the Wide session and a new one started at once (new surface, WebView reloaded). Closing the Wide is useless: Android Auto opens it again. What stopped Maps was the Wide's own `navigationStarted()` (also called 1.5 s after each re-creation). Removed: `navigationStarted()` and its delay, `onMediaCardLaunch()`, `CloseImmediatelyScreen` and the action matching in `WideSession` (only logging is left, debug builds). The `NavigationManagerCallback` (`onStopNavigation` -> close) and the no-op session activity stay. Known limit: the tap still shows the Wide (Android Auto's choice); Maps keeps navigating behind it.
  - **AdBlock shield in the Main**: in the tab bar on the left of the back/forward arrows (`PersistentTabBar` `adBlockOff` / `onToggleAdBlock`, disabled on local pages) and in the radial navigation menu after reload (`NavigationOverlay`; the outer arc now counts its items and gets a 200 dp radius with 7 buttons). Icons `VerifiedUser` (AdBlock on) / `RemoveModerator` (site whitelisted); label `adblock_this_site` (11 locales). `MainScreen` computes the site of the active tab, toggles the whitelist, shows `adblock_site_off/on` through `feedbackMessage` and reloads (`reloadTrigger`). `AdBlockHost.whitelistVersion` (Compose state, bumped on every change) keeps both shields in sync.
  - **Wide buttons layout (user request)**: the top-right `ActionStrip` (the stock Android Auto buttons) keeps a single button, the AdBlock shield, which replaced reload; with the fixed sidebar off it also has search, on the left of the shield. The map strip holds at most 4 actions (`ACTIONS_CONSTRAINTS_MAP`) and `Action.PAN` is mandatory (without it the host sends no `onScroll`/`onFling`/`onScale`, NavigationTemplate docs), so without the sidebar it is PAN + reload, back, home (home at the bottom). The fixed sidebar has 4 buttons: home, back, reload, search (`buildReloadBitmap`, same drawing as the strip icon via `drawReload`). `goBack()` shared by sidebar and strip.
  - **Player card tap, third try (user: "still opens the Wide")**: the session activity changed nothing (as feared) and the Wide was kept whenever it had already called `navigationStarted()`, i.e. whenever its session was still alive in background (the first log was an `onNewIntent`). Now `WideScreen.onMediaCardLaunch()` keeps the Wide only if it was resumed for more than `MEDIA_CARD_IN_USE_MS` (1 s) before the tap (e.g. in the Coolwalk dashboard map area) or if `MediaFocusManager.getActiveSource()` is the Wide (the card shows the Wide's media); otherwise it closes it (`closeApp()`). `WideSession` logs the extra values too (`GH.TargetComponent` = which component Android Auto opened). If Maps' navigation still ends, Android Auto itself ends it when it brings a navigation app to the front, and only the logs can tell.
  - **Wide touch/scroll performance**: every `onScroll` (dozens per second) ran a script with `document.querySelectorAll('*')` + `getComputedStyle` on every element (thousands on YouTube), keeping the page's main thread busy: janky scroll and late taps. Now the native `webView.scrollBy` stays per event, the JS part (`BrowserJavascript.getWideScrollScript`) runs once per frame with the summed deltas (`postOnAnimation`, sub-pixel remainder kept, re-posted after `SCROLL_FLUSH_STALE_MS` if stuck on a recreated WebView) and the scrollable containers are searched at most every 1.5 s (`window.__aabScrollables`, size check before the style read). Same behavior otherwise (window + known selectors + overflow auto/scroll containers), except each container now scrolls once per step (before, one matching both a selector and the scan scrolled twice). Taps: `touch-action: manipulation` was already there (no Chromium 300 ms click delay); what is left is the host's own wait to tell a tap from a double tap before sending `onClick`, which the app cannot change.
  - **Main/Wide script parity check**: bridges compared (`@JavascriptInterface` sets) with every `AndroidBridge.*` call in the scripts. The Wide lacks only `onFolderInputClicked` (not needed: the Wide always uses `LastFileSelection`) and lacked `saveYouTubeSpeed`; both calls are guarded with `typeof`. Fixed: the Wide now saves the YouTube speed (`AppSettings.setYouTubeSpeed`) and restores it into `localStorage` on YouTube pages like the Main; `localStorage` is shared by the two WebViews, so the Main used to overwrite a speed chosen in the Wide with the older saved one.
  - **AdBlock whitelist + shield in the Wide**: `AdBlockHost` keeps a whitelist (host without "www.", subdomains included; `CopyOnWriteArraySet`, read from the WebView network threads; persisted as `adblock_whitelist` in `adblock_prefs`, same file as `AdBlockSettings`): `isWhitelisted()`, `addToWhitelist()`, `removeFromWhitelist()` (also removes entries covering the site, `removeIf` because the COW iterator cannot remove), `siteKey()`. `shouldBlock()` skips whitelisted page or resource hosts (Main and Wide). `WebViewScriptRouter` does not inject the YouTube AdBlock script on whitelisted sites. Wide: a shield action before reload in the `ActionStrip` (check = AdBlock on, crossed = site whitelisted), always present and disabled on home / AAB Media so the strip width (and the page layout) never changes; a tap toggles, shows a `CarToast` (`adblock_site_off` / `adblock_site_on`, 11 locales), invalidates and reloads; `onPageFinished` invalidates only when site/state changed (`shownAdBlockState`).
  - **Dynamic session activity for the player card (not tested)**: new `mediaservice/NoOpActivity` (translucent, finishes at once, no transition, `excludeFromRecents`, `noHistory`, `taskAffinity=""` so it does not bring the Main task to the front). `CarMediaService.setWideActive(active)` (companion flag + weak reference to the running service, applied in `onCreate`): Wide not in use -> `setSessionActivity(noOp PendingIntent)`, Wide resumed -> `setSessionActivity(null)`. `MediaSessionManager.setWideActive()` forwards to it (main thread; no MediaBrowser connection needed); `WideScreen` calls it on resume (true) and pause/destroy (false). Whether Android Auto uses the session activity for the card tap is not documented: the user's log shows it targets the CarAppService directly (`GH.TargetComponent`), so the `WideSession` handling of `MEDIA_SHOW_PLAYBACK_VIEW` stays as the second layer. Side effect: controllers that use the session activity (not the notification, which keeps its own content intent) open nothing while the Wide is not in use.
  - **YouTube AdBlock v12**: `AdBlockHost.shouldBlock(url, pageHost)` never blocks on YouTube pages (main-frame host youtube.com / youtu.be / youtubekids.com) nor resources of youtube.com, youtu.be, youtubekids.com, googlevideo.com, ytimg.com anywhere: blocking doubleclick/googlesyndication during a video made the player error out without the `ad-showing` class, so the script could not skip. Host parsed with `android.net.Uri` (no exceptions on blob:/data:/about:/javascript:). `AdBlockJavascript`: the old 500 ms loop body is `checkAds()`, called at once by a `MutationObserver` on the player (`class` in the subtree + childList: `ad-showing`/`ad-interrupting` and the skip button appearing; coalesced in a microtask), by `yt-navigate-finish` / `spfdone` / `popstate` (window and document; they re-attach the observer to a new player element) and by the 500 ms loop as fallback (it also re-attaches). Skip presses throttled to one per 300 ms. Observer and listeners are removed on re-injection (`window.aabAdObserver`, `window.aabAdNavHandler`).
  - **Weather not shown at car start until "Exit"**: `CarMediaService.onPause()` (AA pause, also sent when the car is switched off) sets `isUserPaused = true` / `showingWeather = false`; the service and the process stay alive between drives, so on the next drive `checkAndUpdateWeatherMetadata()` stayed out of weather mode. "Exit" kills the process (`Process.killProcess`), which is why it fixed it. Now `CarMediaService` observes `CarConnection(this).type` and, on a new connection after a known disconnection, resets the two flags and refreshes the weather unless something is playing/buffering. `onLoadChildren` called `checkAndUpdateWeatherMetadata()` twice (each call cancels the fetch in progress): now once.
  - **Site icons retry (empty favorites icons on first launch)**: icons are Coil 2 `AsyncImage` requests to the Google favicon service; a failed request was never repeated, so on first launch (requests sent while the app was still asking permissions, on Android 17 also the local network one, needed when DNS or proxy are on the LAN; root cause not confirmed, no log) icons stayed empty until restart. New `ui/SiteIcon.kt` `SiteIconImage()` (favorites, tab bar, tab manager): after an error it retries with growing delays (2 s steps, max 5) and at every resume, through a request parameter outside the cache key.
  - **Welcome no longer moves on by itself**: `WelcomeActivity.onResume` started the Main as soon as the base permissions were granted, so the optional "Location for websites" item could never be used. Now `onResume` only refreshes the screen; the user moves on with Start/Skip. `onCreate` still skips the welcome when all base permissions are already granted.
  - **Location for websites (optional) + welcome updated**: neither WebView handled `onGeolocationPermissionsShowPrompt`, so sites (Google Maps in the browser) never got a position. `utils/WebLocation.answer()` (Main tabs and Wide) grants when `AppSettings.webLocation` is on (`web_location`, **on by default**) and the precise location permission is granted (`ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION`, requested by the app only for this; approximate alone = weather only), never retained. First version required the option to be turned on explicitly: the user granted the permission but Maps still got nothing (the welcome had skipped the item). In the car Android treats the app as background for location (Car App Library `CarAppService` docs, "Accessing Location"), so `ACCESS_BACKGROUND_LOCATION` ("Allow all the time") is requested right after the precise permission (`WebLocation.requestMissing()` / `onPermissionResult()`, from the welcome and from Settings through `MainActivity.onRequestPermissionsResult`). The log line `WebLocation ... allowed=... (option, precise, always)` shows why a request was refused. If the car still gets no position with "Allow all the time", the next step is a foreground service of type `location`. The welcome screen now lists Local network (Android 17+, also required to auto-skip the welcome) and "Location for websites (optional)" (requests fine + coarse together and turns the option on when granted; not required to skip); the location text no longer says "exclusively weather". Settings has the same switch (in the car it shows a toast: the permission can only be granted on the phone). Strings `perm_local_network_*`, `perm_web_location_*`, `web_location_phone_toast` in 11 locales.
  - **New default favorites reach existing users, AAB Media fixed**: defaults were applied only to an empty list, so users of older versions never saw new ones (AAB Media). `FavoritesRepository` stores `DefaultsVersion` in the `BOOKMARKS` prefs (kept by `save()`, which clears the file); `DEFAULTS_ADDED_IN` maps a default id to the version that introduced it (missing = 1, lists without a version = 1). On load, defaults newer than the stored version are appended once (new UUID, skipped if the URL is already there) and the version is raised: removed ones do not come back. AAB Media (`isFixed()`, `player.local`) is re-added whenever missing, has no delete button (`FavoriteCard`), its URL field is disabled (`EditFavoriteScreen`) and the view model ignores delete/URL changes for it. `normalizeUrl()` moved to the repository companion (shared with `addMissingDefaults`).
  - **Updater checked (no change)**: the GitHub API `browser_download_url` of the 2.3 APK is `https://github.com/fcaronte/AABrowser/releases/download/2.3/AABrowser-v2.3.apk` (redirect to `release-assets.githubusercontent.com`, 200), so it passes `RELEASE_DOWNLOAD_PREFIX` (same case). The bare prefix gives 404 because it is not a page. A release without an `.apk` asset falls back to the releases page URL, which `startDownload` refuses ("download failed").
  - **Tap on the Android Auto player card no longer opens the Wide (second try, not tested)**: the package has a MediaBrowserService and a Car App Library app (`automotive_app_desc` uses `media` + `template`), so Android Auto treats the Wide as the templated media app and opens it from the media card. The Wide called `navigationStarted()` at start, so Maps' navigation in progress was stopped. The documented action is `androidx.car.app.media.action.SHOW_MEDIA_PLAYBACK` (developer.android.com/training/cars/apps/media), but the user's log showed what Android Auto really sends: `onNewIntent: action=MEDIA_SHOW_PLAYBACK_VIEW`, extras `GH.TargetComponent`, `com.google.android.gms.car.EXTRA_CAR_ACTIVITY_FLAGS`, `EXTRA_STARTED_BY_DASHBOARD`, `androidx.car.app.extra.SESSION_INFO_BUNDLE`, `AppKey`. `WideSession` matches both actions (`MEDIA_CARD_ACTIONS`): in `onCreateScreen` it returns `CloseImmediatelyScreen` (`finishCarApp()` on start, no `WideScreen`); in `onNewIntent` it calls `WideScreen.onMediaCardLaunch()`, which closes the Wide if it has not become the navigation app yet. `navigationStarted()` is now posted `NAVIGATION_START_DELAY_MS` (1.5 s) after start, so a session created by the tap and followed by `onNewIntent` closes before stopping Maps; a Wide that was already open stays (it is already the navigation app, nothing else is navigating). Opening the Main instead is not possible: a template app can only `startCarApp` navigation/call intents or itself. Both callbacks log the intent.
  - **Wide: site and favorite settings (WhatsApp Web not in desktop)**: the Wide home tiles are plain links, so the navigation left with the user agent of the previous page (mobile) and the desktop one was set only in `onPageStarted`, after the request: WhatsApp Web got the mobile version. Now `shouldOverrideUrlLoading` (main frame GETs) reloads the URL with `loadUrl()` when the page needs the other identity (`needsDesktopForUrl` vs `WebViewScriptRouter.hasDesktopUserAgent`), at most `MAX_USER_AGENT_RELOADS` in a row (reset on page finished). The full desktop spoof (`navigator.platform`, `userAgentData`...) is a document start script gated on the desktop UA (`BrowserJavascript.getDesktopSpoofScriptForDesktopUa`), because from `onPageStarted` it ran too late. The favorite of the page's site (`favoriteFor`: same host without "www." or a subdomain, most specific wins; list cached 2 s) now applies in the Wide too: `isDesktopMode` replaces the global desktop setting (sites that require desktop stay desktop, as in the Main), `mobileZoom`/`desktopZoom` replace the Wide's 1.0 scales.
  - **Security review of the pre-existing code (compiled, not tested)**:
    - **DAZN proxy (critical)**: `AndroidBridge.proxyFetch` is visible to every page and iframe (Main and Wide) and made native requests with the user's cookies, without CORS, to any address (other sites, home devices). Now `DaznProxy.proxyFetch(context, pageHost, ...)` answers 403 unless the main-frame page and the target are DAZN (`isDaznHost`: `dazn.com`, `indazn.com` and subdomains) over https, also after redirects (`conn.url.host`). The page host is tracked per WebView (`pageHost`: `onPageStarted` + main-frame `shouldInterceptRequest`). `DaznManager.AUTH_PROXY_JS` sends only DAZN https URLs to the proxy (before, any URL with "auth" in host or path, e.g. `oauth2.googleapis.com`), so the others use the normal WebView network instead of getting the 403.
    - **Updater (serious)**: the update receiver is exported (needed for `DOWNLOAD_COMPLETE`), so any app could make it download and hand an arbitrary APK to KingInstaller (silent install). `UpdateManager.startDownload` accepts only `https://github.com/fcaronte/AABrowser/releases/download/...`, and the downloaded APK is installed only if its package is `com.fcaronte.aabrowser` (`isAppPackage`; the signature is checked by Android on install).
    - **Wide host validator**: `CarServiceWide` allowed every host, so any app could bind as "the car", receive the rendered pages (logged-in sites too) and send taps. Release builds now allow only the hosts of the library allowlist (`androidx.car.app.R.array.hosts_allowlist_sample`: Android Auto, AAOS templates host); debug builds still allow all (DHU, tests).
    - **Bridge URLs**: `openInNewTab` / `openPopup` (Main) and `openInNewTab` / `openLinkInNewTab` / `openPopup` (Wide) accept only http/https with a host (`UrlUtils.isWebUrl`): pages could open `intent:`, `file:`, `javascript:` and other schemes in the app.
    - `AABrowser.md`: KingInstaller package is `com.example.kinginstaller` (the code uses it; the doc said `com.fcaronte.kinginstaller`).
    - Checked and left as is: `ForegroundService` (exported, only "STOP"), `CarMediaService` (exported MediaBrowserService: another app could only spoof the AA title), `BackupRestoreUtils`, `injectText` (same page only), PendingIntents (all immutable), no dynamic receivers, weather networking on `Dispatchers.IO`.
  - **Release 2.3 preparation**: `versionCode 14` / `versionName "2.3"` (`browser/build.gradle`); PayPal donation links `paypalme/FCaronte/4` everywhere (README, `AABrowser.md` changelog template, `donate` strings in 10 locales; the amount is not written in the README); README: "Main features" section (EN + IT, Widescreen marked Beta with its limits) and Claude credits next to Gemini; `AABrowser.md` Wide beta limitations updated (full-screen AA keyboard available, no double-tap zoom, no drag).
  - **Certificate choice remembered**: `SslExceptions` saves the accepted host:port + certificate SHA-256 in the `ssl_exceptions` preferences (loaded once per process by `SslErrorPrompts(context, ...)`), so the warning does not come back after restarting the app; a different certificate for the same site asks again. Settings > Clear data has a new "Accepted certificates (n)" checkbox (`certificates_label`, 11 locales).
  - **AAB Media theme follows the app settings**: `ui/Theme.kt` `appColorScheme()` (extracted from `AABrowserTheme`, not @Composable) is used by `LocalPlayer.themeCss()`: automatic (system or car day/night) or forced light/dark/AMOLED, dynamic phone colors when enabled. Light pages declare `color-scheme: only light` so the WebView's automatic darkening does not invert them. Only roles defined by every app palette are used (background, surfaceContainer, surfaceVariant, outline(Variant), primary, onPrimary, onSurface(Variant)).
  - **YouTube ad skip (AdBlock v11)**: visibility test by size/style instead of `offsetParent` (null inside the fixed m.youtube.com player, so the skip button was never found); skip pressed with a full pointer/mouse sequence plus the inner button of the container; the player's own `<video>` (`#movie_player video`) instead of the first one in the page (home previews); audio restored only on videos we muted for the ad (it used to unmute every muted video, previews included, which could also take the AA player); text search for "Skip/Salta..." only while an ad is showing (it read every button twice a second). Main cause found in the review: `hideSelectors` hides `.video-ads` / `.ytp-ad-module` (since `0ca8e36`), the container of the skip button, so the button was always "invisible" and never pressed (only the 16x speed-up worked): during an ad the button is now accepted even when hidden (`click()` works anyway).
  - **AAB Media crossfade**: if the second player cannot start, the crossfade is not retried for that track (it re-seeked and reopened the file on every `timeupdate`).
  - **Wide resize (split with the player / with the app menu), verified with the DHU (`wide.ini`, surface 1190x480)**:
    - Split with the player on the right: stable area right edge 746, but `rightInsetPx()` subtracted a global "buttons band" that started at 76 px (`DEFAULT_BUTTONS_RESERVE_PX`) and, once learned in one layout, was reused in the others: the WebView ended at 822, 76 px under the player card (same in the phone logs: 541 -> 617, 1439 -> 1515). Now the band is learned per host layout (`learnedReserves`, keyed by the stable area) and is 0 for a layout never seen.
    - Split with the app menu (launcher) on the left: the Wide moves to the right pane (stable 792..1178) but with the fixed sidebar the left inset was ignored (sidebar at 0, WebView from 52): sidebar and page stayed under the menu. Now a left inset at least as wide as the sidebar moves the sidebar next to it (`sidebarStart`); the narrow edge padding (16-24 px) still goes to the sidebar as before. RTL: the WebView now respects the left inset too.
    - Tested: menu -> split (WebView 52..746), split -> full screen (52..1166), full screen -> split (52..746), split -> menu (sidebar 792, WebView 844..1178); sidebar search opens the SearchTemplate. Tapping the app pane while the menu is open makes AA close the menu (host behaviour).
  - **AAB Media: Material You colors, crossfade, visual effects (verified in the Wide on the DHU)**:
    - `LocalPlayer.themeCss()` injects `dynamicDarkColorScheme(context)` (Android 12+, when `AppSettings.dynamicColor` is on) as the CSS variables of `player.css` (`/*AAB_THEME*/` in `index.html`); always the dark version; otherwise the old cyan palette. All the hard-coded colors of `player.css` became variables.
    - Options sheet (tune icon in the header): crossfade Off/3/6/10 s and effects Off/Bars/Wave/Circle, saved in `localStorage` (`aabPlayerState.crossfade`, `.effect`); strings `player_options`, `player_crossfade`, `player_off`, `player_effects`, `player_effect_*` (11 locales).
    - Crossfade (audio queue only, not with repeat-one or tracks shorter than 3x the duration): 3 s before the crossfade the ending track is preloaded on a detached `Audio` (`fadeDeck`), at the crossfade it continues there and fades out (cos) while `#media` starts the next track and fades in (sin, never below 0.02 so the app scripts still see it as audible). Android Auto and the app scripts only see `#media` (detached elements are invisible to them), so it is a normal track change for the AA player. `fadeDeck` is unlocked on the first tap (the WebView plays media only after a gesture). Also driven by `timeupdate` because page timers slow down with the screen off. Tested: switch 3 s before the end, volume ramp 0.31 -> 1.0 in 3 s.
    - Effects: Web Audio `AnalyserNode` (+ `GainNode` used by the crossfade when present) on `#media`, created only on a user tap (an AudioContext created without a gesture stays suspended and `#media` would be silent); canvas over the cover with a dark veil, 30 fps, only for audio while playing and visible. Bars = 32 log bands with falling caps (Winamp style), Wave = oscilloscope with glow, Circle = rotating radial bars with a bass halo.
    - Wide certificate warning: shorter text `ssl_warning_message_car` (AA showed only two lines and cut the advice).
  - **AAB Media: big videos skipped, Android Auto player without artist/cover/real skip (verified on the Pixel 10 emulator, Android 16, WebView 153)**:
    - Big files (e.g. 4K, 140 MB) were skipped right after the first frame. The WebView applies the request's `Range` to the stream returned by `shouldInterceptRequest` by itself (it calls `available()` as total size, `skip(first)`, then reads to EOF: `components/embedder_support/android/util/input_stream_reader.cc`, also for app-supplied 206). `LocalPlayer` skipped too, so every request not starting at 0 got data from twice the offset or `ERR_FAILED` (moov at the end of big MP4s, Chromium re-requests, resume position, seeking; probably also the MP3 "invalid main_data_begin" warnings in the phone logcat). Now `RangeInputStream`: the file from byte 0, ending at the end of the range, `available()` = bytes left to that end (clamped to int), `skip()` at most `Int.MAX_VALUE` per call; no own `Content-Length` (the WebView sets it). Tested: 140 MB 1080p with moov at the end plays in real time, seek to 22 s works, end of video moves to the next video; an M4A with moov at the end resumes at 0:26. On the user's phone logcat the 4K was H.264 3840x2160 (decoder `c2.qti.avc.decoder` started, first frame out, then the page moved to the next video).
    - The WebView has no Media Session API (`'mediaSession' in navigator` false, no `MediaMetadata`): pages could not declare title/artist/artwork/commands, so AA got only `document.title`, artist "AABrowser Audio", no cover, and skip fell back to +/-10 s. `MEDIA_SESSION_TRACKS_JS` now recreates `navigator.mediaSession` (`Navigator.prototype` getter, `MediaSession`/`MediaMetadata` constructors, artwork made absolute, no-op `setPositionState`...), except on YouTube video hosts. Tested: AAB Media -> system session title, artist, cover (embedded, video frame or default note), actions 567 (play/pause/next/previous, no seek); `cmd media_session dispatch next/previous` changes track / restarts it; Google's Media Session sample sees the polyfill without errors.
    - AAB Media counts as a track site: skip defaults at page start (`BrowserScreen.updateMediaCapabilities`, `isTrackSite` in the JS) and `window.aabReportTrackCapabilities()` re-sends the page's buttons at every playback start (the page-start reset could arrive after the first report, Wide included).
    - Covers for AA decoded at <= 512 px (`decodeScaled`); tracks without cover use `cover.png` = `R.drawable.ic_aab_media` drawn by `LocalPlayer.artworkBitmap()`.
    - `CarMediaService` re-posts the notification only when state, actions or metadata change (position updates every 500 ms made > 5 posts/s and the system shed them: "Shedding notify (update)" in the phone logcat).
    - Icon: `res/drawable/ic_aab_media.xml` (white note on a cyan rounded square) for dashboard, tab bar and tab manager (`ui/SiteIcon.kt` `siteIconModel()`), `assets/player/icon.svg` served at `https://player.local/icon.svg` for the page and the Wide home tile.
  - **Home network sites on Android 17 (Home Assistant, Proxmox, home proxy)**: two separate causes.
    - Apps targeting SDK 37 need the runtime permission `ACCESS_LOCAL_NETWORK` to reach LAN addresses (WebView included), otherwise the connection is blocked silently. Declared in the manifest and requested by `MainActivity.checkAndRequestAppPermissions()` together with the others (only on the phone: the car cannot show the system dialog). Docs: developer.android.com/privacy-and-security/local-network-permission.
    - Without `onReceivedSslError` the WebView cancels any invalid certificate silently (self-signed or issued for another name, typical of a reverse proxy reached by IP), where Chrome shows "Continue anyway". Now both WebViews ask: Main = `SslWarningPopup` overlay in the tab (background tabs wait until shown), Wide = `WideSslWarningScreen` (`MessageTemplate`, Cancel primary, back = cancel). The surface is only detached under a pushed template, so the WebView keeps waiting. `SslErrorPrompts` (one per WebView): one prompt at a time, requests with the same host:port + certificate SHA-256 join the open prompt, others are queued, all cancelled on page start / WebView release. Accepted certificates are remembered until the app is closed (`SslExceptions`, shared by Main and Wide); a different certificate on the same host asks again.
  - **Local player renamed "AAB Media", audio and video separated**: Audio / Video tabs (only when the folder has both, with counts) and separate queues: next/previous/shuffle/repeat/auto-advance stay in the queue of the playing file; switching tab only shows the other list, tapping a file there moves the queue. Startup reopens the tab of the last file. Videos: bigger stage (portrait 45vh, landscape panel 58% and height up to `100vh - 230px`), frame preview (`getScaledFrameAtTime` at 10% of the duration, max 30 s, 512 px JPEG) used as poster and as AA cover. Favorites still named "AABrowser Player" are renamed by `FavoritesRepository` (custom names untouched).
  - **Local media player of the app (`https://player.local/`, Main + Wide)**: replaces the poor test "Web Player" favorite (#11, migrated by URL in `FavoritesRepository`). Page in `assets/player/` (`index.html`, `player.css`, `player.js`), served by `LocalPlayer.intercept()` from both WebViews' `shouldInterceptRequest`:
    - `/api/library`: audio/video files of the folder remembered by `LastFileSelection` (subfolders included, max 5000); the page sorts them naturally (`Intl.Collator` numeric).
    - `/media/<id>`: the file, with `Range` -> `206 Partial Content` (fast start and seeking); `<id>` = base64url of the document id, rebuilt inside the granted tree.
    - `/api/tags/<id>`, `/api/art/<id>`: `MediaMetadataRetriever` title/artist/album + embedded cover (LRU caches). `MediaSessionManager` loads `player.local` covers through `LocalPlayer.artworkBitmap()` (no network).
    - Every data request needs the per-run token injected in the HTML (`window.AAB_PLAYER`): other sites in a tab cannot list/play the user's files; a 403 makes the page reload to get the new token.
    - Queue with shuffle, repeat all/one/off, previous (restart after 3 s)/next, resume of the last track and position (localStorage), tap on video = fullscreen, "Choose folder" (phone: system picker via the existing `webkitdirectory` flow; car: last phone choice).
    - Media Session (metadata + play/pause/next/previous/seekto) -> AA shows skip, title, artist and cover.
    - LRCLIB lyrics UI is shared with `/radio`; lookup is user-initiated only, uses the track's metadata, and does not interfere with playback when the service is unavailable.
  - **YouTube saved speed**: the saved speed was applied only once per `<video>` element (YouTube reuses it for every video) and the 1.5 s poller saved any rate, including YouTube's reset to 1x on the next video, overwriting the preference. Now each new video (key = path + `v` + `currentSrc`) gets the saved speed when it starts (unless already ≠ 1x), only user changes are saved (`ratechange`, ignoring our own sets and resets of a not-yet-initialized video), rates go through the player API too (`setPlaybackRate`, menu stays in sync), music also detected by category `Music` (language independent).
  - **Input popup only for text fields**: `INPUT_LISTENERS_JS` opened the keyboard/mic popup on any `<input>` (sliders, checkboxes...); now only text, search, email, url, tel, password, number.
  - **Generic AA metadata from the page Media Session**: `syncMetadata` uses `navigator.mediaSession.metadata` (title, artist, artwork) before the site rules, and resends when only the cover changes.
  - **AA player: skip instead of ±10 s on track sites (Main + Wide)**: new `MEDIA_SESSION_TRACKS_JS` (in `getLifecycleAndMetadataScript`) wraps `navigator.mediaSession.setActionHandler` and stores only `nexttrack`/`previoustrack` in `window.aabMediaSessionHandlers` (play/pause untouched); when the page registers them it calls `AndroidBridge.updateMediaCapabilities(false, next, prev)`. Spotify and YouTube Music report skip from document start. `NEXT_SCRIPT`/`PREVIOUS_SCRIPT` call the page handler first (with `{action}` details), then Spotify's `control-button-skip-forward/back`, then ±10 s. YouTube video hosts keep the playlist logic; `checkYouTubeCapabilities` now skips `music.youtube.com`. The old hook had been dropped in `e913e8d` during a refactor (the scripts still checked `aabMediaSessionHandlers`).
    - The Spotify branch added in `abe34e3` used `url` inside the WebView factory (the URL the tab was created with): tabs that reached Spotify by navigation still seeked ±10 s, and the Wide was not covered. Removed (with `SpotifyManager.getNextScript/getPreviousScript`) in favour of the shared scripts.
    - Capabilities are no longer recomputed from the URL on `onPageFinished` / tab activation (they overwrote what the page reported); `MediaSessionManager.resetMediaState()` resets them on every page start, then `onPageStarted` sets the URL defaults (Spotify / YT Music / YouTube playlist -> skip).
  - **Wide fixed sidebar stealing page taps**: the hit test used `leftInsetPx()` + width + 20 px tolerance (x <= 74 px) while the sidebar is drawn at x 0..38 and the WebView starts at its edge, so taps up to ~36 px (7-8 mm at 119 dpi) inside the page hit home/back/search. Now the hit test uses the drawn bounds (`sb.left`/`sb.right`), with tolerance only towards the screen edge (`SIDEBAR_OUTER_TOUCH_TOLERANCE_PX`).
  - **URL vs search, same rule everywhere**: `UrlUtils.asUrlOrNull()` (new, used by `normalizeInput` in the Main and by the Wide `SiteSearch.resolve`, replacing `HomePage.asUrlOrNull`). Text with spaces is never an address (before, "10.000 passi" matched the local-network `10.` prefix).
  - **Startup crash fixed + file/folder picker for web pages (SAF, no storage permissions)**: new favorite #11 "Web Player" (`web-player.streamlit.app`, local media player in a srcdoc iframe with `<input webkitdirectory>` "Select Folder" and `<input multiple>` "Select Files", filters by file-name extension, plays via blob URLs).
    - Crash `No ActivityResultRegistryOwner was provided via LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner` at startup: `MainScreen` replaces `LocalContext` with `createConfigurationContext()` (a `ContextImpl`, not a wrapper of the Activity), so `LocalActivity` / `LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner` (both resolved from `LocalContext`) were null below it. `MainScreen` now re-provides both (registry only when present: `MainCarActivity` has none).
    - Chromium WebView maps `webkitdirectory` to `MODE_OPEN_MULTIPLE` (`FileModeConversionHelper`) and expects the folder's descendant files, so the app cannot tell it from `multiple`: the input listener JS calls `AndroidBridge.onFolderInputClicked()` on click of a `webkitdirectory` input; the next `MODE_OPEN_MULTIPLE` request (within 1.5 s) opens `ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT_TREE` and returns the folder's files. `showDirectoryPicker()` arrives as `MODE_OPEN_FOLDER` (File System Access is enabled by WebView for targetSdk >= 36) and gets the tree URI.
    - File inputs no longer open the keyboard/mic input popup (`onStartInput`).
    - **Last selection reused** (`LastFileSelection`): phone and car pages are different WebViews and the system picker cannot open on the car, so on the car (Main `MainCarActivity` and Wide) any file request gets the last phone selection. The folder keeps a persisted read grant (survives restarts); single files stay in memory (readable while the phone Activity lives). On the phone, a folder request with a remembered folder shows "Last folder: X — Use it / Choose another" instead of the system picker, so a page reload no longer asks for the permission again.
  - **Tab close**: closing the active tab (tab bar or tab manager) now switches to the tab on its left (the new first tab if it was the first); the Dashboard opens only when no tab is left. Closing a tab on the left of the active one no longer shifts the selection to another tab (same fix for `removeWideTab`).
  - **Settings**: location permission request and restart fallback used `context as? Activity` (always null under `MainScreen`): now `LocalActivity.current`.
  - **Multi-tab Android Auto player: "last started playback wins"** (replaces the old close-tab/reload/navigate-home workaround):
    - Every WebView is now its own media client: each Main tab (`BrowserScreen`) creates its own `MediaSessionManager`, the Wide keeps its own. `MediaFocusManager` arbitrates between all of them.
    - When a playback starts in a client, that client takes the AA player; the previous owner is paused (PAUSE_SCRIPT) and detached, but its page is never closed, reloaded or sent to home.
    - Only the owner publishes metadata/state and receives AA commands (play/pause/next/prev/seek). With no owner, commands are ignored.
    - Selecting the "Wide:" tab in Main converts it in place into a normal Main tab (same id, same WebView, no reload); the Wide stays on its page.
  - **Root causes fixed** (why hooking the player was unreliable):
    - The JS bridge and WebViewClient in `BrowserScreen` are created once in the `AndroidView` factory, so they captured a frozen `isTabActive`: tabs created in background never reported media, tabs created active kept reporting from background. Now read through `rememberUpdatedState`.
    - A single shared Main `MediaSessionManager` had `onPlay/onPause/...` bound to the last created WebView, so AA commands could reach the wrong tab.
    - Bridge calls ran on the JavaBridge thread; the delayed (500 ms) PLAYING could be published after a later pause or after another client took focus. Now all state changes run on the main thread, the pending PLAYING is coalesced, re-checked and cancelled on focus loss.
    - Metadata updates no longer force STATE_PLAYING (a paused page stays paused on AA).
  - **Self-pause right after a playback starts (page change) fixed**: `CarMediaService` called `requestAudioFocus()` on every metadata update (and on AA play). The WebView (Chromium `AudioFocusDelegate`) already holds audio focus while playing, so the service stole it and Chromium paused the video ~1 s after it started (seen in logcat: WebView requestAudioFocus -> service requestAudioFocus -> `onAudioFocusChange(-1)` to `AudioFocusDelegate` -> page `pause`). Same fix as commit `bd244ca` ("fix spotify auto pause"), which had been undone by `3053222`.
  - **YouTube tap-to-seek (Main + Wide)**: the m.youtube.com player (2026: `div.ytPlayerProgressBarDragContainer[role=slider]`, 44 px tall) seeks only by dragging; a tap on the bar is ignored (reproduced with Chrome 155 headless, same UA/viewport as the car WebView: 1075x514 CSS px, dpr 0.744). New `YouTubeManager.getTapToSeekScript()`, injected by `WebViewScriptRouter` for YouTube: a still tap inside the visible bar seeks to that point (skips overlapping buttons, ads, live streams). Drags are untouched (no preventDefault/stopPropagation). In the Wide dragging is still impossible: the Car App Library `onScroll` gives only distances, no finger position, and is converted to page scroll.
  - **YouTube voice search (Wide)**: m.youtube.com voice search does not use `SpeechRecognition`; it records with `getUserMedia({audio})` (checked in the ytmweb bundle). The Wide `onPermissionRequest` granted only `RESOURCE_PROTECTED_MEDIA_ID`, so the mic was denied (`NotAllowedError`). Now it also grants `RESOURCE_AUDIO_CAPTURE` (camera still denied); the Main already granted it.
  - **Wide keyboard re-enabled (⌨️ in the input popup)**: the old flow injected the text into `document.activeElement` after the full-screen `SearchTemplate` closed, but meanwhile the page loses focus (`activeElement` = BODY), so the text went nowhere. Now `openFieldInput()` first runs `BrowserJavascript.getCaptureInputTargetScript()` (stores the field in `window.__aabTypingTarget`, returns value/hint; password values never returned), opens `WideSearchScreen` prefilled (`initialText`, `allowEmpty`), then `getSetInputTextScript(text)` re-focuses that field, sets the value with the native setter (React-safe) + `input`/`change`, and submits only search fields (`form.requestSubmit()` or Enter). Tested in local Chrome 155 (WebView UA): YouTube search after blur -> `/results?search_query=...`; login field typed, not submitted. The popup is suppressed for 1.5 s after typing (`INPUT_POPUP_SUPPRESS_MS`). A docked "bottom" keyboard like the Main's is not available to Car App Library apps (only full-screen `SearchTemplate`/`SignInTemplate`); the Main uses the projected SDK `InputManager`, which template apps cannot use.
  - **Wide double-tap-zoom block on YouTube**: `getDisableDoubleTapScript()` used `style.innerHTML`, blocked by YouTube's Trusted Types ("This document requires 'TrustedHTML' assignment" in logcat), so the style never applied there. Now uses `style.textContent`.
