- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/car/WideSslWarningScreen.kt` (new): certificate warning of the Wide (`MessageTemplate`; deprecated `setTitle`/`setHeaderAction` on purpose, `setHeader` needs Car API 7 and the app supports 5).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/BrowserScreen.kt`, `car/WideScreen.kt`: `onReceivedSslError` -> `SslErrorPrompts`; prompts cancelled on page start and on tab close / WebView release.
- `res/values*/strings.xml`: `ssl_warning_*`, `player_tab_audio`, `player_tab_video` (11 locales); `favorite_default_name_11` = "AAB Media".
- `browser/src/main/assets/player/*`, `utils/LocalPlayer.kt`, `model/FavoritesRepository.kt`: AAB Media (tabs and separate queues, video stage and preview frame, rename migration).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/LocalPlayer.kt` (new): server of `https://player.local/` (page, library, media with Range, tags, covers, token).
- `browser/src/main/assets/player/index.html`, `player.css`, `player.js` (new): the local player page.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/BrowserScreen.kt`, `car/WideScreen.kt`: `LocalPlayer.intercept()` first in `shouldInterceptRequest`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/LastFileSelection.kt`: `rememberedFolderUri()`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/mediaservice/MediaSessionManager.kt`: local covers via `LocalPlayer.artworkBitmap()`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/model/FavoritesRepository.kt` + `res/values*/strings.xml`: favorite #11 -> "AABrowser Player" (`https://player.local/`), migration of the old test URL; `player_*` strings (11 locales).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/AdBlockJavascript.kt`: YouTube speed handling rewritten (`applyRate`, `trackUserSpeed`, `videoKey`, `isMusicContent`).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/BrowserJavascript.kt`: input popup only for text fields; Media Session metadata in `syncMetadata` + cover in the dedupe.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/BrowserJavascript.kt`: `MEDIA_SESSION_TRACKS_JS`; Spotify skip fallback and `{action}` details in `NEXT_SCRIPT`/`PREVIOUS_SCRIPT`; `checkYouTubeCapabilities` ignores YouTube Music.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/BrowserScreen.kt`: track sites (Spotify, YouTube Music) default to skip; no capability recompute on page finished / tab activation; skip callbacks back to the shared scripts.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/SpotifyManager.kt`: removed `getNextScript`/`getPreviousScript`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/mediaservice/MediaSessionManager.kt`: capabilities reset with the page state.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/car/WideScreen.kt`: sidebar hit test on the drawn bounds.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/UrlUtils.kt` + `car/WideDashboardScreen.kt`: `asUrlOrNull()` shared by Main and Wide; `HomePage.asUrlOrNull` removed.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/WebFileChooser.kt` (new): `onShowFileChooser` handling for the Main tabs (`rememberWebFileChooser()`; without an `ActivityResultRegistryOwner` it falls back to `LastFileSelection`) + `WebFileChooserDialog` (reuse the remembered folder). Files: `FileChooserParams.createIntent()`; `webkitdirectory`: tree picker + listing via `DocumentsContract` (one query per folder, hidden entries skipped, max 5000 files); `showDirectoryPicker()`: tree URI with persisted permission.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/LastFileSelection.kt` (new): last phone selection (files in memory, folder persisted in `WEB_FILE_CHOOSER_PREFS` + persisted grant), `deliver()` for the car (Main and Wide), folder listing.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/car/WideScreen.kt`: `onShowFileChooser` -> `LastFileSelection.deliver()`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/model/TabManager.kt`: `removeTabAt()` shared by `closeTab`/`removeWideTab` (left neighbour after closing the active tab, index kept on the same tab).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/SettingsScreen.kt`: `LocalActivity.current` instead of `context as? Activity`.
- `res/values*/strings.xml`: `file_chooser_last_folder`, `file_chooser_use_folder`, `file_chooser_change_folder` (11 locales).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/MainScreen.kt`: re-provides `LocalActivity` and `LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner` next to the localized `LocalContext`; tab bar close goes to the Dashboard only when no tab is left.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/BrowserScreen.kt`: removed the broken `BrowserScreen`/`BrowserScreenInternal` wrapper and the two launchers; `onShowFileChooser` delegates to `WebFileChooser`; bridge method `onFolderInputClicked()`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/BrowserJavascript.kt`: `INPUT_LISTENERS_JS` ignores file inputs and reports `webkitdirectory` clicks.
- `model/FavoritesRepository.kt` + `res/values*/strings.xml`: default favorite #11 "Web Player".
- `browser/build.gradle`, `gradle/libs.versions.toml`: `documentfile` dependency removed (unused).
- `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`: Updated project memory log.
- `CLAUDE.md`: Imports `AABrowser.md` + `PROJECT_MEMORY.md` so every Claude Code session (CLI or Android Studio plugin) loads the project context automatically.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/mediaservice/CarMediaService.kt`: Removed `requestAudioFocus()` from the metadata path and from `onPlay` (audio focus belongs to the WebView).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/mediaservice/MediaFocusManager.kt`: Strict ownership (`isFocusOwner` true only for the real owner), pauses the previous owner on switch, 1 s re-claim guard against play/pause ping-pong, no more `navigateToHome()` of the Wide.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/mediaservice/MediaSessionManager.kt`: One instance per WebView, thread-safe (main thread), `isPlaybackStart` flag, `canTakeFocus` / `fallbackTitle` providers, `releaseMediaFocus()` (clears the AA player if owner), pauses playbacks that cannot own the player.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/BrowserScreen.kt`: Owns its `MediaSessionManager` (created/connected per tab, disconnected on dispose), uses up-to-date `isTabActive`, releases the player on page start, removed the tab-activation "claim" script.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/ui/MainScreen.kt`: Removed the shared media session and the manual player reset on tab close (the closed tab clears the player only if it owned it).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/car/WideScreen.kt`: Sends `isPlaybackStart`, releases the player on every page start (not only home), page title as fallback; grants microphone (`RESOURCE_AUDIO_CAPTURE`) to pages; keyboard button back in the input popup with capture-then-type flow.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/car/WideDashboardScreen.kt`: `WideSearchScreen` accepts `initialText` and `allowEmpty`.
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/model/TabManager.kt`: Non-destructive `switchTab` for the wide tab; `findTabIndexByUrlOrHost` ignores the wide mirror tab (opening a site from Main never lands on it).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/YouTubeManager.kt`: `getTapToSeekScript()` (tap on the YouTube mobile progress bar = seek).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/WebViewScriptRouter.kt`: Injects the tap-to-seek script on YouTube pages (shared by Main and Wide).
- `browser/src/main/java/com/fcaronte/aabrowser/utils/BrowserJavascript.kt`: `MEDIA_LISTENERS_JS` reports only audible media (muted previews / background videos can't steal the player), unmute while playing counts as a start, media already playing when listeners attach is reported; `getDisableDoubleTapScript()` uses `textContent` (Trusted Types safe).

## 3. Architectural Decisions
- **Bluetooth input reaches the car through a screen of the phone, not through the car**: the events of a device paired with the phone go to the phone's foreground app, so `BtInputActivity` captures them (pointer capture + key dispatch) and `BtInputRouter` forwards them to the car screen that is in front; the two car screens are `Target`s registered only while in front. Everything that does not need Android (shortcut table, which keys stay with the phone, pointer clamping and speed, wheel distance, button differences) is `BtInput`, pure and tested; add a shortcut there with a test. The driving cover must stop it like it stops touches: a new car-screen input path checks `DrivingState.isLocked` / `isDriveLocked()`. Keys that belong to the phone (Back, Home, volume, media keys) are never taken: the wheel and the headset keep their way to the media session. The Wide gets the mouse as the host's own gestures (`onClick`, `onScroll`), so every Wide feature keeps one entry point; the Main gets real touches at the root of its window. Reading `/dev/input` with the Shizuku shell service (it would work with the phone locked) is the other possible capture and was left out of v1 on purpose.
- **In the Wide a page's top bar leaves the host's corner free**: the host keeps one unremovable button at the top right, so any page with something there (home, Media pages, Other Apps, the TV strip) uses the `in-wide` class (set by the app at document start) and `--host-end`; never hard-code the corner in a page's CSS.
- **The Wide's Exit ends the process**: `finishCarApp()` is an orderly close and Android Auto answers with its home (the Main and the Wide side by side); killing the process is what the Main's exit does, and Android Auto recreates the Wide. Never kill it for anything else.
- **The Wide's home is a page of the app, not HTML in the code**: it follows the app's theme, looks like the Main's dashboard and edits the same favorites list; anything new on the Main's dashboard (a button, a setting that changes how a favorite behaves) goes into `HomeApi` + `assets/home` too, and the favorite rules live only in `FavoriteEdits` (the Main's view model and the home API call it: never a second copy). The page's mutations are GET requests with the token that only the home HTML carries; after any change of the saved list from outside a composition call `FavoritesRepository.notifyExternalChange()` and `Snapshot.sendApplyNotifications()`, or the Main keeps its stale in-memory list and can overwrite the change.
- **In a car window a touch target is at least ~36 CSS px, in a page whose scale is 0.74 device px per CSS px (119 dpi)**: the Main's 28 dp buttons are too small there. Notices go to the top of the page (the bottom has the controls). Test a new page at 1075x514 and 390x844, light and dark.
- **Where the host gives no finger position (drag, fling, Back) the split panel that gets it is shown**: the active panel has a visible mark; gestures that do have a position (tap, pinch) decide the panel by themselves. A fullscreen video in the browser panel fills only that panel (user, 15/11/2026: it must not cover the split view); the app stays visible and its zone keeps its taps, and the car's Back leaves the video first. The app's panel has a minimum readable width (bar + 240 dp of the car) before the percentage applies.
- **No general "desktop mode" setting**: desktop or mobile is decided per site (icon) and per favorite (edit form, with the zoom); the order is site → favorite → mobile. Only the base zooms (mobile, desktop) are global.
- **The page decides "the user paused" only from the app's hooks, never from the `pause` event**: an audio focus lost, a call or a dropped signal pause a media element exactly like the user does, and a recovery that read the event would either fight every pause or never help. The user's pause arrives through `aabMarkUserPause` / `aabMarkUserPlay` (the app's `PAUSE_SCRIPT`, `STOP_SCRIPT`, `PLAY_SCRIPT`: Android Auto, wheel, voice, another playback taking the player) and the page's own controls; anything else that stops is a failure and is retried, with growing waits so that a dead channel costs nothing. A recovery that touches audio first asks the phone (`otherAudioPlaying`: a call or another player): never take the audio from someone else. New pages that want the same behaviour reuse `recover.js` and must call `userPause()` on every pause path they own.
- **A page element styled with `display` needs its own `[hidden]` rule**: author CSS (`.icon-button { display: flex }`) beats the `hidden` attribute, which only works where the stylesheet has an explicit `…[hidden] { display: none }`. Check a newly hidden element with `getClientRects()` in the headless test, not with the attribute.
- **Favorites are never a chip among categories**: in the CarWeb Media lists (TV, Radio, Podcast) they are a toggle beside the search field, always in view, so they cannot scroll away with many categories.
- **A phone app and the browser share audio through Android, not through us**: the app takes the focus by itself; we only observe (`VirtualAppAudio`) and resume the browser when it is certain that the app interrupted it (`AudioHandoff`), never after a pause the user chose. Do not add an `AudioFocusRequest` for an app on the virtual display.
- **A virtual display has the shape of the view that shows it**: never swap its width and height to force an orientation (the picture and the touches would be deformed); to get a portrait app, give the view a portrait shape (the split panel, capped by `VirtualSplit.displayWidth`) and limit the density (`VirtualSplit.density`) so that the app sees a phone's width. The bar of a phone app is always beside the app, never over it. Split sizes are never dragged (the Wide has no finger position for drags): they are chosen in Settings. In the Wide's split, gestures without a position (scroll, fling, pinch, Back) go to the panel tapped last.
- **One settings menu, driven by a schema, for both car apps**: a new setting goes into `SettingsSchema` (a row with its string id and key, plus `showIf` if it depends on another value) and into `SettingsApi.registry` (read and a validated write); `SettingsSchemaTest` fails when the two disagree or when a text is missing in a language. The page never knows a setting. Things only the phone can do (pickers, system dialogs, addresses and passwords, restart, open a link) go through `AndroidBridge.run(name)` of the Main's settings WebView and are written in `SettingsPhoneActions`; where there is no bridge the page says so. Never let addresses or passwords through the page. The settings WebView loads nothing but app pages. Sliders are not allowed (the Wide cannot drag): use `Stepper`.
- **What the screen of the phone is doing comes from the system; the events only hurry it up**: `ACTION_SCREEN_ON/OFF` can be missed (an app opened on a sleeping phone that the shell wakes in the same instant lost it), so any state shown to the user about the phone ("standby") is re-read from `PowerManager` while it is shown, and the receiver is registered before the first read. A timeout is not a fix: it hides a real standby too.
- **A stream given to the WebView through `shouldInterceptRequest` has the length `available()` says, and a media element stops there**: fine for fetch/XHR (hls.js, mpegts.js read to the end), fatal for `<audio src>`/`<video src>` (the radio ended after 2 s). An endless stream for a media element goes through `IptvProxy.LiveStream`; a finite file needs its real size. Never wire an audio element to Web Audio before knowing that every stream it will play is same-origin and healthy, and whatever is built on the proxy path needs a way back (`proxyFailed`), because tests in desktop Chrome with a Node mock cannot show this kind of bug.
- **The app name in a text is `CarWeb`, and a rename is not a find and replace**: texts that the user reads (strings in the 11 locales, the artist shown on the Android Auto player, the names of files that are shared) say CarWeb, from `R.string.app_name` where code needs it; `AABrowser` stays only as the package name and in internal identifiers (log tags, console prefixes, theme function, the old-name migration). Changing a name changes the grammar around it: elisions and articles (French `d'`, Italian `ad`, Hungarian `az`/`a`), case suffixes (Hungarian), gender (Portuguese): take the forms the same language already uses for CarWeb (`grep CarWeb values-xx/strings.xml`) and read the sentences again. A locale file can be left behind: after a rename, `grep -c <old name> res/values*/strings.xml` must give 0 everywhere.
- **The GitHub repository has two names for a while and one object knows them (`GitHubRepo`)**: updates look on `CarWeb` first, then on `AABrowser`; `isOfficialReleaseUrl` accepts exactly those two (never `kododake/AABrowser` or `slashmax/AABrowser`, the projects CarWeb comes from, which have the old name); the links to open and the identification sent to services use the name that answered last, the OLD one before a check has answered (it works before the rename and, through the redirect, after it); a network error ends the check (the second name is on the same server). Never write the repository name or address anywhere else, and never put the new name in a static string before the rename: it is a 404 until then. When no installation knows only the old name any more, remove it from `GitHubRepo.NAMES` (and from `GitHubRepoTest`). The package name stays `com.fcaronte.aabrowser`.
- **A radio station is not "working" because Radio Browser says so**: `lastcheckok` only means the connection opened and some audio arrived; there are stations that never answer, or that send the same stored burst at every connection and then nothing (a frozen source), or HLS lists that no longer advance. The Radio page watches the playback itself (20 s to the first sound, 8 s of frozen time) and says so instead of staying mute. Do not hide, reorder or delete stations on the strength of one test: a frozen source can come back, and the list is the user's.
- **The four CarWeb Media pages share one header nav, identical in all of them**: Media, TV, Radio, Podcast in that order, each always in the same place; the page you are on is shown (accent colour, `aria-current`) but is a `<span>`, never a link to itself (reloading Media stops the music, and TV/Radio/Podcast would restart). The HTML is static and repeated in each file on purpose (no flash, no dependency on the page's script) and `pages-nav.test.js` fails if the four copies drift apart (order, hrefs, SVGs, labels, the one marked page). A new page in this family goes into all four navs and into the test. Whatever is added to those headers must fit a 360 px wide phone (the Media header also holds the options button and "Scegli cartella"): check the widths with `pagenav.mjs` before adding a fifth item.
- **One dock for both apps, from one model**: the sidebar buttons are the Main FAB's actions (`SidebarAction`), one saved list (`sidebar_actions`) for the Wide and the Main, each app showing the buttons that exist there (`SidebarHost`; tabs only in the Main). Home is essential and cannot be hidden. The Wide has no mode switch: the dock is always built, and the single top-right button the host requires shows or hides it (and the phone app's bar with it). In the Main the dock replaces the radial menu when turned on (never both: the round button toggles the dock). New buttons go into `SidebarAction` first, then the Wide (`performDockAction`, `WideDockIcons`), the Main (`NavigationOverlay.performDockAction`, `icon()`) and the Settings card (it follows the enum by itself).
- **No EPG for the default IPTV list** (evaluation above): do not build one on scraped data. An EPG, when done, comes from the user's own Xtream account or from the `url-tvg` of their M3U lists, read as a stream and kept per list.
- **The IDE edit tools time out on `WideScreen.kt` (3,000 lines) and apply the edit anyway**: after "timed out" wait ~40 s and check with Grep, then go on; batch several chunks per call.
- **A Web Audio graph only takes a same-origin source**: `createMediaElementSource` routes the element's audio through the graph for good, and a cross-origin stream without CORS then comes out as silence (the analyser reads zeros and the element is mute: measured). Any page that wires an element (Music, Radio) must make sure everything the element will ever play is of the page's origin (the app's `IptvProxy` for direct streams, hls.js/MSE for HLS, `/media/<id>` for files) before wiring, and create the `AudioContext` inside a tap. Never add effects to a page that plays direct third-party streams without this.
- **A notice about what the user sees comes from what the view draws, not from what a request answered**: the "this app stays black" message is based on a sample of the `TextureView` (`BlackFrame`), has to last (six black looks in a row) and speaks once per app. The request for a protected display is always made, in silence, and its reason is in the log only. Do not add switches for things the app can simply try.
- **Visual effects are drawn by one module** (`effects.js`: `AABEffects.attach`); each page owns its audio graph and says when to draw. A new effect goes there, with a line in `effects.test.js`; the choice lists (`EFFECTS`) come from the module.
- **The power button wins over any screen wake lock** (a lock only holds against the timeout): an app on the car screen has to wake the phone again after `ACTION_SCREEN_OFF` (wake lock with `ACQUIRE_CAUSES_WAKEUP` plus `PowerManager.wakeUp`, from the shell service, with retries and a flicker guard), wake it on a tap on the car screen, put the standby screen back on `ACTION_SCREEN_ON`, and say on the car screen that the phone went to sleep: a black car screen with no word is a bug. Anything the service does for the power state answers with a String that goes to the app log.
- **The desktop/mobile choice is per site, and a site is its registrable name without `www.` and without `m.`, `mobile.`, `touch.`**: sites change host between the two versions (`m.youtube.com`/`www.youtube.com`); never key a choice on the exact host of the page it was made on.
- **What's new is data, not code**: `assets/whatsnew/<versionName>/<language>.txt`, shown once after an update (never on a fresh install) on whichever screen opens first (phone dialog, Main card in the car, Wide long message) with one shared state, marked seen only when the user closes them; English is the fallback; each release adds its folder with the bilingual notes. On the car screen never a dialog window: a card in the Main, a template in the Wide.
- **Every touch that goes to a virtual display is rebuilt, never forwarded as it came**: the Wide's from Android Auto's gestures (`VirtualTouch`), the Main's from the view's real events (`RawTouch`: same pointers and positions, finger tool type, touchscreen source, device 0, flags 0, pressure and size 1, times of the event only when they are of now). The Wide's rebuilt touches are the ones proven to swipe; keep both builders alike.
- **In the Wide the phone app goes under the Android Auto buttons, like the fullscreen video** (user, 14/11/2026): `WideScreen.fullscreenMargins` for the layer, then the fixed menu; the app's own controls in the corners may end up under the buttons, and its side bar sits on the layer's left edge. Do not go back to the stable area.
- **A screen that has to be seen at a glance is not dimmed to the system minimum**: the standby screen is at 15% brightness with 90% / 70% white text, because at `BRIGHTNESS_OVERRIDE_OFF` the text was invisible and the phone looked frozen; whatever stays on for hours moves a little (OLED).
- **When an option cannot be honoured, say it**: the protected-content switch may be refused by the system (`secure=refused(...)` from the service); the views show `vapp_secure_refused` instead of leaving a black app unexplained.
- **The area of a fullscreen video is the screen, not the fullscreen element's box**: YouTube for computers fullscreens `<html>`, as tall as its content (0 px), and a size taken from that element made the video vanish. `fullscreenBox()` in `video-fit.js` uses the element only when it is a real element covering at least half of the screen. Test such scripts with the site's two UIs (mobile and desktop user agents) before calling them done; headless Chrome with the WebView user agent and a CDP driver reproduces YouTube's fullscreen (the EU cookie dialog sits in a cross-origin iframe: set the `SOCS` cookie first; click through `Input.dispatchMouseEvent` or `Runtime.evaluate` with `userGesture: true`).
- **Phone apps on the car screen live on a virtual display made by the Shizuku service, and the phone has to stay awake for them**: on Android 17 the display shares the phone's display group, so a sleeping or locked phone pauses the apps ("Apro l'app…" forever, or a frozen image). Whatever keeps the phone awake (wake lock) and puts the black standby screen over the lock screen is done by the shell service and by activities started from the shell (no background-activity-start limits); it is bound to `VirtualAppSession.updateInUse` (app in front on the car + `sharesPhoneGroup`), released 8 s after the last app leaves, and released by Shizuku itself (`daemon(false)`) if the app process dies. Never keep the phone awake for an app that is not in front on the car.
- **Views that show a virtual display in a car window are TextureViews** (Main tab and Wide Presentation): a SurfaceView stayed empty in the projected window and under Compose layers. The display has the shape of its view (landscape in the car, portrait on a vertical car screen), rotation frozen, orientation requests ignored: never rotate the display.
- **Touch on the virtual display**: Main forwards the view's raw `MotionEvent`s (display size = view size); the Wide receives only gestures from Android Auto and rebuilds touches (`VirtualTouch`), so its pinch, fling and drag are approximations. Protected content (Netflix, Widevine L1) cannot be drawn on a virtual display: the "protected content" switch only asks for a `SECURE` display and falls back when the system refuses.
- **`app://package` opens only from the Other Apps page (Main) or from the Wide home / Apps page, in the main frame and with a user gesture**; the page's API and icons need its token. Package names of the user's apps never go into the logs (they are personal context).
- **UI added to a page in fullscreen goes inside the fullscreen element**: Chromium makes the rest of the document inert while an element is fullscreen, so a popover or an element outside it is drawn but takes no taps. A bare `<video>` in fullscreen cannot host anything.
- **Fullscreen video in the Wide**: every site goes under Android Auto's button bands, centred (user choice, 10/11/2026); a site's controls in the corners may end up under them, and Back always leaves fullscreen. Anything Android Auto takes beyond its usual bands for a layout (a call, a panel) always stays out. The fixed menu: one choice for all the sites, kept by the app.
- **State of the CarWeb Media pages lives in the app too**: what a player page must keep across a reinstall goes through `AABPageStore` (store.js) to `MediaPages` (`media_pages`, in Google's backup and in the export file). Never addresses or passwords there: they stay in `iptv_sources`, which never goes to Google's backup and only into the file the user exports. Page writes are GET `/api/pages/save` with the token (`shouldInterceptRequest` gets no request body).
- **The backup file goes through Android's file picker, with typed values**: never read back files from public folders by path. A new prefs file that should survive a reinstall goes into `BackupRestoreUtils.PREFS`; one tied to this phone or holding certificates stays out.
- **While a voice command listens, "the current track" is `playerMetadata()`**: the session's metadata is "In ascolto…" then, and comparing with it replaced the kept track metadata.
- **One notification post per update**: `startForeground` already posts it; `notify` only if it fails (Android drops media notifications above about 5 a second).
- **Videos of the player pages**: fullscreen goes to `.stage` (with its bar), never to the bare `<video>`; a tap on the video shows the bar. A page element that must take the Wide's scroll alone sets `window.__aabScrollOnly`.
- **Explicit next/previous are not the buttons**: with seek declared and skip not, `onSkipToNext`/`onSkipToPrevious` come only from explicit requests (voice, assistant), so the scripts may do the real "next" there (YouTube: the suggested video). A page that wants its own next without turning Android Auto's −10/+10 s into skip buttons defines `window.aabNextItem`/`aabPreviousItem` (Podcast); registering `nexttrack` would change the buttons.
- **Favorites cross the lists**: TV favorites carry their list (`s`) and play from it, Radio favorites keep their data in `state.extra`, followed podcasts are global. A favorite never forces a change of the list chosen in the menu.
- **Prompts on the car screen are cards or overlays, never dialog windows** (page dialogs, certificates, site permissions, the Passenger disclaimer).
- **Podcasts: nothing but names and feed addresses from the catalog, no API keys** (Podcast Index and the like are not needed: Apple plus RSS/OPML cover everything; never put a key in the repository). Feeds are read with SAX so they can be tested on the JVM; anything that must stop early throws through `StoppingHandler.stop`.
- **Media keys the state does not declare never reach `onSkipToNext`**: Android Auto forwards them and the session gets them, but the framework default drops them. What must react to a key goes in `onMediaButtonEvent`. Declaring skip replaces Android Auto's on-screen ±10 s with skip buttons: only the user's choice `youtubeVideoSkip` does it on a single video.
- **Shares follow their own rule, not the voice one**: the Main by default, the Wide only when it is in front (`VoiceCommandRouter.shareTarget`). Voice commands keep `choose()`.
- **Podcasts (if built): nothing copyrighted in the repository**: no catalog, audio or covers shipped; charts and search asked at run time, audio from the publisher's server, covers from the feed, no Podcast Index key in the repository.
- **Share to CarWeb**: connected to the car it always goes to the car, also with "always"; only away from the car it opens the phone Main. The default stays "only with the car connected".
- **A file received from another app is read only through its grant**: the grant given with the intent dies with the receiving activity, so anything used later needs `grantUriPermission` on our own package (released at the next file). Never accept `file:` addresses or the app's own provider in an exported activity.
- **`onPageFinished` does not mean "this page is open"**: the WebView calls it also for cancelled navigations, with their address. Whatever stores the URL checks `view.url == url`, and a URL reported by the WebView never becomes a `loadUrl` (it would undo a Back and cut the forward pages).
- **Lists of TV and Radio are added only in the native Settings of the phone**: addresses and passwords never go through the page, which only asks to open Settings (bridge true only for the phone Main on `player.local`) or says to use the phone.
- **Phone Back**: every full-screen overlay has its `PhoneBackHandler` (later composed = answers first); the last Back on the home is the system's (handler off for 3 s), never a process kill, because the process also runs the car apps.
- **AdBlock list = downloaded, never bundled**: the lists (HaGeZi by default, AdGuard, or any address) are GPL-3.0 and change daily; the app downloads the chosen one (3 days), keeps only hashes outside the backup, and never replaces a good list with a broken download. Rules beyond plain domains (wildcards, regex, element hiding) are skipped on purpose: a full uBlock-style engine is step 3, only if needed.
- **Ads that catch taps**: without multiple windows a `_blank` link in a frame replaces the whole page, and `shouldOverrideUrlLoading` does not see frame navigations, so what happens inside a frame is decided by the page script (`POPUP_INTERCEPTOR_JS`). Blocking an ad is not enough when its transparent link stays over the player: remove the link and pass the tap to what is below. A blocked page answers 204, never an empty 200.
- **Pages opened by scripts**: every `window.open` and script-driven link goes through `PopupGuard` (same site, tapped link's site, sign-in; never an ad host); blocked windows get a fake window object, never null. New bridge methods that open pages must take the tapped link and ask `PopupGuard`; the per-site shield turns all of it off.
- **Commands from outside a screen go to the car first**: `VoiceCommandRouter` keeps every registration with `inCar`; the phone Main gets a command only when no car screen of that kind is open, and links shared to the car (`carOnly`) never go to the phone.
- **Only two car apps, CarWeb (projection) and CarWebWide (navigation)**: the Wide as a POI or weather category app (map template with the mandatory panel) was tried and dropped (user, 07/11/2026), and a third icon is not wanted. Do not retry other Car App Library categories for the Wide without new facts. Both apps must stay visible in Android Auto's menu: a hidden CarWebWide cannot be started from CarWeb (user test 07/11/2026), so a single icon is not possible.
- **Regular expressions run on ICU on Android**: `java.util.regex` there is ICU, stricter than the JVM of the unit tests (a lone `}` or `{` is a syntax error on the phone only, and a pattern in an `object` breaks the whole class). Always escape `{` `}` `[` `]` outside character classes and quantifiers; a new pattern can be checked against the system ICU on the PC (`uregex_open`).
- **Requests to the CarWeb Media pages stay in the app**: a voice request goes through `MediaVoice` and the page fetches it with its token; never through URL parameters (any site could navigate to them and a reload would repeat them). Native code never passes stream addresses or results through the plan.
- **One name matcher**: anything that finds a channel, station or file by name goes through `MediaMatcher`; start by itself only at `CONFIDENT` or above, otherwise show the list.
- **Gemini does not drive CarWeb**: in the car Gemini neither calls `onPlayFromSearch` nor sends a media search to Android Auto for CarWeb, even when CarWeb is an Android Auto media app (07/11/2026). Do not add components or categories to try again without new facts (a Google change, or a log line with `onPlayFromSearch from=` and Gemini's package). What Gemini does is open the package (`ACTION_MAIN`), and Android Auto gives it the Wide: `MainHandOver` brings the Main in front when "Il tocco sul player apre CarWeb" says so. Never close the Wide for it: Android Auto reopens it and sends the Main back.
- **The player card tap**: Android Auto sends it to the component in the session extra `android.media.session.BROWSE_SERVICE`, if it is in its menu; otherwise to the first app of the package in the menu (the Wide, which takes the navigation space and is reopened if it closes). Whatever component is declared there must also answer as a MediaBrowser (AA connects to it, and the card stays empty if nobody answers): `CarService` forwards to `CarMediaService`. Never add a MEDIA category car app for this: it is one more icon in the menu (user: only CarWeb and CarWebWide), and it becomes AA's media app for the package.
- **Station text is never a title as it is**: everything goes through `AABTrackParser`, and a lyrics result is accepted only when it matches artist and title (in either order); better no lyrics than another song's. Words like "now", "next", "dopo" count as labels only with a colon. Add new real samples to `track-parser.test.js`.
- **Fullscreen from code needs a user activation**: `requestFullscreen()` run by `evaluateJavascript` always fails. To enter fullscreen without the user, arm a layer in the page and dispatch a `MotionEvent` tap to the WebView (trusted input for Chromium), calling `requestFullscreen` inside the layer's `touchend` with `preventDefault`; never tap the page directly (it would pause the video or follow a link). Synthetic taps must not count as user taps (`lastTouchAt`). Never act on a WebView right after resume: wait for the window focus and a drawn frame (`postVisualStateCallback`), and never leave a layer in the page that depends on the page's timers to go away.
- **Car screens work only when parked unless Passenger mode is on**: "moving" is what Android Auto says (`DrivingState`, AA SDK `DRIVING_STATUS`), never our own checks (GPS, speed: user decision); unknown status = no lock. New car-screen UI must stay under the cover while locked (Main: below zIndex 97; Wide: below `driveLockReference`), except the voice listening card and messages. Anything that types in the car goes through the Passenger keyboard when it is on (`AppSettings.passengerKeyboard`, Main only with `carInputManager != null`). The AA SDK connection lives on its own thread: never move it to the main thread (an SDK callback error would close the app in the car).
- **The weather exists only in the car**: `CarMediaService` is also bound on the phone (Main tabs, system media resumption), so anything shown on the player when nothing plays must check `isInCar()`. Outside the car the only notification is the one of a page that plays (or is paused) on the phone. To remove the notification use `removeNotification()` (`stopForeground(REMOVE)`), never `cancel()` alone; any start through `startForegroundService` must still reach `startForeground` (`onStartCommand`).
- **Wide drags have no position**: never try to pick the column being dragged (Car App Library gives only distances). On YouTube video pages the page is pinned and the columns scroll (user's choice, nothing below the video on the left); everywhere else page and columns scroll together so every part stays reachable.
- **Every metadata change of `CarMediaService` goes through `setSessionMetadata()`**: while a voice command listens the player shows "In ascolto…" and other metadata must wait.
- **Voice from outside a screen (assistant, steering wheel keys) goes through `VoiceCommandRouter`**, never through the AA player owner: the user may want to speak with nothing playing or with another tab playing.
- **Never unmute a YouTube video the app did not mute, except once at the start of a `/watch` video**: previews and user mutes must stay muted (a playing preview takes the AA player).
- **Steering wheel buttons**: apps never get the press duration of "back" (Car App Library and AA SDK alike) nor the voice button (assistant). Voice from the wheel = the assistant + `onPlayFromSearch`; a held media key is the only other possible hook, and only if the car sends it (check the `Media button` log first).
- **Media keys follow audio, not the Android Auto card**: the steering wheel keys reach `CarMediaService` only when AABrowser is the app that last played audio. Never try to grab them by playing silence (the car may switch its source away from the radio); anything shown on the weather card must work without the keys (▶ through the card's own controls reaches the session).
- **What must work with nothing playing goes on the player, not on the keys**: Android Auto sends the taps on the player buttons (transport controls and custom actions) to the session it shows, always; the wheel keys follow the last audio app. Never put custom actions on page states: Android Auto fills the free slots of a player without skip actions with its own ±10 s seek buttons, and a custom action takes their place.
- **The Main does nothing while it is behind**: `MainCarActivity` stops its lifecycle when another app is in front, so Compose state changes (tabs, URLs, screens) wait until it starts again. Anything triggered from outside (voice, assistant, player buttons) that needs the page must bring the screen to the front (`bringToFront`) or act on the WebView directly; player commands (JS on the owner WebView) work anyway.
- **Voice command phrases live in `VoiceCommands` (code, all languages together), not in strings.xml**: the recognizer language is the phone's, the UI language can be another one. Add a phrase to every language's list, with canonical names ("youtube", "amazon": other spellings go to `ALIASES`, whole words only), and add a test line in `VoiceCommandsTest` (`./gradlew --offline :browser:testDebugUnitTest`). User-visible messages stay in strings.xml (11 locales).
- **Settings singletons are initialized in `CarApplication.onCreate()`**: Android Auto can start the process for the Wide only (or the media service), without any Activity or `MainScreen`, so anything the Wide reads (settings `State`s, lists, whitelist) must be loaded at process start, never from a Main screen.
- **Writing into page fields (Main and Wide)**: `INSERT_TEXT_JS` first (`execCommand('insertText')` at the caret, then a synthetic paste), never `innerText`/`textContent` + fake events first (Lexical reverts them, Quill puts them at the start). Wide replacing text: set the DOM selection and dispatch `selectionchange` before `insertText`. Plain inputs fallback: native value setter + input/change. Both screens find the field again (`activeElement`, else the captured `__aabTypingTarget` or the last focused `__savedInputElement`) and give it the focus back; the caret moves to the end only if it left the field. Text handed to the Android Auto keyboard must be one line. Test new typing code in headless Chrome on playground.lexical.dev and a Quill page.
- **Messages of the Main in the car go through the Main's own card** (`feedbackMessage` via `onShowFeedback`), never a `Toast`: from `MainCarActivity` a Toast appears on the phone, not on the car screen. The Wide uses `CarToast`.
- **Location for websites**: every site asks (`SitePermissionPrompts`); the phone permission is asked only when a site needs it (precise; if the user picks approximate the site gets that), and on the phone the site's request waits for Android's answer. Do not retain geolocation grants in the WebView (`callback.invoke(origin, allowed, false)`). Location on the car screen counts as background: without "Allow all the time" a site is refused with a message (a location foreground service would be the other way). No Settings button for the phone permission (the user read it as "location for every site").
- **Adding a default favorite**: add it to `getDefaultFavorites()` with a new id, map that id to `DEFAULTS_VERSION + 1` in `DEFAULTS_ADDED_IN` and raise `DEFAULTS_VERSION`; existing users get it once at the end of their list. Never reuse an id, also of removed defaults ("9" = DAZN, removed in 2.5).
- **Releases for the updater**: every GitHub release must carry the APK as an asset whose name ends in `.apk` (CI); the tag is compared as a dotted number with the installed `versionName`.
- **The Wide never calls `navigationStarted()`**: it does not guide, and Android Auto allows a single active navigation, so the call ended Maps' navigation in progress. The surface does not need it. Android Auto opens the Wide on a player card tap anyway (package with media service + template app) and re-creates the session if the Wide finishes itself: do not try to close it on those intents.
- **User agent before the request**: the server picks desktop/mobile from the request headers, so the UA must be set before the navigation starts (`loadUrl` after setting it, or a reload from `shouldOverrideUrlLoading`); setting it in `onPageStarted` only affects the next request. Navigator spoofing must be a document start script, gated on the UA the page was requested with.
- **Wide layout = per host layout**: anything learned from the visible/stable areas (buttons band) belongs to that stable area (full screen, split with the player, split with the menu are different layouts). Unknown layout = no assumptions: the page must never end up under the host UI.
- **AAB Media audio graph**: `createMediaElementSource(#media)` only inside a user tap (silence otherwise) and it cannot be undone for the page; the crossfade's second player stays detached from the DOM so the AA integration only ever sees `#media`.
- **Streams returned by `shouldInterceptRequest` must start at byte 0**: the WebView applies the request's Range itself (skip + read to EOF) and uses `available()` as the total size (an int: ranges starting beyond 2 GB cannot be served). Bound the stream at the end of the range, never pre-skip, and let the WebView set `Content-Length`.
- **No Media Session API in the WebView**: anything that relies on `navigator.mediaSession` (metadata, `setActionHandler`) works only through the polyfill in `MEDIA_SESSION_TRACKS_JS`, which must run at document start (it is in `getLifecycleAndMetadataScript`). YouTube video hosts are excluded on purpose (playlist logic).
- **Invalid certificates: always ask, never proceed silently**: `handler.proceed()` only after an explicit user choice, remembered per host:port + certificate fingerprint (persisted; Settings > Clear data > "Accepted certificates" forgets them). Never accept all errors globally: it would make every site open to interception.
- **`AndroidBridge` is an attack surface**: every `@JavascriptInterface` method can be called by any page and any iframe loaded in the WebView. Methods that reach the network, open URLs or touch files must check the page (main-frame host tracked per WebView) and validate their arguments (`UrlUtils.isWebUrl` for URLs). Never add a generic "fetch for the page" method.
- **The DAZN code stays** (login proxy `proxyFetch`, DAZN-only CORS preflight, click interceptor, site search): without it DAZN does not work at all, not even its unprotected content (user decision, 26/10/2026). Only the default favorite and the README mentions were removed; do not offer to remove the code again.
- **Pages open tabs and popups only right after a tap** (10 s, checked by the app only: a page-side `navigator.userActivation` check broke Google sign-in popups, which open after an await): any new bridge method that opens or navigates must check the recent tap; page dialogs are never answered by the app.
- **Backup**: cookies/site storage (`app_webview/`) and accepted certificates never go to Google's backup or to a new phone; anything new that stores logins or tokens must be added to both backup rule files.
- **Updates**: KingInstaller installs without asking, so the app hands it only an APK it verified itself (package, signing certificates, newer version); never fall back to an unchecked file or URI.
- **Update APK access**: download into the app's own external folder, never the public Downloads: there the app cannot read or delete files it did not create (`Permission denied`, measured with `run-as`), and a leftover with the same name makes the new file take another one. Stage in the cache, then validate package, signing certificates, and version before FileProvider handoff. Open KingInstaller on `.MainActivity`: it shows the loaded file and the install choices (user confirmed); `.ApkHandler` skips them. Reasons of a refusal go to `Log`, also in release: an update failure on someone's phone cannot be diagnosed otherwise.
- **hls.js full build**: never the light one for IPTV or radio: a third of the channels keep the audio in a separate track. Anything played through `IptvProxy` keeps the channel's headers down to the segments.
- **One page per WebView, no hidden copies**: never mirror a page of one app in a hidden WebView of the other (sites with one session per browser, like WhatsApp Web, drop one of the two). A dead WebView (`onRenderProcessGone`) is never reused: remove, destroy, recreate.
- **Site permissions**: microphone, location and car files reach a page only through `SitePermissionPrompts` (or the user's auto switch); the camera is always denied; DRM (`RESOURCE_PROTECTED_MEDIA_ID`) stays automatic. App pages (AAB Media) never ask.
- **Page text into scripts only as JSON strings** (`JSONObject.quote`): never hand-escape quotes. Native code that needs to run a script in a page evaluates it directly; never add bridge methods that evaluate page-provided text in the main frame (every frame can call the bridge).
- **Navigation helpers act only for the main frame and only toward web addresses**: intent:// fallbacks must pass `UrlUtils.isWebUrl`; Wide internal `about:` commands only from the home page.
- **Never answer CORS preflights for the server**: a fake `OPTIONS` 200 with permissive headers lets any page send credentialed requests the browser would block. Only for DAZN (target or Origin host via `DaznProxy.isDaznHost`), and never with substring host checks.
- **Reading Widevine logs**: "Create MediaDrmBridge with level N" is the numeric SecurityLevel (1 = L3 SW_SECURE_CRYPTO, 5 = L1 HW_SECURE_ALL), and secure decoders end in `.secure`. On Android, SW_SECURE_DECODE and above require hardware secure codecs (L1). L3 licences may lack keys a service reserves for L1 (DAZN): a clear video playing while protected ones fail with "requested key has not been loaded" means missing keys, not the network. Fetch/XHR wrappers must never build `new Request(input)` before passing the original input on (it consumes the body).
- **Exported components accept intents from any app**: the update receiver must never trust the intent's URL or file (official release prefix + package check before KingInstaller, which installs without asking).
- **Wide host validator**: release builds accept only the library allowlist hosts; if a new Android Auto host version is refused (the Wide does not appear in the car), check the `CarServiceWide` validator first.
- **SDK 37 local network**: any new feature that talks to LAN devices (WebView pages, sockets, mDNS) depends on `ACCESS_LOCAL_NETWORK`; it can be granted only from the phone UI.
- **Local player queues are per kind**: audio and video never share a queue; the playing file's kind decides next/previous, the visible tab is only a view.
- **Local player = web page of the app, not native**: same page in Main and Wide, video needs the WebView anyway, AA integration comes for free from the injected scripts (media listeners, Media Session). Files are served by the app (no blob URLs): seeking works, nothing to re-select after a reload, works on the car without a picker.
- **Page buttons must not be named "Play"/"Pausa"/"Riproduci"...**: `PLAY_SCRIPT`/`PAUSE_SCRIPT` click buttons by those labels/classes after toggling the media themselves, so a toggle button with such a label would undo the AA command (the player page uses icon-only `#playToggle`).
- **Who decides the AA player buttons (skip vs ±10 s)**: the URL gives only the defaults at page start (`onPageStarted`); after that the page is the source of truth (Media Session `nexttrack`/`previoustrack`, YouTube playlist check). Never recompute from the URL later in the same page, and never in WebView factory callbacks with the composable `url` (it is the URL the tab was created with).
- **Below `MainScreen`, `LocalContext` is not the Activity**: use `LocalActivity.current` (re-provided by `MainScreen`), never `LocalContext.current as? Activity`, and `LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner.current` may be null (Android Auto `MainCarActivity`).
- **Web file chooser contract**: the `ValueCallback` must be completed exactly once. Either call it and return true, or return false without calling it (the WebView then cancels by itself); doing both crashes with "Duplicate showFileChooser result". Only SAF intents are used, so no storage permission is needed.
- **Per-WebView media clients + single arbiter**: `MediaFocusManager` is the only place that decides who owns the AA player; `MediaSessionManager` instances never talk to each other.
- **Who can take the player**: a client claims it only on a real start (JS `play` event / unmute, or a paused→playing transition) and only if it can take focus (visible Main tab or the Wide). Background tabs that start by themselves (autoplay) are paused.
- **Stale reports never claim**: periodic updates (`timeupdate`, Spotify polling) from a non-owner that was already playing are treated as late reports and paused (rate limited to 1/s).
- **Focus release**: on page start, tab close, Wide surface/WebView release. The owner sends STATE_NONE + empty metadata so `CarMediaService` goes back to weather/idle.
- **Injected JS must be Trusted Types safe**: YouTube enforces `require-trusted-types-for 'script'`, so never assign strings to `innerHTML`/`outerHTML`/`insertAdjacentHTML` in injected scripts (use `textContent` / DOM APIs).
- **Never request Android audio focus from the app side** (`CarMediaService` or elsewhere): each WebView requests it through Chromium when its media really starts; any other request from our package takes it away and Chromium pauses the page. The WebViews also pause each other natively through audio focus, which is consistent with "last started wins".

## 4. Next Steps (TODO)
- **Test for the future (user, 15/11/2026; no car with these controls at hand, maybe with the head unit emulator): the rotary knob and touchpad of cars such as BMW iDrive.** Not verified and no code for them: in CarWebWide, Android Auto should send scroll and zoom through the PAN button (`onScroll`, `onScale`) but gives no position to click a point of the page, and the dock buttons (drawn by us on the surface) cannot be reached; in CarWeb (Main) the keys may arrive as directional keys (the WebView turns on its spatial navigation by itself on D-pad events, Compose has its focus traversal): to be seen. If it matters: log the keys in `MainCarActivity`, and for the Wide think about moving the virtual pointer of the Bluetooth input (`BtInputRouter`) with the pan gestures. Mouse and keyboard over Bluetooth, and voice, are the ways today.
- **Issues from the user's test of 15/11/2026 (evening)** (analysis from the code, nothing reproduced; the fixes are NOT verified in the car):
  1. **Wide, first start: IPTV, Media… pages shifted out of the screen on the right; fine after trying again** — FIXED by a guess: after the startup reapply sequence settles (and 1.5 s after each page of `player.local` / `home.local` finishes) `WideScreen.healStaleLayout` compares the page's layout width (`innerWidth × devicePixelRatio`, `scrollWidth`) with the WebView's width and reloads the page once per URL if they differ more than 12 px; log line `layout check: view= inner= scroll= stale=`. If the log says `stale=false` while the page is still wrong, the cause is elsewhere (the favorite's zoom, `in-wide`).
  2. **Wide keyboard (Bluetooth)** — FIXED: `WideScreen.btKey` turns the key into `BrowserJavascript.getBtKeyScript` (text, Backspace, Delete, Enter, arrows, Home/End, Page, Tab, Ctrl+A/C/X/Z/Y), tested in headless Chrome (`/tmp/aab-videofit-test/btkeys.mjs`, 19 checks). Log `bt key <action> -> <result>`.
  3. **PARTLY FIXED (third round) — Wide sidebar icons = the Main's**: `WideDockIcons` now draws the Main's Material vectors (`dockIcon` of `MainDock`, read from the `ImageVector` paths onto a bitmap), so the dock is the same in both apps (not seen on a device: if the dock shows empty buttons, the path drawing failed). **FIXED in the fourth round (the user chose the Wide's look): the home tiles of the CarWeb pages** — the Main uses the drawables `ic_aab_media`, `ic_tv`, `ic_radio`, `ic_podcast`, `ic_apps` (`siteIconModel`), the Wide home the SVGs of `LocalPlayer` (`ICON_URL`, `TV_ICON_URL`, ...) inside a tinted circle (`HomeApi.iconCandidates`): one look for both (render the Main's drawables to PNG in `HomeApi` and show them without the tinted circle), best with a screenshot of the Main's dashboard to match. Also the old text of this item: the Wide sidebar icons (`WideDockIcons` draws bitmaps in code; the Main uses the Material icons of `MainDock`/`NavigationOverlay`), and the **CarWeb Media page icons** (Media, TV, Radio, Podcast, Other Apps) differ between the two apps (Compose `ic_*` vectors in the Main tiles and dock; SVG assets and drawn bitmaps in the Wide home and pages): one set for both.
  4. **Wide, the top-right host button** — FIXED: three lines when the sidebar is hidden, an X when it is shown (`iconSidebarToggle` / `iconSidebarClose`, `toggleDock` calls `invalidate()`).
  5. **FIXED (second round, to verify) — Wide, split view with the app on the right: an extra black piece between the app and its bar, and a thin empty line at the top** (see the Recent Updates entry). Cause unknown: perhaps the portrait display inside a wider panel (bands from `VirtualSplit.displayWidth`), the `focusMark` or the host's band; a screenshot would settle it.
  6. **Wide sidebar buttons not centred in split view** — FIXED: `layoutSidebar` is symmetric around the centre of the SURFACE (margin = the bottom inset, at least 8 px); the host's top band is no longer counted.
  7. **Wide home: "↗ CarWeb" covered when the Wide is shown small on the right** — FIXED by hiding it (`home.css`, `.in-wide #openMain` under 760 CSS px).
  8. **Bluetooth with the phone's screen off** — not possible without a screen of the app in front (the system gives events to the foreground app); done instead: `BtInputActivity` shows over the lock screen and, after 8 s without a touch, fades its text and drops the brightness to 2%, so it looks like a black lock screen (a touch brings the text back). Reading `/dev/input` with the Shizuku shell service would work with the screen really off (bigger job).
- Test (user) Bluetooth mouse and keyboard (phone, Main and Wide in the car, parked): pair the mouse and the keyboard with the phone, Settings > Altre App > "Apri la schermata di controllo" (the screen must say which car screen receives, "CarWeb" or "CarWebWide", and "nessuna schermata" when neither is in front): the pointer appears at the centre and moves (speed: try 50% and 200% in the same card), left click opens links, buttons, the dock and the tab bar, a drag scrolls, the wheel scrolls (Main and Wide), right click and the side button go back, Esc too; a click in a page field and typing (letters, accents of the keyboard layout, Backspace, Enter, Tab, arrows), Ctrl+L opens the search, Ctrl+T/W/Tab the tabs (Main), F5 reloads, Alt+←/→; a phone app on the car screen takes clicks and drags (Main and Wide); the ⌨️ 🎤 popup does not show while the screen is open; moving (or passenger mode off with the car moving) the pointer and keys do nothing; leaving the phone screen or Back stops everything and the pointer disappears. If it does not work, send the log (tag `BtInput`: `Capture started`, `Target … WxH`, `button …`, `wheel …`, `shortcut …`, `key … -> true|false`); no `button` lines at all = the pointer capture was not granted (a tap on the phone screen retries it). If typing does nothing in the Wide (`key … -> true` but no text), say so: the plan is the JavaScript insertion for printable characters.
- Test (user) the round of 15/11 in the car: the Wide's Exit button (it must reopen the Wide, not the Main beside it), the split staying when switching tabs (Main) and pages (Wide), a fullscreen video in the browser panel not covering the app (taps on the app panel, on the video's exit button; scroll to the app), TV fullscreen strip (taps on a chip, ⏮ ⏭, the page buttons, nothing under the host's top-right button in the Wide), Other Apps with the search field beside the Shizuku status (the keyboard of Android Auto for typing), CarWeb opened from the list in the Wide, Settings labels and the two lists (search engine and YouTube quality). Report the log lines `refreshSplit:`, `split tap` and `layout raw` after a Wide split test.
- Test (user) the new Wide home (car or DHU): open CarWebWide and the home must look like CarWeb's (title, ↗ CarWeb, mic, lens, pencil, cards, Riprendi and Impostazioni at the bottom) in the app's theme: automatic with the car's day/night, then Settings > theme forced light / dark / AMOLED and back to the home; the cards open their pages; the lens opens the search, the mic listens; ↗ CarWeb opens the Main; Settings opens the page; the update banner (debug build, "Update test" on). Check that scrolling the grid with Android Auto's drag works when there are many favorites, and that the tap targets are big enough. If the page stays empty or white: log tags `HomeApi` and the Wide's `shouldInterceptRequest` (the page, the assets `/home.js` `/home.css`, `/api/home/*`).
- Test (user) the Wide's split screen again and send the log right after (`adb logcat -d`, lines `split tap` and `layout raw`): tap the browser panel and scroll, tap the app and scroll (the azure mark on the active panel follows), pinch each panel, a fullscreen video (tap the player's exit button wherever it is; car Back; dock Back), the app panel is wide enough to read when the host's column is on the right (Settings > Altre App > the width), the keyboard popup over the app.

- **Work plan "one dock" (user request of 14/11/2026, one commit per step): steps 1-4 DONE (`f00915e`, `a087a1f`, `3c0ade1`, `c435cc7`), 5-7 TO DO.**
  5. Shared settings page — 5a DONE (`196a840`: the page, `SettingsApi`, the Wide home button and the dock gear, rows for the Wide and the Main dock); 5b TO DO: the rest of the categories and the Main using it. The idea: a web page like the Apps page (`https://player.local/settings`, served by the app, token on `/api/settings/*`), used by the Main (a tab) and by the Wide (the dock's settings button: set `WideScreen.SETTINGS_PAGE_AVAILABLE` to true and make `SidebarAction.SETTINGS` load the page). Migrate category by category (start with the Wide's own: dock size, reopen last page, the dock buttons, the video menu choice; then general, AdBlock, YouTube, media player, passenger); options that only work on the phone (file pickers, backup, system permissions, adding IPTV lists, the QR of a log) show "do it on the phone" in the car, as `iptv_add_on_phone` and `web_location_phone_toast` already do. A "Settings" button on both homes (the Main dashboard has one; the Wide home gets a new one). The phone Back and the car Back must close it like any page; texts in the 11 locales; keep the Compose Settings screen on the phone until the page covers everything.
  6. Align the homes: the Wide home becomes a web page with a small API for the favorites (add, edit name/address/colour/desktop/zoom, delete, move, reset defaults), like the Main's `DashboardScreen` + `EditFavoriteScreen` + `FavoritesViewModel`; the text fields use the existing Wide input flow (`openFieldInput`, passenger keyboard). One repository for both: after the Wide changes it the Main's ViewModel has to reload (it keeps its own list in memory).
  7. TV: a full-width channel bar in fullscreen (strip of logos and names, scroll and tap to pick the channel, also in the Wide) with the channel name and, when there is data, what is on air (extends the `.zap` panel); EPG only from Xtream (`get_short_epg`, `xmltv.php`) and the `url-tvg`/`x-tvg-url` of M3U lists (XMLTV as a stream, matched by `tvg-id`, cached per list, only the coming hours kept).

If a channel stays black or silent, its codec (AC3 audio, HEVC video) is the likely cause.
- Test (user) default IPTV-org list: first TV open auto-selects the playlist matching the app locale, a user-selected M3U/Xtream source remains remembered, changing app language selects the corresponding language list, unsupported language uses the general playlist.
- Test (user) language: app in Russian (Android per-app language): Wide and car Main texts, keyboard "Очистить" and the ЙЦУКЕН layout in the Wide too.
- App name (if the user picks a new one): change only the labels (app name, Wide label, `service_name` "AAB Media" if wanted, README, release notes), never the applicationId `com.fcaronte.aabrowser`: the updater and KingInstaller need the same package to update existing installs.
- Audit (report "AABrowser — Audit di sicurezza, bug e affidabilità"): all blocks done on 30/10/2026 (Stato column updated) except S8: in the Main, check that Zappr plays every channel (the Main already blocks active mixed content); if so, set the Wide to `MIXED_CONTENT_COMPATIBILITY_MODE` like the Main. Optional: KingInstaller signing certificate check (needs the user's fingerprint).
- Test (user) fullscreen after the background (`355725a`): a YouTube video (and another site's player) in fullscreen in the Main, home button, back to AABrowser: fullscreen comes back by itself within about a second, the app never looks frozen, the video does not pause and nothing on the page is tapped; same in the car with another Android Auto app in front and back. If it still freezes or stays small, share the debug log: the `Fullscreen restore: <step> (+N ms)` lines show where it stopped (no line after "layer armed" = no frame drawn, i.e. the WebView stayed hidden; "no fullscreen, layer removed"; "layer gone, no tap"). Also ask whether the freeze happens after the background without fullscreen too (then it is not the restore).
- Test (user) Passenger mode and keyboard, in the car or on the head unit emulator: share the log (long press on the GitHub icon, phone) after a drive and look at the `DrivingState` lines: which source works (`car` connection, `host` of the Main, both, or `not available`). Moving with Passenger mode off: Main and Wide covered by the message, audio and the AA player keep going, voice commands (wheel double tap, Wide mic) still work, parked again = page back. Passenger mode on (from the phone): no cover. Passenger keyboard: Main page field (⌨️ of the popup), Main search (tab bar/dashboard), favorite editor in the car; Wide page field and lens; ⌫, shift, ?123/àé pages, Clear, ✕, Enter writes (WhatsApp/Gemini editors too), car back closes the Wide keyboard. If the status never arrives (`not available` in both sources) nothing is locked: say so before the 2.5 notes.
- Google Maps location: works for the user (01/11/2026). If the device turns out to run 2.4 (update test), check again on the 2.4.1 build: phone Main -> Android dialog if precise is missing, then the site prompt at the same tap; car Main and Wide -> without precise + "Allow all the time" the message on the car screen, with them the prompt and then the blue dot. If a position never arrives in the car even with "Allow all the time", the next step is a foreground service of type `location`.
- Test (user) after the second 31/10 round: WhatsApp Web in the Main (car keyboard and dictation, phone and car): if it still writes nothing, a debug log with the `Voice input written` / `Car keyboard text written` lines. Still to try: `confirm()` on w3schools tryit pages (tryjs_confirm, tryjs_alert, tryjs_prompt).
- Test (user) after audit Block 5: open WhatsApp Web in the Wide with the Main alive (phone or car): no "Usa qui" unless WhatsApp is also open in a Main tab; the Main tab bar has no "Wide:" tab; closing/switching tabs still works. Renderer crash: not easy to trigger by hand (it happens when Android kills the WebView renderer for memory); after it the Main tabs reload their pages instead of staying blank.
- Test (user) after audit Block 4 (debug build): Settings > "Update test" on, back to the dashboard: banner and notification of the latest release; Wide closed and reopened: home banner; "Download": the APK downloads, then either KingInstaller opens (same signing key as the release) or the "not valid" message (debug key); turn the switch off after the test.
- Test (user) after audit Block 3: a page `confirm()` (e.g. delete in a web app) shows the question, Cancel keeps the item (Main phone, Main car, Wide); Google login popup shows accounts.google.com at the top and closes; "open in new tab" links and login popups still open after a tap; Wide lens title translated.
- Test (user) after audit Block 2: YouTube voice search asks the microphone once per site; Google Maps in the browser asks the location; "Solo questa volta" asks again after leaving the site, "Non consentire" never until removed in Settings; the Settings list (expand, remove) and Clear data; in the car: Main popup and Wide screen (three choices, back = not now), a file input of a site asks once, AAB Media "Choose folder" does not ask; "grant without asking" on = no prompts.
- Test (user) after audit Block 1: Main in the car: type a quote `"`, a backslash and an apostrophe with the car keyboard and with the voice input; "open in the app" links (intent://) on sites still open the web page (Main and Wide); Wide home: "reopen last page" and "fixed sidebar" toggles and the update banner links still work.
- Verify the weather fix: on the phone without the car no weather and no AABrowser notification (also after opening tabs, changing network, rebooting); a video playing on the phone keeps its notification and it goes away when the tab is closed; in the car the weather card still appears at start (it now waits for `CarConnection` to report the car: if it never appears, read the `New car connection` / `checkAndUpdateWeatherMetadata ... inCar=` lines in a debug build); unplugging the car removes weather and notification; starting the Main in the car does not crash (`startForegroundService` contract).
- Test (user) the in-app update once GitHub answers again (incident of 08/11/2026): the debug build 2.5.1 on the phone must find 2.6 at the opening of the Dashboard and hand it to KingInstaller. The asset `CarWeb-v2.6.apk` of the recreated release could not be checked during the outage. Never tried on a device before the release: the invisible links over the players, the choice of the AdBlock list, the Share entry appearing and going away with the car, the Wide sidebar size, the TV formats.
- Verify in the car: whether the Main comes back to the front for a command (`bringToFront` via the AA SDK; only reachable today with the Main as AA player owner, e.g. a Main tab paused, or with the Wide closed); Wide "portami a …": does Android Auto start Maps/Waze navigation (then add it back to hints/README/notes), or does the debug log show `Navigation request came back to the Wide`?
- Test (user): "Ok Google, metti danza delle streghe su AAB Media" in the car with Main or Wide open; double tap ⏮ with nothing playing, with a Main tab playing and in the Wide (a single tap still goes back after ~0.4 s); Wide YouTube: swipe the right column (video stays up, the page scrolls only at the end of the column), fling; playlist still pinned, back to search results keeps the position; home feed previews stay muted; "metti …" starts with audio; log sharing (long press GitHub) on the phone, message in the car. Wide fullscreen + phone call: share the log right after (lines `CALLBACK onVisibleAreaChanged` and `Fullscreen video margins`).
- YouTube black screen: test with "Remove ads in advance" on and off on the same videos (Main and Wide); with it on, send the logcat lines `AABrowser: ad data removed` / `after ad removal` / `content playing after` of a black wait (debug build) to decide what to keep removing (e.g. keep the data of server-stitched ads and let the DOM fallback skip them).
- Steering wheel: hold ⏮ and ⏭ in the car and send the `CarMediaService` lines `Media button` / `Transport` (debug build): if a hold is recognizable it can start a voice command.
- Verify voice commands by voice on the phone and in the car (Main tab bar / menu, Wide sidebar / top strip): recognition through Android Auto, live text, "metti … su YouTube" plays, "cerca … su Amazon", "apri <preferito>", "portami a …" (Main: Maps web; Wide: Maps/Waze of AA), pause/resume/next. Then "Ok Google / Hey Gemini, metti <canzone> su AABrowser" in the car with the Main or the Wide open (the app name must be understood by the assistant).
- Verify the YouTube AdBlock in a Wide opened directly (app closed with Exit or phone restarted, Main not opened): the first video's ad is skipped or never shown.
- Verify (not tested): YouTube ads in Main and Wide with AdBlock on (bumper 5 s muted and fast at once, skip pressed, video plays; logcat "YouTube AdBlock Active (v12)"); shield in the Wide: disabled on home, toggle on a site shows the toast and reloads, ads come back on that site (also in the Main), toggle back; player card tap with Maps navigating (session activity no-op + `WideSession`); Wide scrolling on YouTube/WhatsApp Web is smooth and taps respond sooner, side panels still scroll; YouTube speed chosen in the Wide survives opening YouTube in the Main.
- Verify weather: pause something from AA, switch the car off, next drive (without opening the app): the player card shows the weather. Favorites icons on a fresh install appear without restarting. Welcome: after the base permissions it stays, the location item asks precise and then "Allow all the time".
- Verify location for websites: the optional welcome item asks precise location, then "Allow all the time"; Google Maps in the browser shows the blue dot and follows it on the phone, then in the car with the phone locked (Main car and Wide); Settings switch off -> sites get "permission denied". If it fails, read the `WebLocation` log line.
- Verify favorites: an old list without AAB Media gets it at the end; AAB Media has no delete button and its URL cannot be edited; a reset to defaults and a backup restore keep working; deleting a favorite and restarting the app does not bring it back (except AAB Media).
- Verify in the car: navigate with Maps, play something in AABrowser, tap the AA player card: Maps and its navigation must stay. If the Wide still opens, read the `WideSession` log (debug build) to see the action Android Auto sends and match it in `WideSession`.
- Verify in the Wide: WhatsApp Web from the home tile opens the desktop QR page; a favorite with "desktop" on/off and a zoom gets them in the Wide (also global desktop on + favorite off = mobile); going back from a desktop site to a mobile one reloads with the mobile user agent.
- Verify after the security review: DAZN login and playback (Main phone, Main car, Wide); Google login popup and "open in new tab" links (Main and Wide); update banner -> download -> KingInstaller install; a **release** build in the car: the Wide must still appear in Android Auto (if not, `CarServiceWide` host validator).
- Verify before publishing 2.3: certificate accepted once is not asked again after closing the app, and "Accepted certificates" in Settings forgets it; AAB Media colors in light, dark (dynamic) and AMOLED, phone and car; YouTube ads (pre-roll, mid-roll, skippable and not) on phone, Main car and Wide; muted YouTube previews stay muted.
- Verify in the real car: Wide in split with the player (page no longer under the card), with the app menu (sidebar and page in the right pane), back to full screen; the phone logs showed 800x420 and 2217x1052 head units.
- Verify AAB Media with real music: crossfade sounds smooth (no repeat or gap at the start of the fade), effects react, no silence after enabling an effect; Material You colors follow the phone palette (Main and Wide).
- Verify on the phone and in the car: the user's 4K test MP4 plays to the end in AAB Media; AA shows title, artist, cover and skip for AAB Media (Main and Wide), skip changes track; Spotify / YouTube Music skip now go through the page's own Media Session handlers (polyfill): check they still change track.
- Media played by a detached element (`new Audio()` / `createElement('audio')` never added to the DOM, e.g. Google's Media Session sample) is invisible to `MEDIA_LISTENERS_JS` (it only sees elements in the document), so such pages never take the AA player. Possible fix: hook `HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play` to attach the listeners, and let `syncMetadata` use the last played element instead of `document.querySelector('video, audio')`.
- Emulator (Pixel_10_A16, host GPU) crashed (qemu SIGSEGV) as soon as a WebView video was rendered; with `-gpu swiftshader_indirect` it is stable.
- Verify on the phone (Android 17): the local network permission request appears at startup; Home Assistant via the home proxy (IP) and Proxmox (`https://IP:8006`) show the certificate warning, "Continue anyway" loads the page, "Cancel" stops it; the same site opens without asking again, also after closing the app. Then in the car: Main (popup) and Wide (Android Auto screen, also the back button = cancel).
- If a LAN site still fails after both fixes, read logcat for `SslErrorPrompts` (certificate error code) and for the WebView net error of the page (which net error a missing local network permission produces is not verified yet).
- Verify AAB Media: folder with music and videos -> two tabs with counts; music queue never moves to a video (also shuffle, repeat all, end of queue); tapping a video switches the queue; video bigger in the car, preview before play, AA cover for videos; the old favorite is renamed "AAB Media".
- Verify on the device: Spotify (Main tab opened directly and reached by navigation, and Wide) shows skip and changes track; YouTube Music skip; a SoundCloud-like site gets skip when it plays; plain videos keep ±10 s; YouTube playlists unchanged.
- Verify the Wide fixed sidebar: taps right next to it reach the page; the three buttons still respond across their whole width (also RTL).
- Verify a URL typed in the Wide search (lens) opens the site; "10.000 passi" searches.
- Verify the local player (favorite "AAB Media"): phone "Choose folder" -> list -> play/seek/shuffle/repeat/next; AA shows skip, title and cover; the same folder on the car (Main and Wide) without choosing again; resume after closing the app; a video file plays and goes fullscreen on tap.
- Verify YouTube speed: set 1.5x, next video starts at 1.5x, preference kept after an ad and after restarting the app; music videos at 1x.
- Player ideas for later: search/filter in the list, folder navigation, playlists (m3u), subtitles for videos.
- Verify on the phone: Web Player -> "Select Folder" (first time: system picker; then "Use it / Choose another" dialog, also after a reload); "Select Files"; on the car (Main and Wide) "Select Folder" loads the folder chosen on the phone.
- Verify tab close from the tab bar (active tab -> left neighbour) and from the tab manager; Settings location permission button on the phone.
- Optional: a real picker from the car would need a transparent proxy Activity started on the phone (background activity start limits).
- Deploy and verify on device / Android Auto: Main tab A -> tab B -> back to A; Main <-> Wide; "Wide:" tab selection; closing the owner tab; muted autoplay sites while music plays.
- If a site keeps resuming after being paused (ping-pong), check the `MediaFocusManager` logs (`DENIED`) and tune `RECLAIM_GUARD_MS`.
- Verify YouTube voice search in the Wide on the car (mic routing through Android Auto).
- Verify the Wide keyboard on DHU/car: tap a field -> ⌨️ -> type -> text in the field (YouTube search submits). `AABrowser.md` still says the keyboard is disabled in Wide Mode: update it if the test passes.
- Optional (not done): a custom keyboard drawn on the Wide surface would allow a docked bottom keyboard, but it must be locked while driving (Android Auto enforces that only for its own keyboards).
- Verify YouTube tap-to-seek on the device (Main phone/car and Wide). If YouTube renames the bar class, update the selector in `getTapToSeekScript()`.
- Optional (not done): in the Wide, horizontal swipe on a fullscreen video could seek ±N s, since real dragging is not possible there.
- Continue maintaining `PROJECT_MEMORY.md`.
